# 1936 세계대전 P0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 로비 옵션 `worldWar1936`("1936 세계대전", 다른 포크 모드와 배타) 하나로, 새 지도 `World 1936`(1936년 1월 1일의 실제 국경, 주 약 1,200개, 해역 약 150개)에서 사람은 나라를 하나 고르고 나머지 나라는 AI가 맡아, 준비 1분 뒤 하루 0.75초 달력으로 1945년 8월까지 원작 방식(병력·타일 공격)으로 싸우게 한다. 전쟁은 선포해야 시작되고(방어 연쇄, 시작 진영 3개 고정), AI는 역사 달력을 가끔 벗어나며 따르고, 승점을 잃으면 항복하며, 45분에 진영 점수로 판정한다. 옵션이 꺼진 모든 게임은 비트 단위로 지금과 같다.

**Architecture:** 정적 시나리오(주 번호 `regions.bin` + `scenario.json`)는 지도 파일로 싣고 engine-lib `ScenarioIndex`가 엔진과 화면 모두에서 같은 코드로 읽는다. 게임 상태는 `GameImpl`이 가진 `WorldWar` 하나(전쟁 쌍, 항복, 달력 굴림, 항복 이양 대기열)이고, 스냅샷 선택 키와 업데이트(`GameUpdateType.WorldWar`)로 나간다. 시작할 때 엔진과 화면이 같은 engine-lib 함수로 영토를 일괄로 채워 타일 업데이트를 보내지 않는다. 시계 `WorldWarExecution` 하나가 준비 끝, 하루 처리(달력, 항복), 이양을 돈다. 규칙의 본문은 갈래 파일(`Diplomacy.ts`, `HistoryRun.ts`, `Capitulation.ts`, `Victory.ts`)에 있고, 기반 Task가 **최종 시그니처를 가진 빈 함수(스텁)** 와 모든 연결 지점을 먼저 꽂는다. 지도 자료는 오프라인 Go 도구(원본 그리기 `cmd/ww1936build` + 지도 생성기 확장)가 만들어 저장소에 넣는다.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 6, Vitest 4, zod + zbin, Lit + Tailwind 4, WebGL2, Go 1.24 이상(map-generator. 이 계획의 Go 코드는 Go 1.27로 빌드·테스트했다)

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-world-war-1936-design.md` (이 계획과 같은 커밋). spec에서 *(내 판단)*으로 표시한 항목은 모두 확정 사항이며 그대로 구현한다. spec에 없는 구현 결정은 아래 "결정 사항"이 정한다.

---

## 시작 전에 (환경)

- **세계 의회가 먼저 합쳐져 있어야 한다**(주인 결정 11). 실행자는 세계 의회(`world-congress`)가 합쳐진 커밋에서 이 브랜치를 시작한다: `git -C /home/eser/dev/OpenFrontIO-ww merge --no-ff world-congress`(또는 주인이 정한 기준 브랜치). 이 계획의 코드 앵커는 세계 의회가 들어간 뒤의 파일을 기준으로 한다(예: `Schemas.ts`의 `congress` 다음, `GameUpdateType.Congress` 다음, `MessageType.CONGRESS` 다음). 세계 의회가 없는 기준에서 실행해야 하면 "맨 뒤에 붙인다"는 규칙만 지키고 `congress` 관련 줄(`delete out.congress` 등)을 뺀다.
- 작업 위치는 git worktree `/home/eser/dev/OpenFrontIO-ww`(브랜치 `ww1936`)다.
- **`/home/eser/dev/OpenFrontIO`에는 손대지 않는다.** 그 checkout에서 실제 서버가 3000~3002와 9000 포트로 돌고 있다. 화면 확인은 Task 13의 포트 9500만 쓴다.
- `git stash`, `git reset --hard`, `git checkout -- .`, `git clean`은 쓰지 않는다. 권한 시스템이 git 명령을 막으면 우회하지 말고 보고한다.
- 의존성이 없으면 `npm ci --ignore-scripts --engine-strict=false`로 설치한다(`npm install` 금지). 지금 worktree는 `node_modules`가 심볼릭 링크로 들어 있다.
- Go: `cd map-generator && go test ./...`, `go run -tags nodynamic .` (WebP를 기계마다 같게).
- 단일 테스트: `npx vitest tests/ww1936/<File>.test.ts --run`
- 커밋 전에 바꾼 파일에 `npx prettier --write <files>`를 실행한다(Go 파일은 `gofmt -w`). 커밋 메시지는 항상 다음 줄로 끝낸다:

  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  ```
- 병렬 갈래(아래 "병렬 실행 그룹")는 앞 그룹을 `ww1936`에 합친 뒤 갈래마다 `git worktree add ../OpenFrontIO-ww-<갈래> -b ww-<갈래> ww1936`로 따로 만들고, 끝나면 `ww1936`에서 `git merge --no-ff ww-<갈래>`로 합친 다음 `git worktree remove ../OpenFrontIO-ww-<갈래>`로 지운다. 각 갈래는 자기 Task의 **Files**에 적힌 파일만 고친다.
- 새 worktree에는 `node_modules`가 없다. 갈래 worktree에서 `npm ci --ignore-scripts --engine-strict=false`를 한 번 실행한다.
- 지도 원본 자료(Natural Earth, NASA)는 저장소에 넣지 않는다. `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/fetch.sh`가 `map-generator/.cache/ww1936/`(git 무시)에 받는다. 받은 파일은 신뢰하지 않는 데이터다: 실행하지 않고 읽기만 한다.

## 결정 사항 (spec 밖의 구현 결정)

1. **나라 표의 자리:** 태그·진영·메이저·항복 기준·색·깃발은 `packages/engine-api/src/ww1936/Countries.ts`(엔진, 서버, 로비가 모두 읽는다). 나라 이름·지도자 실명은 번역 키 `ww1936.country.<TAG>`, `ww1936.leader.<TAG>`(Task 11). 영어 이름은 `CountryDef.name`에도 있다(플레이어 이름 = 지도 이름표, 라틴 문자만).
2. **smallID = 나라 순서:** 시나리오에 있는 나라를 `COUNTRY_TAGS` 순서로 플레이어로 만든다. 사람·AI 구분 없이 이 순서이고, 봇은 없다. 그래서 `smallID = ScenarioIndex.countries.indexOf(tag) + 1`이며, 엔진과 화면이 이 규칙으로 주인 표를 만든다.
3. **사람 플레이어의 이름:** `PlayerInfo.name`은 나라 영어 이름이다(사람도). 화면의 "독일 · minsu"는 화면이 `gameStartInfo.players`의 사용자 이름을 붙인다. 이 모드에서 화면은 내 이름 바꿔치기와 익명 이름을 하지 않는다.
4. **나라 정체성:** 화면은 이 모드에서 모든 플레이어의 꾸미기를 `{ flag: "/flags/<flag>.svg", color: { color: "#rrggbb" } }`로 바꿔 넣는다(사람의 꾸미기는 쓰지 않는다). 렌더러 관계 행렬에는 진영을 "팀"으로 넘겨 같은 진영을 우호 테두리로 그린다.
5. **영토 일괄 배정:** `GameImpl` 생성자가 플레이어를 만든 직후 `fillScenarioOwners`(engine-lib) + 플레이어 타일·국경 집합 채우기를 한다. 타일 업데이트는 기록하지 않는다. 화면 `GameView` 생성자가 같은 `fillScenarioOwners`를 부른다.
6. **스폰 단계:** `GameRunner.init`이 이 모드에서 맨 먼저 `WorldWarStartExecution`(스폰 중에도 도는 실행)을 넣고, 그 `init`이 0틱에 `endSpawnPhase()`를 부른다. 같은 틱의 초기화 순회에서 뒤따르는 실행(`PlayerExecution` 등)이 바로 초기화된다. 스폰 타이머 실행은 넣지 않는다.
7. **준비 끝 틱:** `WW_PREP_TICKS = 600`. 스폰이 0틱에 끝나므로 `prepEndTick = 600`이다. 날짜 번호 `dayIndex(t) = t ≤ 600 ? 0 : floor((t − 600) × 2 / 15)`.
8. **전쟁 판정:** 이 모드에서 `PlayerImpl.canAttackPlayer`는 맨 앞에서 `WorldWar.canFight(a, b, tick)`(= 준비 아님 && 전쟁 중)를 요구한다. `isFriendly`는 같은 진영이면 참이다. 원작 동맹은 `customAllianceDuration: 0`으로 꺼진다.
9. **포위 흡수:** `PlayerExecution`의 `noCapture`가 이 모드에서 참이다(확장 전쟁과 같은 자리).
10. **PRNG:** 달력 굴림은 `WorldWarExecution(seed)`의 `PseudoRandom`으로 한다. `GameRunner`는 `simpleHash(gameID) + 1936`을 넘긴다. 실행의 `init`에서 한 번만, `HISTORY` 순서대로 항목마다 `chance(10)` 하나와 `nextInt(0, 21)` 하나를 굴린다(빠지는 항목도 둘 다 굴려 흐름을 맞춘다).
11. **승자 형식:** 진영 승리는 `setWinner(진영 id 문자열, …)`이고 `GameImpl.makeWinner`가 이 모드에서 `["team", 진영 id, 진영 사람 clientID…]`를 만든다. 비동맹 나라 승리는 그 플레이어. 승리 사유는 `WIN_REASONS` 맨 뒤의 `"ww_score"`, `"ww_last_camp"`.
12. **점수 정수화:** 점수는 0~10,000 기준점(basis point): `floor(7000 × 통제 승점 / 전체 승점) + floor(3000 × 땅 타일 / 전체 땅 타일)`. 부동소수 비교를 피한다.
13. **메시지:** 새 `MessageType.WORLD_WAR`(맨 뒤, 분류 ATTACK) 하나. 매개변수 이름이 `Tag`로 끝나면 화면 `EventsDisplay`가 번역된 나라 이름으로 바꾼다. 키: `events_display.ww1936_prep_over`, `ww1936_war_declared {attackerTag, targetTag, allies}`, `ww1936_war_joined {actorTag, enemyTag}`, `ww1936_capitulated {loserTag, victorTag}`.
14. **와이어·스냅샷:** 새 설정 키 2개(`worldWar1936`, `historicalAi`), 새 인텐트 `declare_war {target}`, `PlayerSchema.country`, `GameInfoSchema.countryPicks`, 클라이언트 메시지 `pick_country {tag | null}`, `GameUpdateType.WorldWar`, `MessageType.WORLD_WAR`, 승리 사유 2개는 각 목록의 맨 뒤에 붙인다. 새 Execution 3개(`DeclareWar`, `WorldWar`, `WorldWarStart`)를 `ExecutionRegistry`에 이름순으로 등록한다. 스냅샷 새 키 `GameSnapshot.worldWar`는 선택 키이고 버전은 올리지 않는다(세계 의회와 같은 방식).
15. **해시:** 이 모드에서만 `GameImpl.hash()`에 `WorldWar.hash()`를 더한다.
16. **지도 자료의 자리:** 원본 그리기 도구는 `map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/`, 그 입력 표는 `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/src/`, 그 출력은 `map-generator/assets/maps/world1936/image.png`(지형, 생성기 입력)와 `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/{regions.png, scenario.src.json}`(주 래스터와 주 정보). `assets/maps/<지도>/`에는 `image.png`, `info.json`만 둔다(`tests/MapConsistency.test.ts` 규칙). 생성기는 `info.json`의 `"ww1936": true`를 보고 `scenarios/<폴더>/`를 읽는다.
17. **테스트 지도:** 엔진 테스트는 200×120 작은 지도 `tests/testdata/maps/ww1936mini`(나라 6개: 프랑스, 독일, 이탈리아, 폴란드, 소련, 영국, 주 10개, 해역 2개)를 쓴다. `scripts/ww1936/make-test-map.ts`가 만든다. 시나리오의 `countries`에 있는 나라만 플레이어가 되고, 없는 나라가 나오는 달력 항목·시작 전쟁은 건너뛴다.
18. **보류 지도:** Task 1은 `GameMapType.World1936`을 손으로 `Maps.gen.ts`에 더하고, 실제 파일은 Task 4가 만든다. 그 사이 `tests/MapConsistency.test.ts`는 `PENDING_MAPS`(Task 1이 만들고 Task 4가 비운다)에 든 지도를 건너뛴다.

## Global Constraints

- **옵션 끔 = 지금과 비트 단위로 같음:** `worldWar1936`이 없거나 거짓인 게임은 이 모드의 상태·실행·PRNG·업데이트·스냅샷 키·메시지를 하나도 만들지 않는다. `normalizeWorldWar1936Config`는 끈 설정을 **같은 객체 그대로** 돌려준다. `tests/VanillaDigest.test.ts`의 기존 고정값(`c330352312204618`, `cc52cbbfd8b1a3bc`, `a7dac00d9005a3ad`, `7ab01036826a4a06`, `dddf7fe292970133`, `091085284bca29e0`, 그리고 세계 의회가 더한 것)과 Task 1이 더하는 "명시적으로 끔" 고정값이 그대로 통과해야 한다. 기존 FullGameSnapshot 변형도 그대로다.
- **배타:** 켜면 `domesticMode`, `warfareExpansion`, `strategicWeapons`, `congress`가 지워지고 지도·크기·방식·봇·시간·동맹·핵 단위가 spec §2.2대로 강제된다. 엔진(`Config` 생성자), 서버(`ConfigPatch`), 로비가 모두 같은 함수(`normalizeWorldWar1936Config`/`applyWorldWar1936Rules`)를 쓴다.
- **결정성:** `packages/engine*`에서 `Math.random`, `Date.now`, `new Date`, `Math.pow/exp/log/sin/cos/atan2…`, 2가 아닌 지수의 `**`를 쓰지 않는다(`Math.sqrt/floor/ceil/round/min/max/abs`는 허용). 날짜는 정수 그레고리력 함수. 무작위는 결정 사항 10의 PRNG만. 점수는 정수 기준점.
- **와이어는 뒤에만 추가:** 결정 사항 14. 기존 필드·열거값·유니온 순서를 바꾸지 않는다.
- **스냅샷:** 새 Execution은 `snapshot`/`restoreSnapshot`을 갖고 레지스트리에 등록한다. `restoreSnapshot`/`restoreState`는 모든 필드를 직접 대입하고 다른 객체의 메서드를 부르지 않는다. 화면 갱신 표시(`dataDirty`)는 `tests/util/Snapshot.ts`의 `DERIVED_FIELDS`에 넣는다. 정적 시나리오(`ScenarioIndex`)는 스냅샷에 넣지 않고 복원 때 지도 파일에서 다시 만든다.
- **패키지 경계(`tests/LayerBoundaries.test.ts`):** `engine-api`는 zod/zbin/resources만, `engine-lib`는 여기에 `engine-api`, `engine`은 여기에 `engine-lib`. `shared`는 `engine-api`, `engine-lib`만. `src/client`는 `engine`을 import하지 않는다.
- **홈 화면 청크:** `src/client/Main.ts`와 그 정적 import는 게임 클라이언트(`hud/`, `render/`, `view/`, `Transport` …)를 import하지 않는다. 로비 쪽 새 이벤트는 `LobbyEvents.ts`에, 나라 고르기 컴포넌트는 지연 로딩 모달(`SinglePlayerModal`, `HostLobbyModal`, `JoinLobbyModal`)만 import한다.
- **화면 문구:** 모두 `translateText()`를 거치고 키는 `resources/lang/en.json`(키 정렬 필수, `tests/EnJsonSorted.test.ts`)과 `resources/lang/ko.json` 둘 다에 넣는다. 다른 언어 파일은 건드리지 않는다. 예외(spec §11): 주·해역 이름은 `scenario.json`의 `name`/`nameKo`.
- **화면 크기와 조작:** §14d 원작 크기(버튼 글자 `text-xs`~`text-sm`, 다이얼 `size-11`). 전체 화면을 덮는 창, 화면 흔들림 없음. 호버로만 보이는 정보 없음(`title` 금지). 선전포고는 두 번 탭(3초 안). 폰·태블릿 가로 화면에서 지도 가운데를 가리지 않는다.
- **깃발 정책:** `resources/flags/Empire of Japan.svg`(욱일기)와 하켄크로이츠가 든 그림은 어느 나라에도 연결하지 않는다(Task 1 테스트가 막는다).
- **엔진 변경에는 테스트가 따른다**(CLAUDE.md). 테스트는 실제 게임(`tests/util/Setup.ts`, 또는 `GameRunner` + 테스트 지도)으로 하고 목(mock)을 쓰지 않는다(화면 테스트의 가짜 GameView는 예외).
- **파일 소유:** 각 Task는 자기 **Files**의 파일만 고친다. 다른 갈래 파일이 필요하면 그 Task의 **Interfaces**에 적힌 이름만 쓴다.

## Review Focus

1. **첫 영토가 엔진과 화면에서 타일 하나까지 같고, 첫 틱들의 타일 업데이트가 0개다.** 테스트: Task 1 `tests/ww1936/ScenarioStart.test.ts` "the view fills the same owners as the engine", "no tile updates on the opening ticks".
2. **준비 1분 동안은 이미 전쟁 중인 쌍도 싸우지 못하고 선포도 안 되며, 600틱에 풀린다.** 테스트: Task 1 `tests/ww1936/WarGate.test.ts` "a war cannot be fought during the preparation minute", Task 5 `tests/ww1936/Diplomacy.test.ts` "no declaration during the preparation minute".
3. **선포는 대상의 진영만 끌어들이고 선포국의 진영은 끌어들이지 않으며, 참전은 둘 사이에만 머문다.** 테스트: Task 5 "a declaration brings in the target's faction, never the attacker's", "a join stays between the two".
4. **항복 이양 도중의 상태:** 이양 대상 타일을 다른 나라가 먼저 가져갔거나, 이양 도중 저장·복원하거나, 이양이 끝나야 정복 처리(금·통계)가 한 번 일어난다. 테스트: Task 6 `tests/ww1936/Capitulation.test.ts` "tiles someone else took are skipped", "the conquest is settled once, when the last tile moves"; Task 12 `tests/ww1936/WorldWarSnapshot.test.ts` "restoring mid-transfer continues exactly".
5. **사람이 고른 나라가 겹치거나 시나리오에 없거나 아예 없을 때:** 서버는 겹침을 거절하고, 엔진은 겹침·없는 태그를 무시하고 추천 순서의 빈 나라를 준다. 테스트: Task 8 `tests/server/CountryPick.test.ts` "a taken country is refused", Task 1 `tests/ww1936/ScenarioStart.test.ts` "duplicate and unknown picks fall back to the next free country".

## 병렬 실행 그룹

- **그룹 0 — Task 1(기반):** 모든 갈래의 선행 조건. 다른 갈래가 쓰는 타입·이름·시그니처는 Task 1의 **Interfaces**가 정한다. 혼자 한다.
- **그룹 1 (Task 1을 합친 뒤 동시에, worktree 6개):**
  - 갈래 M (지도 자료, 차례로): **Task 2 → Task 3 → Task 4** (`map-generator/**`, `resources/maps/world1936/**`, `CREDITS.md`의 지도 자료 절, `tests/MapConsistency.test.ts`의 보류 해제, `tests/ww1936/World1936Data.test.ts`)
  - 갈래 E (엔진 규칙, 차례로): **Task 5 → Task 6** (`engine/ww1936/{Diplomacy,HistoryRun,Capitulation,Victory}.ts`)
  - 갈래 A (AI): **Task 7** (`engine/execution/utils/AiAttackBehavior.ts`). 엔진 규칙(갈래 E)의 본문 없이 `WorldWar.setWar`로 시험한다.
  - 갈래 L (로비·서버): **Task 8**
  - 갈래 U (게임 화면, 차례로): **Task 9 → Task 10** (HUD, 렌더 패스). 엔진은 스텁이어도 된다: 화면 테스트는 가짜 데이터로 한다.
  - 갈래 T (번역): **Task 11** (`resources/lang/en.json`, `ko.json`)
- **그룹 2 — Task 12(결정성·성능):** 그룹 1을 모두 합친 뒤. 모드 켬 스냅샷 오라클, 켬 고정값, 시작 시간 측정.
- **그룹 3 — Task 13(전체 검증):** 맨 마지막. 실제 지도, 실제 화면.

`tests/VanillaDigest.test.ts`의 `describe` 안 맨 끝(세계 의회의 마지막 테스트 뒤)에 추가:

```ts
  // World War 1936 (spec §2.3): explicitly off, or historicalAi without the
  // mode, changes nothing. Pins taken from the first test.
  test("the World War 1936 option explicitly off changes nothing", async () => {
    expect(await digest({ worldWar1936: false })).toBe("c330352312204618");
  }, 60_000);

  test("historicalAi without the mode changes nothing", async () => {
    expect(await digest({ historicalAi: true })).toBe("c330352312204618");
  }, 60_000);
```

Run: `npx vitest tests/VanillaDigest.test.ts --run`
Expected: 전부 PASS(엔진이 아직 모르는 키는 무시된다. 이후 모든 Task의 지킴이다).

- [ ] **Step 2: 나라 표 (engine-api)**

`packages/engine-api/src/ww1936/Countries.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * World War 1936 (spec §5.1): the countries of 1 January 1936. The order is
 * the player order: a country's smallID is its index among the countries
 * present in the loaded scenario, plus one (plan decision 2). Tags follow
 * ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 where one exists and are this fork's own otherwise.
 * Names and leaders on screen come from resources/lang
 * (`ww1936.country.<TAG>`, `ww1936.leader.<TAG>`).
 */
export const COUNTRY_TAGS = [
  // Majors
  "DEU",
  "ITA",
  "JPN",
  "GBR",
  "FRA",
  "USA",
  "SUN",
  // Europe
  "POL",
  "CSK",
  "AUT",
  "HUN",
  "ROU",
  "YUG",
  "BGR",
  "GRC",
  "ALB",
  "TUR",
  "BEL",
  "NLD",
  "LUX",
  "CHE",
  "ESR",
  "ESN",
  "PRT",
  "IRL",
  "DNK",
  "ISL",
  "NOR",
  "SWE",
  "FIN",
  "EST",
  "LVA",
  "LTU",
  "DZG",
  // Middle East and Asia
  "IRQ",
  "IRN",
  "SAU",
  "YEM",
  "AFG",
  "CHN",
  "CCP",
  "GXC",
  "SXI",
  "YUN",
  "XBM",
  "SIK",
  "TIB",
  "MCK",
  "MNG",
  "TTU",
  "THA",
  "NPL",
  "RAJ",
  // Africa
  "ETH",
  "LBR",
  "ZAF",
  // Americas
  "CAN",
  "MEX",
  "GTM",
  "HND",
  "SLV",
  "NIC",
  "CRI",
  "PAN",
  "CUB",
  "HTI",
  "DOM",
  "COL",
  "VEN",
  "ECU",
  "PER",
  "BOL",
  "BRA",
  "PRY",
  "URY",
  "ARG",
  "CHL",
  // Oceania
  "AUS",
  "NZL",
] as const;
export type CountryTag = (typeof COUNTRY_TAGS)[number];

export function isCountryTag(v: string): v is CountryTag {
  return (COUNTRY_TAGS as readonly string[]).includes(v);
}

/** The starting factions (spec §5.2), in camp order (Score.campRank). */
export const FACTION_IDS = ["axis", "allies", "comintern"] as const;
export type FactionId = (typeof FACTION_IDS)[number];

/** Lobby list sections (spec §8.1). */
export const COUNTRY_GROUPS = [
  "europe",
  "asia",
  "americas",
  "africa_oceania",
] as const;
export type CountryGroup = (typeof COUNTRY_GROUPS)[number];

export interface CountryDef {
  /** English name: the player's name, and the label drawn on the map. */
  readonly name: string;
  /** File name stem in resources/flags (drawn as /flags/<flag>.svg). */
  readonly flag: string;
  /** Territory colour, RGB. */
  readonly color: readonly [number, number, number];
  readonly faction: FactionId | null;
  readonly major: boolean;
  /** Share of its starting victory points (percent) lost at which it capitulates. */
  readonly surrenderPct: number;
  readonly group: CountryGroup;
}

type Extra = Partial<Pick<CountryDef, "faction" | "major" | "surrenderPct">>;

function def(
  name: string,
  flag: string,
  color: readonly [number, number, number],
  group: CountryGroup,
  extra: Extra = {},
): CountryDef {
  return {
    name,
    flag,
    color,
    group,
    faction: extra.faction ?? null,
    major: extra.major ?? false,
    surrenderPct: extra.surrenderPct ?? 80,
  };
}

const AXIS = { faction: "axis" } as const;
const ALLIES = { faction: "allies" } as const;
const COMINTERN = { faction: "comintern" } as const;

export const COUNTRIES: Readonly<Record<CountryTag, CountryDef>> = {
  DEU: def("Germany", "German Empire", [110, 110, 110], "europe", { ...AXIS, major: true }),
  ITA: def("Italy", "italy", [64, 140, 70], "europe", { ...AXIS, major: true }),
  JPN: def("Japan", "jp", [226, 205, 140], "asia", { ...AXIS, major: true }),
  GBR: def("United Kingdom", "gb", [200, 60, 70], "europe", { ...ALLIES, major: true }),

export const FACTION_LEADER: Readonly<Record<FactionId, CountryTag>> = {
  axis: "DEU",
  allies: "GBR",
  comintern: "SUN",
};

export const MAJOR_TAGS: readonly CountryTag[] = COUNTRY_TAGS.filter(
  (t) => COUNTRIES[t].major,
);

/** Unpicked humans get the first free country in this order (spec §7.1). */
export const PICK_ORDER: readonly CountryTag[] = (() => {
  const first: CountryTag[] = ["DEU", "GBR", "SUN", "USA", "FRA", "ITA", "JPN"];
  return [...first, ...COUNTRY_TAGS.filter((t) => !first.includes(t))];
})();
```

`packages/engine-api/src/ww1936/WorldWarTypes.ts`:

```ts
/** World War 1936 (spec §3.1): ticks of the opening preparation minute. */
export const WW_PREP_TICKS = 600;
/** Game length in minutes (spec §2.2). */
export const WW_GAME_MINUTES = 45;
/** First sea-zone id: land regions are 1..SEA_ZONE_BASE-1 (spec §4.7). */
export const SEA_ZONE_BASE = 40000;
/** Tiles handed over per tick after a capitulation (spec §7.5). */
export const WW_TRANSFER_TILES_PER_TICK = 20_000;
/** Starting troops as a percent of max troops (spec §5.3). */
export const WW_START_TROOPS_PCT = 50;
/** Victory points a capital adds to its region (spec §7.5). */
export const WW_CAPITAL_VP = 15;

/** Why a declaration of war is refused (`declareWarBlock`, UI reason text). */
export const DECLARE_WAR_BLOCKS = [
  "prep",
  "self",
  "dead",
  "capitulated",
  "faction",
  "at_war",
] as const;
export type DeclareWarBlock = (typeof DECLARE_WAR_BLOCKS)[number];

/** One war, by smallIDs (a < b), in the order the wars began. */
export interface WarData {
  a: number;
  b: number;
  sinceTick: number;
}

export interface CapitulationData {
  smallID: number;
  /** smallID of the country that took the rest of its land; 0 if none. */
  victor: number;
  tick: number;
}

/** What the screens read (GameUpdateType.WorldWar), sent when it changed. */
export interface WorldWarData {
  prepEndTick: number;
  wars: WarData[];
  capitulated: CapitulationData[];
}
```

`packages/engine-api/src/ww1936/ScenarioTypes.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { COUNTRY_TAGS } from "./Countries";
import { SEA_ZONE_BASE } from "./WorldWarTypes";

/** A tile as map coordinates [x, y]. */
const XY = z.tuple([
  z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  z.number().int().nonnegative(),
]);

export const REGION_TERRAINS = ["plains", "highland", "mountain"] as const;

export const ScenarioCountrySchema = z.object({
  tag: z.enum(COUNTRY_TAGS),
  capital: XY,
});

export const ScenarioRegionSchema = z.object({
  id: z
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(1)
    .max(SEA_ZONE_BASE - 1),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  nameKo: z.string().min(1),
  owner: z.enum(COUNTRY_TAGS),
  vp: z.number().int().min(1),
  vpTile: XY,
  tiles: z.number().int().min(1),
  terrain: z.enum(REGION_TERRAINS),
});

export const ScenarioSeaZoneSchema = z.object({
  id: z
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(SEA_ZONE_BASE + 1)
    .max(65535),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  nameKo: z.string().min(1),
  tiles: z.number().int().min(1),
});

/** scenario.json next to regions.bin (spec §4.7). */
export const ScenarioDataSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  width: z.number().int().positive(),
  height: z.number().int().positive(),
  countries: z.array(ScenarioCountrySchema).min(2),
  regions: z.array(ScenarioRegionSchema).min(1),
  seaZones: z.array(ScenarioSeaZoneSchema),
});

export type ScenarioData = z.infer<typeof ScenarioDataSchema>;
export type ScenarioCountry = z.infer<typeof ScenarioCountrySchema>;
export type ScenarioRegion = z.infer<typeof ScenarioRegionSchema>;
export type ScenarioSeaZone = z.infer<typeof ScenarioSeaZoneSchema>;
```

`tests/ww1936/Countries.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  COUNTRIES,
  COUNTRY_TAGS,
  FACTION_LEADER,
  MAJOR_TAGS,
  PICK_ORDER,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const FLAGS = path.join(__dirname, "../../resources/flags");
// Spec §5.4: the Rising Sun flag is never a country's flag.
const FORBIDDEN_FLAGS = ["Empire of Japan", "Empire of Japan1"];

describe("the 1936 country table", () => {
  test("79 unique tags, each with a definition", () => {
    expect(COUNTRY_TAGS).toHaveLength(79);
    expect(new Set(COUNTRY_TAGS).size).toBe(79);
    expect(Object.keys(COUNTRIES).sort()).toEqual([...COUNTRY_TAGS].sort());
  });

  test("seven majors and three fixed factions led by Germany, Britain and the Soviet Union", () => {
    expect([...MAJOR_TAGS]).toEqual(["DEU", "ITA", "JPN", "GBR", "FRA", "USA", "SUN"]);
    const members = (f: string) =>
      COUNTRY_TAGS.filter((t) => COUNTRIES[t].faction === f);
    expect(members("axis")).toEqual(["DEU", "ITA", "JPN", "MCK"]);
    expect(members("allies")).toEqual(["GBR", "FRA", "RAJ", "ZAF", "CAN", "AUS", "NZL"]);
    expect(members("comintern")).toEqual(["SUN", "MNG", "TTU"]);
    expect(FACTION_LEADER).toEqual({ axis: "DEU", allies: "GBR", comintern: "SUN" });
  });

  test("every flag exists and none is the Rising Sun", () => {
    for (const tag of COUNTRY_TAGS) {
      const flag = COUNTRIES[tag].flag;
      expect(FORBIDDEN_FLAGS, tag).not.toContain(flag);
      expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(FLAGS, `${flag}.svg`)), `${tag}: ${flag}`).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(COUNTRIES.JPN.flag).toBe("jp");
    expect(COUNTRIES.DEU.flag).toBe("German Empire");
  });

  test("the pick order lists every country once, majors first", () => {
    expect(PICK_ORDER.slice(0, 7)).toEqual(["DEU", "GBR", "SUN", "USA", "FRA", "ITA", "JPN"]);
    expect([...PICK_ORDER].sort()).toEqual([...COUNTRY_TAGS].sort());
  });

  test("names are Latin only (the map's label font)", () => {
    for (const tag of COUNTRY_TAGS) {
      expect(COUNTRIES[tag].name, tag).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z .-]+$/);
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/ww1936/Calendar.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  civilFromDays,
  dateOfDay,
  dayIndex,
  dayOfDate,
  daysFromCivil,
  dayStartTick,
  formatIsoDate,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";

describe("the 1936 calendar", () => {
  test("civil dates round-trip through day numbers", () => {
    expect(daysFromCivil(1970, 1, 1)).toBe(0);
    expect(daysFromCivil(2000, 3, 1)).toBe(11017);
    for (const z of [-25567, 0, 11016, 11017, 19000]) {
      const d = civilFromDays(z);
      expect(daysFromCivil(d.year, d.month, d.day)).toBe(z);
    }
    expect(formatIsoDate(civilFromDays(-25567))).toBe("1900-01-01");
  });

  test("days since 1936-01-01 of the spec's events", () => {
    expect(dayOfDate("1936-01-01")).toBe(0);
    expect(dayOfDate("1936-07-17")).toBe(198);
    expect(dayOfDate("1939-09-01")).toBe(1339);
    expect(dayOfDate("1941-06-22")).toBe(1999);
    expect(dayOfDate("1944-06-06")).toBe(3079);
    expect(dayOfDate("1945-08-21")).toBe(3520);
    expect(formatIsoDate(dateOfDay(1339))).toBe("1939-09-01");
  });

  test("one day is 7.5 ticks after the preparation minute; 45:00 is 1945-08-21", () => {
    expect(dayIndex(0, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(0);
    expect(dayIndex(WW_PREP_TICKS, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(0);
    expect(dayIndex(WW_PREP_TICKS + 7, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(0);
    expect(dayIndex(WW_PREP_TICKS + 8, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(1);
    expect(dayIndex(WW_PREP_TICKS + 15, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(2);
    expect(dayIndex(27_000, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(3520);
    expect(formatIsoDate(dateOfDay(dayIndex(27_000, WW_PREP_TICKS)))).toBe("1945-08-21");
    for (const day of [0, 1, 2, 3, 198, 1339]) {
      const t = dayStartTick(day, WW_PREP_TICKS);
      expect(dayIndex(t, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(day);
      if (day > 0) expect(dayIndex(t - 1, WW_PREP_TICKS)).toBe(day - 1);
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/ww1936/Regions.test.ts`:

```ts
import { decodeRegions, encodeRegions } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Regions";

describe("regions.bin", () => {
  test("round-trips runs of region ids", () => {
    const w = 7;
    const h = 3;
    const ids = Uint16Array.from([
      1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 40001, 40001,
      1, 1, 2, 2, 2, 40001, 0,
      300, 300, 300, 300, 65535, 65535, 65535,
    ]);
    const bin = encodeRegions(ids, w, h);
    expect(Array.from(bin.slice(0, 4))).toEqual([0x52, 0x47, 0x4e, 0x31]);
    expect(decodeRegions(bin, w, h)).toEqual(ids);
  });

  test("a long run takes a multi-byte length", () => {
    const ids = new Uint16Array(4000 * 2036).fill(7);
    const bin = encodeRegions(ids, 4000, 2036);
    expect(bin.length).toBeLessThan(32);
    expect(decodeRegions(bin, 4000, 2036)).toEqual(ids);
  });

  test("rejects a wrong size, a bad magic and runs that do not cover the map", () => {
    const bin = encodeRegions(Uint16Array.from([1, 1, 2, 2]), 2, 2);
    expect(() => decodeRegions(bin, 4, 1)).toThrow(/2x2/);
    const bad = bin.slice();
    bad[0] = 0;
    expect(() => decodeRegions(bad, 2, 2)).toThrow(/magic/);
    expect(() => decodeRegions(bin.slice(0, bin.length - 1), 2, 2)).toThrow();
  });
});
```

`tests/ww1936/ModeConfig.test.ts`:

```ts
import { GameMapSize, GameMapType, GameMode, UnitType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { GameConfig } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import { Config } from "@openfront/engine-lib/configuration/Config";
import {
  applyWorldWar1936Rules,
  normalizeWorldWar1936Config,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ModeConfig";
import { testGameConfig } from "../util/Wire";

describe("World War 1936 settings", () => {
  test("off: the very same object comes back", () => {
    const c = testGameConfig({ domesticMode: true });
    expect(normalizeWorldWar1936Config(c)).toBe(c);
    const f = testGameConfig({ worldWar1936: false, gameMap: GameMapType.Europe });
    expect(normalizeWorldWar1936Config(f)).toBe(f);
  });

  test("on: every other fork mode goes, and the map, rules and timer are fixed", () => {
    const c: GameConfig = testGameConfig({
      worldWar1936: true,
      domesticMode: true,
      warfareExpansion: true,
      strategicWeapons: true,
      congress: true,
      gameMap: GameMapType.Europe,
      gameMapSize: GameMapSize.Compact,
      gameMode: GameMode.Team,
      playerTeams: 2,
      bots: 100,
      randomSpawn: true,
      maxTimerValue: 10,
      disabledUnits: [UnitType.Warship],
      doomsdayClock: { enabled: true, speed: "fast" },
    });
    const n = normalizeWorldWar1936Config(c);
    expect(n).not.toBe(c);
    expect(c.domesticMode).toBe(true); // the input is untouched
    expect(n.domesticMode).toBeUndefined();
    expect(n.warfareExpansion).toBeUndefined();
    expect(n.strategicWeapons).toBeUndefined();
    expect(n.congress).toBeUndefined();
    expect(n.doomsdayClock).toBeUndefined();
    expect(n.playerTeams).toBeUndefined();
    expect(n).toMatchObject({
      gameMap: GameMapType.World1936,
      gameMapSize: GameMapSize.Normal,
      gameMode: GameMode.FFA,
      bots: 0,
      randomSpawn: false,
      nations: "default",
      maxTimerValue: 45,
      customAllianceDuration: 0,
      spawnImmunityDuration: 0,
      historicalAi: true,
    });
    expect(n.disabledUnits).toEqual([
      UnitType.Warship,
      UnitType.AtomBomb,
      UnitType.HydrogenBomb,
      UnitType.MIRV,
      UnitType.MissileSilo,
      UnitType.SAMLauncher,
    ]);
    expect(normalizeWorldWar1936Config(n)).toEqual(n); // idempotent
  });

  test("historicalAi can be turned off", () => {
    const n = normalizeWorldWar1936Config(
      testGameConfig({ worldWar1936: true, historicalAi: false }),
    );
    expect(n.historicalAi).toBe(false);
  });

  test("applyWorldWar1936Rules edits a stored config in place", () => {
    const c = testGameConfig({ worldWar1936: true, domesticMode: true });
    applyWorldWar1936Rules(c);
    expect(c.domesticMode).toBeUndefined();
    expect(c.gameMap).toBe(GameMapType.World1936);
  });

  test("Config reads the normalized config", () => {
    const cfg = new Config(
      testGameConfig({ worldWar1936: true, domesticMode: true, strategicWeapons: true }),
      false,
    );
    expect(cfg.worldWar1936()).toBe(true);
    expect(cfg.historicalAi()).toBe(true);
    expect(cfg.domesticMode()).toBe(false);
    expect(cfg.strategicWeapons()).toBe(false);
    expect(cfg.disableAlliances()).toBe(true);
    expect(cfg.isUnitDisabled(UnitType.AtomBomb)).toBe(true);
    expect(cfg.isUnitDisabled(UnitType.City)).toBe(false);
    expect(cfg.maxTimerMinutes()).toBe(45);
    const off = new Config(testGameConfig({ historicalAi: true }), false);
    expect(off.worldWar1936()).toBe(false);
    expect(off.historicalAi()).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/ModeConfig.ts`:

```ts
import {
  GameMapSize,
  GameMapType,
  GameMode,
  UnitType,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { GameConfig } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import { WW_GAME_MINUTES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";

/** Units the mode turns off (spec §2.2): the atom bomb returns in P6. */
export const WW1936_DISABLED_UNITS: readonly UnitType[] = [
  UnitType.AtomBomb,
  UnitType.HydrogenBomb,
  UnitType.MIRV,
  UnitType.MissileSilo,
  UnitType.SAMLauncher,
];

/**
 * World War 1936 (spec §2.2): in place, fixes a config whose mode is on.
 * Does nothing when the mode is off. The server runs it on its stored config.
 */
export function applyWorldWar1936Rules(c: GameConfig): void {
  if (c.worldWar1936 !== true) return;
  delete c.domesticMode;
  delete c.warfareExpansion;
  delete c.strategicWeapons;
  delete c.congress;
  delete c.conquestPercent;
  delete c.doomsdayClock;
  delete c.overtime;
  delete c.playerTeams;
  c.gameMap = GameMapType.World1936;
  c.gameMapSize = GameMapSize.Normal;
  c.gameMode = GameMode.FFA;
  c.bots = 0;
  c.randomSpawn = false;
  c.nations = "default";
  c.maxTimerValue = WW_GAME_MINUTES;
  c.customAllianceDuration = 0;
  c.spawnImmunityDuration = 0;
  c.historicalAi = c.historicalAi ?? true;
  const disabled = [...(c.disabledUnits ?? [])];
  for (const u of WW1936_DISABLED_UNITS) {
    if (!disabled.includes(u)) disabled.push(u);
  }
  c.disabledUnits = disabled;
}

/**
 * The config the engine, the server and the lobby play with. Returns the
 * very object it was given when the mode is off (so vanilla stays
 * bit-identical), otherwise a fixed copy.
 */
export function normalizeWorldWar1936Config(c: GameConfig): GameConfig {
  if (c.worldWar1936 !== true) return c;
  const out: GameConfig = { ...c };
  applyWorldWar1936Rules(out);
  return out;
}
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/Regions.ts`:

```ts
// regions.bin (spec §4.7): "RGN1", u32 width, u32 height, u32 run count,
// then runs of (LEB128 length >= 1, LEB128 id 0..65535) in map.bin tile
// order. Integer arithmetic only: the engine decodes it.
const MAGIC = [0x52, 0x47, 0x4e, 0x31];
const MAX_VARINT_MUL = 34_359_738_368; // 2^35: five 7-bit groups

function pushVarint(out: number[], value: number): void {
  let v = value;
  while (v >= 0x80) {
    out.push((v % 0x80) | 0x80);
    v = Math.floor(v / 0x80);
  }
  out.push(v);
}

export function encodeRegions(
  ids: Uint16Array,
  width: number,
  height: number,
): Uint8Array {
  if (ids.length !== width * height) {
    throw new Error(`regions: ${ids.length} ids for a ${width}x${height} map`);
  }
  const body: number[] = [];
  let runs = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; ) {
    let j = i + 1;
    while (j < ids.length && ids[j] === ids[i]) j++;
    pushVarint(body, j - i);
    pushVarint(body, ids[i]);
    runs++;
    i = j;
  }
  const out = new Uint8Array(16 + body.length);
  out.set(MAGIC, 0);
  const view = new DataView(out.buffer);
  view.setUint32(4, width, true);
  view.setUint32(8, height, true);
  view.setUint32(12, runs, true);
  out.set(body, 16);
  return out;
}

export function decodeRegions(
  bytes: Uint8Array,
  width: number,
  height: number,
): Uint16Array {
  if (bytes.length < 16 || MAGIC.some((b, i) => bytes[i] !== b)) {
    throw new Error("regions.bin: bad magic");
  }
  const view = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const w = view.getUint32(4, true);
  const h = view.getUint32(8, true);
  const runs = view.getUint32(12, true);
  if (w !== width || h !== height) {
    throw new Error(`regions.bin is ${w}x${h}, the map is ${width}x${height}`);
  }
  const out = new Uint16Array(w * h);
  let pos = 16;
  const read = (): number => {
    let v = 0;
    let mul = 1;
    for (;;) {
      if (pos >= bytes.length) throw new Error("regions.bin: truncated");
      const b = bytes[pos++];
      v += (b & 0x7f) * mul;
      if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return v;
      mul *= 0x80;
      if (mul > MAX_VARINT_MUL) throw new Error("regions.bin: varint too long");
    }
  };
  let at = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < runs; r++) {
    const len = read();
    const id = read();
    if (len < 1 || id > 0xffff || at + len > out.length) {
      throw new Error(`regions.bin: bad run ${r}`);
    }
    out.fill(id, at, at + len);
    at += len;
  }
  if (at !== out.length || pos !== bytes.length) {
    throw new Error("regions.bin: the runs do not cover the map exactly");
  }
  return out;
}
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/ScenarioIndex.ts`:

```ts
import { GameMap, TileRef } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameMap";
import { COUNTRY_TAGS, CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import {
  ScenarioData,
  ScenarioRegion,
  ScenarioSeaZone,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/ScenarioTypes";
import { SEA_ZONE_BASE } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { decodeRegions } from "./Regions";

/**
 * The static World War 1936 scenario: a region id per tile and what
 * scenario.json says about regions, sea zones and countries. Built once from
 * the map files, by the engine and by the screens alike.
 */
export class ScenarioIndex {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  /** Countries in the scenario, in COUNTRY_TAGS order: smallID = index + 1. */
  readonly countries: readonly CountryTag[];
  /** Land regions sorted by id. */
  readonly landRegions: readonly ScenarioRegion[];
  private readonly regionById = new Map<number, ScenarioRegion>();
  private readonly seaById = new Map<number, ScenarioSeaZone>();
  private readonly capitals = new Map<CountryTag, readonly [number, number]>();
  private readonly owners: Uint16Array;
  private readonly _totalVp: number;

  constructor(
    readonly data: ScenarioData,
    readonly regionIds: Uint16Array,
  ) {
    this.width = data.width;
    this.height = data.height;
    if (regionIds.length !== data.width * data.height) {
      throw new Error(
        `scenario: ${regionIds.length} region ids for ${data.width}x${data.height}`,
      );
    }
    const present = new Set(data.countries.map((c) => c.tag));
    this.countries = COUNTRY_TAGS.filter((t) => present.has(t));
    for (const c of data.countries) this.capitals.set(c.tag, c.capital);
    for (const s of data.seaZones) this.seaById.set(s.id, s);
    let maxId = 0;
    let vp = 0;
    for (const r of data.regions) {
      this.regionById.set(r.id, r);
      if (r.id > maxId) maxId = r.id;
      vp += r.vp;
    }
    this._totalVp = vp;
    this.landRegions = [...data.regions].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
    this.owners = new Uint16Array(maxId + 1);
    for (const r of data.regions) {
      const owner = this.smallIdOf(r.owner);
      if (owner === 0) {
        throw new Error(`scenario: region ${r.id} belongs to ${r.owner}, which is missing`);
      }
      this.owners[r.id] = owner;
    }
  }

  static fromFiles(data: ScenarioData, regionsBin: Uint8Array): ScenarioIndex {
    return new ScenarioIndex(
      data,
      decodeRegions(regionsBin, data.width, data.height),
    );
  }

  /** The country's smallID, or 0 when it is not in this scenario. */
  smallIdOf(tag: CountryTag): number {
    return this.countries.indexOf(tag) + 1;
  }

  tagOfSmallId(smallID: number): CountryTag | undefined {
    return smallID >= 1 ? this.countries[smallID - 1] : undefined;
  }

  regionAt(tile: TileRef): number {
    return this.regionIds[tile];
  }

  /** A land region; undefined for sea zones, 0 and unknown ids. */
  region(id: number): ScenarioRegion | undefined {
    return id >= 1 && id < SEA_ZONE_BASE ? this.regionById.get(id) : undefined;
  }

  seaZone(id: number): ScenarioSeaZone | undefined {
    return this.seaById.get(id);
  }

  capitalXY(tag: CountryTag): readonly [number, number] {
    const xy = this.capitals.get(tag);
    if (xy === undefined) throw new Error(`scenario: no capital for ${tag}`);
    return xy;
  }

  capitalTile(tag: CountryTag): TileRef {
    const [x, y] = this.capitalXY(tag);
    return y * this.width + x;
  }

  vpTile(region: ScenarioRegion): TileRef {
    return region.vpTile[1] * this.width + region.vpTile[0];
  }

  /** smallID of each land region's 1936 owner, indexed by region id. */
  ownerByRegion(): Uint16Array {
    return this.owners;
  }

  totalVp(): number {
    return this._totalVp;
  }

  /** Victory points each country starts with, indexed by smallID. */
  startVpBySmallId(): number[] {
    const out = new Array<number>(this.countries.length + 1).fill(0);
    for (const r of this.landRegions) out[this.owners[r.id]] += r.vp;
    return out;
  }
}

/**
 * Gives every land tile its 1936 owner (spec §5.3). The engine runs it once
 * at the start (without tile updates) and every screen runs it on its own
 * copy of the map, so both start from the same owners.
 */
export function fillScenarioOwners(map: GameMap, index: ScenarioIndex): void {
  if (map.width() !== index.width || map.height() !== index.height) {
    throw new Error(
      `scenario is ${index.width}x${index.height}, the map ${map.width()}x${map.height()}`,
    );
  }
  const owners = index.ownerByRegion();
  const ids = index.regionIds;
  for (let t = 0; t < ids.length; t++) {
    const id = ids[t];
    if (id === 0 || id >= SEA_ZONE_BASE || id >= owners.length) continue;
    if (!map.isLand(t) || map.isImpassable(t)) continue;
    const owner = owners[id];
    if (owner !== 0) map.setOwnerID(t, owner);
  }
}
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/Calendar.ts`:

```ts
// World War 1936 (spec §3.1): one day is 0.75 s, 15 ticks are two days.
// Integer Gregorian arithmetic (H. Hinnant's days_from_civil), no Date.

export interface CivilDate {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
}

/** Days since 1970-01-01. */
export function daysFromCivil(y: number, m: number, d: number): number {
  const yy = m <= 2 ? y - 1 : y;
  const era = Math.floor(yy / 400);
  const yoe = yy - era * 400;
  const mp = (m + 9) % 12;
  const doy = Math.floor((153 * mp + 2) / 5) + d - 1;
  const doe = yoe * 365 + Math.floor(yoe / 4) - Math.floor(yoe / 100) + doy;
  return era * 146097 + doe - 719468;
}

export function civilFromDays(days: number): CivilDate {
  const z = days + 719468;
  const era = Math.floor(z / 146097);
  const doe = z - era * 146097;
  const yoe = Math.floor(
    (doe - Math.floor(doe / 1460) + Math.floor(doe / 36524) - Math.floor(doe / 146096)) / 365,
  );
  const doy = doe - (365 * yoe + Math.floor(yoe / 4) - Math.floor(yoe / 100));
  const mp = Math.floor((5 * doy + 2) / 153);
  const day = doy - Math.floor((153 * mp + 2) / 5) + 1;
  const month = mp < 10 ? mp + 3 : mp - 9;
  const year = yoe + era * 400 + (month <= 2 ? 1 : 0);
  return { year, month, day };
}

const EPOCH_1936 = daysFromCivil(1936, 1, 1);

/** Days since 1936-01-01 at `tick`; 0 until the preparation minute ends. */
export function dayIndex(tick: number, prepEndTick: number): number {
  return tick <= prepEndTick ? 0 : Math.floor(((tick - prepEndTick) * 2) / 15);
}

/** The first tick of day `day`. */
export function dayStartTick(day: number, prepEndTick: number): number {
  return prepEndTick + Math.ceil((day * 15) / 2);
}

export function dateOfDay(day: number): CivilDate {
  return civilFromDays(EPOCH_1936 + day);
}

/** Days since 1936-01-01 of an ISO date "YYYY-MM-DD". */
export function dayOfDate(iso: string): number {
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso);
  if (m === null) throw new Error(`not an ISO date: ${iso}`);
  return daysFromCivil(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3])) - EPOCH_1936;
}

export function formatIsoDate(d: CivilDate): string {
  const pad = (n: number) => (n < 10 ? `0${n}` : `${n}`);
  return `${d.year}-${pad(d.month)}-${pad(d.day)}`;
}
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/History.ts`:

```ts
import { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";

/**
 * "declare": a declaration of war that brings in the target's faction.
 * "join": a war between the two only (spec §7.3).
 */
export type HistoryKind = "declare" | "join";

export interface HistoryEntry {
  date: string;
  kind: HistoryKind;
  actor: CountryTag;
  target: CountryTag;
}

/** One entry in this many is skipped (PseudoRandom.chance), spec §7.4. */
export const HISTORY_SKIP_ODDS = 10;
/** The rest happen 0..this many days late. */
export const HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS = 20;

/** Wars already running on 1936-01-01 (spec §5.3). */
export const WW_INITIAL_WARS: readonly (readonly [CountryTag, CountryTag])[] = [
  ["ITA", "ETH"],
];

/** The P0 historical calendar (spec §7.4), by date. */
export const HISTORY: readonly HistoryEntry[] = [
  { date: "1936-07-17", kind: "declare", actor: "ESN", target: "ESR" },
  { date: "1937-07-07", kind: "declare", actor: "JPN", target: "CHN" },

  { date: "1940-04-09", kind: "declare", actor: "DEU", target: "DNK" },
```

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/Score.ts`:

```ts
import { FACTION_IDS, FactionId } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";

/** One country's numbers for the verdict (spec §7.6). */
export interface CountryScoreInput {
  smallID: number;
  faction: FactionId | null;
  major: boolean;
  /** Alive and not capitulated. */
  alive: boolean;
  /** Victory points it controls now (its VP tiles). */
  vp: number;
  tiles: number;
}

/** A faction, or one unaligned country (camp = its smallID). */
export interface CampScore {
  camp: FactionId | number;
  members: number[];
  vp: number;
  tiles: number;
  /** 0..10000 (plan decision 12). */
  scoreBp: number;
  livingMajors: number;
}

/** 70% victory points + 30% land, in basis points. */
export function scoreBp(
  vp: number,
  tiles: number,
  totalVp: number,
  totalTiles: number,
): number {
  const v = totalVp > 0 ? Math.floor((7000 * vp) / totalVp) : 0;
  const t = totalTiles > 0 ? Math.floor((3000 * tiles) / totalTiles) : 0;
  return v + t;
}

function campRank(c: CampScore): number {
  return typeof c.camp === "number"
    ? FACTION_IDS.length + c.camp
    : FACTION_IDS.indexOf(c.camp);
}

/**
 * Camps with a living member, best first: score, then victory points, then
 * the fixed camp order (factions, then countries by smallID).
 */
export function campScores(
  inputs: readonly CountryScoreInput[],
  totalVp: number,
  totalTiles: number,
): CampScore[] {
  const camps = new Map<FactionId | number, CampScore & { alive: boolean }>();
  const sorted = [...inputs].sort((a, b) => a.smallID - b.smallID);
  for (const c of sorted) {
    const key = c.faction ?? c.smallID;
    let camp = camps.get(key);
    if (camp === undefined) {
      camp = { camp: key, members: [], vp: 0, tiles: 0, scoreBp: 0, livingMajors: 0, alive: false };
      camps.set(key, camp);
    }
    camp.members.push(c.smallID);
    camp.vp += c.vp;
    camp.tiles += c.tiles;
    if (c.alive) {
      camp.alive = true;
      if (c.major) camp.livingMajors++;
    }
  }
  const out: CampScore[] = [];
  for (const camp of camps.values()) {
    if (!camp.alive) continue;
    out.push({
      camp: camp.camp,
      members: camp.members,
      vp: camp.vp,
      tiles: camp.tiles,
      scoreBp: scoreBp(camp.vp, camp.tiles, totalVp, totalTiles),
      livingMajors: camp.livingMajors,
    });
  }
  return out.sort(
    (a, b) => b.scoreBp - a.scoreBp || b.vp - a.vp || campRank(a) - campRank(b),
  );
}

/** The one camp that still has a living major, or null (spec §7.6). */
export function lastCampStanding(camps: readonly CampScore[]): CampScore | null {
  const withMajor = camps.filter((c) => c.livingMajors > 0);
  return withMajor.length === 1 ? withMajor[0] : null;
}

/** MVP order inside a camp: own score, then smallID. */
export function rankMembers(
  inputs: readonly CountryScoreInput[],
  members: readonly number[],
  totalVp: number,
  totalTiles: number,
): { smallID: number; scoreBp: number }[] {
  return inputs
    .filter((c) => members.includes(c.smallID))
    .map((c) => ({
      smallID: c.smallID,
      scoreBp: scoreBp(c.vp, c.tiles, totalVp, totalTiles),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.scoreBp - a.scoreBp || a.smallID - b.smallID);
}
```

```ts
/** World War 1936 (spec §4.7): regions.bin and the validated scenario.json. */
async function loadScenarioFiles(
  data: MapData,
): Promise<Pick<MapFiles, "regionsBin" | "scenario">> {
  const [regionsBin, scenarioBytes] = await Promise.all([
    data.file("regions.bin"),
    data.file("scenario.json"),
  ]);
  const scenario = ScenarioDataSchema.parse(
    JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(scenarioBytes)),
  );
  return { regionsBin, scenario };
}
```

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/WorldWar.ts`:

```ts
import {
  COUNTRIES,
  CountryTag,
  FactionId,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import {
  WorldWarData,
  WW_PREP_TICKS,
  WW_START_TROOPS_PCT,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { zInt, zTiles } from "@openfront/engine-lib/snapshot/SnapshotType";
import { dayIndex } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import { WW_INITIAL_WARS } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/History";
import { ScenarioIndex } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Game, Player } from "../game/Game";

export const WorldWarStateSchema = z.object({
  prepEndTick: zInt(),
  lastDay: zInt(),
  wars: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  capitulated: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), zInt(), zInt()])),
  startVp: z.array(zInt()),
  historyRolled: z.boolean(),
  history: z.array(z.tuple([zInt(), z.boolean()])),
  transfers: z.array(
    z.object({ from: zInt(), to: zInt(), tiles: zTiles(), next: zInt() }),
  ),
});
export type WorldWarState = z.infer<typeof WorldWarStateSchema>;

/** A capitulated country's remaining tiles moving to its victor (spec §7.5). */
export interface Transfer {
  from: number;
  to: number;
  tiles: Uint32Array;
  next: number;
}

/**
 * World War 1936 game state (spec §7): who is at war with whom, who has
 * capitulated, the rolled historical calendar and pending land transfers.
 * Factions are fixed in P0 (COUNTRIES[tag].faction).
 */
export class WorldWar {
  private readonly size: number;
  private warMatrix: Uint8Array;
  private _wars: [number, number, number][] = [];
  private _capitulated: [number, number, number][] = [];
  private _startVp: number[] = [];
  private _historyRolled = false;
  private _history: [number, boolean][] = [];
  private _transfers: Transfer[] = [];
  private _prepEndTick = WW_PREP_TICKS;
  private _lastDay = -1;
  /** Not state (DERIVED_FIELDS): whether the screens need an update. */
  private dataDirty = true;

  constructor(
    private readonly mg: Game,
    readonly scenario: ScenarioIndex,
  ) {
    this.size = scenario.countries.length + 1;
    this.warMatrix = new Uint8Array(this.size * this.size);
  }

  /**
   * Spec §5.3, §7.1: capitals, starting troops, starting victory points and
   * the wars of 1 January 1936. GameImpl calls it once, after the territory.
   */
  start(): void {
    for (const p of this.mg.allPlayers()) {
      const tag = this.tagOf(p.smallID());
      if (p.name() !== COUNTRIES[tag].name) {
        throw new Error(`player ${p.smallID()} is ${p.name()}, expected ${tag}`);
      }
      const capital = this.scenario.capitalTile(tag);
      p.setSpawnTile(capital);
      this.mg.stats().recordSpawnTile(p, capital);
      p.setTroops(
        Math.floor((this.mg.config().maxTroops(p) * WW_START_TROOPS_PCT) / 100),
      );
    }
    this._startVp = this.scenario.startVpBySmallId();
    for (const [x, y] of WW_INITIAL_WARS) {
      const a = this.scenario.smallIdOf(x);
      const b = this.scenario.smallIdOf(y);
      if (a !== 0 && b !== 0) this.setWar(a, b, 0);
    }
  }

  // ---- Queries ----

  prepEndTick(): number {
    return this._prepEndTick;
  }

  inPrep(tick: number): boolean {
    return tick < this._prepEndTick;
  }

  day(tick: number): number {
    return dayIndex(tick, this._prepEndTick);
  }

  tagOf(smallID: number): CountryTag {
    const tag = this.scenario.tagOfSmallId(smallID);
    if (tag === undefined) throw new Error(`no country with smallID ${smallID}`);
    return tag;
  }

  /** The living player of a country, or null. */
  playerOfTag(tag: CountryTag): Player | null {
    const id = this.scenario.smallIdOf(tag);
    if (id === 0) return null;
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(id);
    return p.isPlayer() ? p : null;
  }

  factionOf(smallID: number): FactionId | null {
    const tag = this.scenario.tagOfSmallId(smallID);
    return tag === undefined ? null : COUNTRIES[tag].faction;
  }

  /** Members of a faction present in this scenario, by smallID. */
  factionMembers(faction: FactionId): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];
    this.scenario.countries.forEach((tag, i) => {
      if (COUNTRIES[tag].faction === faction) out.push(i + 1);
    });
    return out;
  }

  sameFaction(a: number, b: number): boolean {
    if (a === b) return false;
    const f = this.factionOf(a);
    return f !== null && f === this.factionOf(b);
  }

  isAtWar(a: number, b: number): boolean {
    if (a <= 0 || b <= 0 || a >= this.size || b >= this.size) return false;
    return this.warMatrix[a * this.size + b] === 1;
  }

  /** Spec §7.2: at war, and the preparation minute is over. */
  canFight(a: number, b: number, tick: number): boolean {
    return !this.inPrep(tick) && this.isAtWar(a, b);
  }

  enemiesOf(smallID: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];
    for (let b = 1; b < this.size; b++) {
      if (this.isAtWar(smallID, b)) out.push(b);
    }
    return out;
  }

  isCapitulated(smallID: number): boolean {
    return this._capitulated.some(([s]) => s === smallID);
  }

  startVp(smallID: number): number {
    return this._startVp[smallID] ?? 0;
  }

  /** Human clientIDs of a camp (a faction), for the team winner (decision 11). */
  humanClientIDsOf(faction: FactionId): string[] {
    const out: string[] = [];
    for (const id of this.factionMembers(faction)) {
      const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(id);
      if (p.isPlayer() && p.clientID() !== null) out.push(p.clientID()!);
    }
    return out;
  }

  // ---- Changes (the lanes' functions call these) ----

  /** Starts a war; false when it already runs or a == b. */
  setWar(a: number, b: number, tick: number): boolean {
    if (a === b || this.isAtWar(a, b)) return false;
    if (a <= 0 || b <= 0 || a >= this.size || b >= this.size) return false;
    const lo = Math.min(a, b);
    const hi = Math.max(a, b);
    this._wars.push([lo, hi, tick]);
    this.warMatrix[lo * this.size + hi] = 1;
    this.warMatrix[hi * this.size + lo] = 1;
    this.dataDirty = true;
    return true;
  }

  /** Ends every war of a country (on capitulation). */
  endWarsOf(smallID: number): void {
    const kept = this._wars.filter(([a, b]) => a !== smallID && b !== smallID);
    if (kept.length === this._wars.length) return;
    this._wars = kept;
    this.rebuildMatrix();
    this.dataDirty = true;
  }

  addCapitulation(smallID: number, victor: number, tick: number): void {
    if (this.isCapitulated(smallID)) return;
    this._capitulated.push([smallID, victor, tick]);
    this.dataDirty = true;
  }

  queueTransfer(from: number, to: number, tiles: Uint32Array): void {
    this._transfers.push({ from, to, tiles, next: 0 });
  }

  transfers(): Transfer[] {
    return this._transfers;
  }

  historyRolled(): boolean {
    return this._historyRolled;
  }

  /** Per HISTORY entry: [fire day, or -1 when skipped; fired yet]. */
  history(): readonly (readonly [number, boolean])[] {
    return this._history;
  }

  setHistory(plan: [number, boolean][]): void {
    this._history = plan;
    this._historyRolled = true;
  }

  markHistoryDone(index: number): void {
    const entry = this._history[index];
    if (entry !== undefined) this._history[index] = [entry[0], true];
  }

  lastDay(): number {
    return this._lastDay;
  }

  setLastDay(day: number): void {
    this._lastDay = day;
  }

  // ---- Screens, snapshots, hash ----

  data(): WorldWarData {
    return {
      prepEndTick: this._prepEndTick,
      wars: this._wars.map(([a, b, sinceTick]) => ({ a, b, sinceTick })),
      capitulated: this._capitulated.map(([smallID, victor, tick]) => ({
        smallID,
        victor,
        tick,
      })),
    };
  }

  /** The data when it changed since the last call, else null. */
  takeData(): WorldWarData | null {
    if (!this.dataDirty) return null;
    this.dataDirty = false;
    return this.data();
  }

  hash(): number {
    let h = 17;
    for (const [a, b] of this._wars) h = (Math.imul(h, 31) + a * 97 + b) | 0;
    for (const [s, v] of this._capitulated) {
      h = (Math.imul(h, 31) + s * 131 + v + 7) | 0;
    }
    return h >>> 0;
  }

  snapshot(): WorldWarState {
    return {
      prepEndTick: this._prepEndTick,
      lastDay: this._lastDay,
      wars: this._wars.map((w) => [w[0], w[1], w[2]]),
      capitulated: this._capitulated.map((c) => [c[0], c[1], c[2]]),
      startVp: [...this._startVp],
      historyRolled: this._historyRolled,
      history: this._history.map((h) => [h[0], h[1]]),
      transfers: this._transfers.map((t) => ({
        from: t.from,
        to: t.to,
        tiles: t.tiles.slice(),
        next: t.next,
      })),
    };
  }

  restoreState(s: WorldWarState): void {
    this._prepEndTick = s.prepEndTick;
    this._lastDay = s.lastDay;
    this._wars = s.wars.map((w) => [w[0], w[1], w[2]]);
    this._capitulated = s.capitulated.map((c) => [c[0], c[1], c[2]]);
    this._startVp = [...s.startVp];
    this._historyRolled = s.historyRolled;
    this._history = s.history.map((h) => [h[0], h[1]]);
    this._transfers = s.transfers.map((t) => ({
      from: t.from,
      to: t.to,
      tiles: t.tiles.slice(),
      next: t.next,
    }));
    this.rebuildMatrix();
    // A restored game's screens have seen nothing yet.
    this.dataDirty = true;
  }

  private rebuildMatrix(): void {
    this.warMatrix.fill(0);
    for (const [a, b] of this._wars) {
      this.warMatrix[a * this.size + b] = 1;
      this.warMatrix[b * this.size + a] = 1;
    }
  }
}
```

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/ScenarioPlayers.ts`:

```ts
import {
  Cell,
  Nation,
  PlayerInfo,
  PlayerType,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import type { ClientID, Player as StartPlayer } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import {
  COUNTRIES,
  CountryTag,
  PICK_ORDER,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { PseudoRandom } from "@openfront/engine-lib/PseudoRandom";
import { ScenarioIndex } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";

/**
 * Spec §7.1: each human keeps a valid, untaken pick (first come, in player
 * order); everyone else gets the first free country in PICK_ORDER. Humans
 * beyond the number of countries get none (they watch).
 */
export function assignCountries(
  players: readonly Pick<StartPlayer, "clientID" | "country">[],
  present: readonly CountryTag[],
): Map<ClientID, CountryTag> {
  const taken = new Set<CountryTag>();
  const out = new Map<ClientID, CountryTag>();
  for (const p of players) {
    const pick = p.country;
    if (pick !== undefined && present.includes(pick) && !taken.has(pick)) {
      out.set(p.clientID, pick);
      taken.add(pick);
    }
  }
  for (const p of players) {
    if (out.has(p.clientID)) continue;
    const free = PICK_ORDER.find((t) => present.includes(t) && !taken.has(t));
    if (free === undefined) continue;
    out.set(p.clientID, free);
    taken.add(free);
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Every country of the scenario as a player, in scenario order (plan
 * decision 2): picked ones as humans, the rest as nations whose spawn cell
 * is their capital. The name is the country's English name (decision 3).
 */
export function scenarioPlayers(
  players: readonly StartPlayer[],
  scenario: ScenarioIndex,
  random: PseudoRandom,
): { players: PlayerInfo[]; nations: Nation[] } {
  const assigned = assignCountries(players, scenario.countries);
  const humanByTag = new Map<CountryTag, StartPlayer>();
  for (const p of players) {
    const tag = assigned.get(p.clientID);
    if (tag !== undefined) humanByTag.set(tag, p);
  }
  const infos: PlayerInfo[] = [];
  const nations: Nation[] = [];
  for (const tag of scenario.countries) {
    const def = COUNTRIES[tag];
    const human = humanByTag.get(tag);
    if (human !== undefined) {
      infos.push(
        new PlayerInfo(
          def.name,
          PlayerType.Human,
          human.clientID,
          random.nextID(),
          human.isLobbyCreator ?? false,
          null,
          human.friends ?? [],
          null,
          def.flag,
        ),
      );
      continue;
    }
    const info = new PlayerInfo(
      def.name,
      PlayerType.Nation,
      null,
      random.nextID(),
      false,
      null,
      [],
      null,
      def.flag,
    );
    infos.push(info);
    const [x, y] = scenario.capitalXY(tag);
    nations.push(new Nation(new Cell(x, y), info));
  }
  return { players: infos, nations };
}
```

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/WorldWarStartExecution.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { Execution, Game } from "../game/Game";
import { execSnapshotType } from "../snapshot/ExecutionSnapshot";
import type { ExecRecord } from "../snapshot/SnapshotContext";

/**
 * World War 1936 (plan decision 6): there is no spawn phase. Added first,
 * it ends the spawn phase from its init on tick 0, so the executions after
 * it in the same init pass (PlayerExecution …) start that very tick.
 */
export class WorldWarStartExecution implements Execution {
  private active = true;

  init(mg: Game, _ticks: number): void {
    mg.endSpawnPhase();
    this.active = false;
  }

  tick(_ticks: number): void {}

  isActive(): boolean {
    return this.active;
  }

  activeDuringSpawnPhase(): boolean {
    return true;
  }

  snapshot(): ExecRecord {
    return WorldWarStartExecutionSnapshot.write({ active: this.active });
  }

  restoreSnapshot(s: WorldWarStartState): void {
    this.active = s.active;
  }
}

const WorldWarStartStateSchema = z.object({ active: z.boolean() });
type WorldWarStartState = z.infer<typeof WorldWarStartStateSchema>;

export const WorldWarStartExecutionSnapshot = execSnapshotType({
  name: "WorldWarStart",
  version: 1,
  schema: WorldWarStartStateSchema,
  cls: () => WorldWarStartExecution,
});
```

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/WorldWarExecution.ts`:

```ts
import { MessageType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { GameUpdateType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameUpdates";
import { PseudoRandom } from "@openfront/engine-lib/PseudoRandom";
import { zRandom } from "@openfront/engine-lib/snapshot/SnapshotType";
import { z } from "zod";
import { Execution, Game } from "../game/Game";
import { execSnapshotType } from "../snapshot/ExecutionSnapshot";
import type {
  ExecRecord,
  SnapshotReader,
  SnapshotWriter,
} from "../snapshot/SnapshotContext";
import { checkCapitulations, stepTransfers } from "./Capitulation";
import { processHistory, rollHistory } from "./HistoryRun";

/**
 * World War 1936 clock (spec §7): the preparation end, once a day the
 * historical calendar and the capitulation check, every tick the land
 * transfers, and the WorldWar update when the data changed.
 */
export class WorldWarExecution implements Execution {
  private active = true;
  private mg: Game | null = null;
  private random: PseudoRandom;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.random = new PseudoRandom(seed);
  }

  init(mg: Game, _ticks: number): void {
    this.mg = mg;
    const ww = mg.worldWar();
    if (ww !== null && !ww.historyRolled()) {
      rollHistory(ww, this.random, mg.config().historicalAi());
    }
  }

  tick(ticks: number): void {
    const mg = this.mg;
    const ww = mg?.worldWar() ?? null;
    if (mg === null || ww === null) {
      this.active = false;
      return;
    }
    if (ticks === ww.prepEndTick()) {
      mg.displayMessage("events_display.ww1936_prep_over", MessageType.WORLD_WAR, null);
    }
    // Once a day, from the first tick after the preparation minute (day 0
    // runs at prepEndTick).
    if (!ww.inPrep(ticks)) {
      const day = ww.day(ticks);
      if (day !== ww.lastDay()) {
        ww.setLastDay(day);
        processHistory(mg, ww, day, ticks);
        checkCapitulations(mg, ww, ticks);
      }
    }
    stepTransfers(mg, ww);
    const data = ww.takeData();
    if (data !== null) mg.addUpdate({ type: GameUpdateType.WorldWar, data });
  }

  isActive(): boolean {
    return this.active;
  }

  activeDuringSpawnPhase(): boolean {
    return false;
  }

  snapshot(w: SnapshotWriter): ExecRecord {
    return WorldWarExecutionSnapshot.write({
      active: this.active,
      initialized: this.mg !== null,
      random: w.random(this.random),
    });
  }

  restoreSnapshot(s: WorldWarExecState, r: SnapshotReader): void {
    this.active = s.active;
    this.mg = s.initialized ? r.game : null;
    this.random = r.random(s.random);
  }
}

const WorldWarExecStateSchema = z.object({
  active: z.boolean(),
  initialized: z.boolean(),
  random: zRandom(),
});
type WorldWarExecState = z.infer<typeof WorldWarExecStateSchema>;

export const WorldWarExecutionSnapshot = execSnapshotType({
  name: "WorldWar",
  version: 1,
  schema: WorldWarExecStateSchema,
  cls: () => WorldWarExecution,
});
```

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/DeclareWarExecution.ts`:

```ts
import { PlayerID } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { zPlayerRef } from "@openfront/engine-lib/snapshot/SnapshotType";
import { z } from "zod";
import { Execution, Game, Player } from "../game/Game";
import { execSnapshotType } from "../snapshot/ExecutionSnapshot";
import type {
  ExecRecord,
  SnapshotReader,
  SnapshotWriter,
} from "../snapshot/SnapshotContext";
import { declareWar, declareWarBlock } from "./Diplomacy";

/** World War 1936 (spec §7.3): the declare_war intent. */
export class DeclareWarExecution implements Execution {
  private active = true;
  private mg: Game | null = null;

  constructor(
    private player: Player,
    private targetID: PlayerID,
  ) {}

  init(mg: Game, _ticks: number): void {
    this.mg = mg;
  }

  tick(ticks: number): void {
    this.active = false;
    const mg = this.mg;
    const ww = mg?.worldWar() ?? null;
    if (mg === null || ww === null || !mg.hasPlayer(this.targetID)) return;
    const target = mg.player(this.targetID);
    if (declareWarBlock(mg, ww, this.player, target, ticks) !== null) return;
    declareWar(mg, ww, this.player, target, ticks);
  }

  isActive(): boolean {
    return this.active;
  }

  activeDuringSpawnPhase(): boolean {
    return false;
  }

  snapshot(w: SnapshotWriter): ExecRecord {
    return DeclareWarExecutionSnapshot.write({
      active: this.active,
      initialized: this.mg !== null,
      player: w.player(this.player),
      targetID: this.targetID,
    });
  }

  restoreSnapshot(s: DeclareWarState, r: SnapshotReader): void {
    this.active = s.active;
    this.mg = s.initialized ? r.game : null;
    this.player = r.player(s.player);
    this.targetID = s.targetID;
  }
}

const DeclareWarStateSchema = z.object({
  active: z.boolean(),
  initialized: z.boolean(),
  player: zPlayerRef(),
  targetID: z.string(),
});
type DeclareWarState = z.infer<typeof DeclareWarStateSchema>;

export const DeclareWarExecutionSnapshot = execSnapshotType({
  name: "DeclareWar",
  version: 1,
  schema: DeclareWarStateSchema,
  cls: () => DeclareWarExecution,
});
```

```ts
  /**
   * World War 1936 (spec §5.3, plan decision 5): every land tile gets its
   * 1936 owner at once. No tile updates: the screens fill the same owners.
   */
  private assignScenarioTerritory(index: ScenarioIndex): void {
    fillScenarioOwners(this._map, index);
    const n = this._width * this._height;
    for (let t = 0; t < n; t++) {
      const owner = this._map.ownerID(t);
      if (owner === 0) continue;
      (this._playersBySmallID[owner - 1] as PlayerImpl)._tiles.add(t);
    }
    for (let t = 0; t < n; t++) {
      const owner = this._map.ownerID(t);
      if (owner === 0 || !this._map.isBorder(t)) continue;
      (this._playersBySmallID[owner - 1] as PlayerImpl)._borderTiles.add(t);
    }
    for (const p of this._players.values()) {
      p._lastTileChange = this._ticks;
      p._tileChangeVersion++;
    }
    this._territoryVersion++;
  }
```

```ts
  isFriendly(other: Player, treatAFKFriendly: boolean = false): boolean {
    if (other === this) {
      return true;
    }
    if (other.isDisconnected() && !treatAFKFriendly) {
      return false;
    }
    // World War 1936 (plan decision 8): faction members are friends.
    const ww = this.mg.worldWar();
    if (ww !== null && ww.sameFaction(this._smallID, other.smallID())) {
      return true;
    }
    return this.isOnSameTeam(other) || this.isAlliedWith(other);
  }
```

```ts
import { PlayerExecution } from "./execution/PlayerExecution";
import { declareWarBlock } from "./ww1936/Diplomacy";
import { scenarioPlayers } from "./ww1936/ScenarioPlayers";
import { WorldWarExecution } from "./ww1936/WorldWarExecution";
```

2. `createGameRunner`의 본문(`const gr = new GameRunner(` 앞까지)을 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts
  const config = new EngineConfig(gameStart.config, false, gameStart.listed);
  // The normalized config: World War 1936 fixes the map (spec §2.2). It is
  // the very gameStart.config object when the mode is off.
  const gameMap = await loadGameMap(config.gameConfig(), map);
  const random = new PseudoRandom(simpleHash(gameStart.gameID));

  let game: Game;
  if (config.worldWar1936()) {
    // World War 1936 (spec §7.1): every country of the scenario is a player.
    const scenario = gameMap.scenario;
    if (scenario === undefined) {
      throw new Error("World War 1936 needs the world1936 regions and scenario files");
    }
    const { players, nations } = scenarioPlayers(
      gameStart.players,
      scenario,
      random,
    );
    game = createGame(
      players,
      nations,
      gameMap.gameMap,
      gameMap.miniGameMap,
      config,
      gameMap.teamGameSpawnAreas,
      scenario,
    );
  } else {
    const humans = gameStart.players.map((p) => {
      return new PlayerInfo(
        p.username,
        PlayerType.Human,
        p.clientID,
        random.nextID(),
        p.isLobbyCreator ?? false,
        p.clanTag,
        p.friends ?? [],
        p.teamIndex ?? null,
      );
    });
    const nations = createNationsForGame(
      gameStart,
      gameMap.nations,
      gameMap.additionalNations,
      humans.length,
      random,
    );
    game = createGame(
      humans,
      nations,
      gameMap.gameMap,
      gameMap.miniGameMap,
      config,
      gameMap.teamGameSpawnAreas,
    );
  }
```

```ts
// World War 1936 (spec §8.1): pick a country in the lobby (null gives it up).
export class SendPickCountryEvent implements GameEvent {
  constructor(public readonly tag: CountryTag | null) {}
}
```

(맨 위 import에 `import type { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";`)

`src/client/Transport.ts`:
1. import에 `SendPickCountryEvent`(`./LobbyEvents`)와 `ClientPickCountryMessage`(`@openfront/shared/WireSchemas`)를 더한다.
2. 이벤트 클래스들 사이에:

```ts
// World War 1936 (spec §7.3).
export class SendDeclareWarIntentEvent implements GameEvent {
  constructor(public readonly target: PlayerView) {}
}
```

3. 구독 묶음의 `this.subscribe(SendSpectateEvent, …)` 다음에:

```ts
    this.subscribe(SendPickCountryEvent, (e) => {
      this.sendMsg({
        type: "pick_country",
        tag: e.tag,
      } satisfies ClientPickCountryMessage);
    });
    this.subscribe(SendDeclareWarIntentEvent, (e) =>
      this.sendIntent({ type: "declare_war", target: e.target.id() }),
    );
```

`src/client/view/GameView.ts`:
1. import에:

```ts
import { GameUpdateType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameUpdates"; // 이미 있으면 생략
import { COUNTRIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import type { WorldWarData } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import {
  fillScenarioOwners,
  ScenarioIndex,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";
```

2. 필드에:

```ts
  /** World War 1936: the last WorldWar update's data. */
  private _worldWarData: WorldWarData | null = null;
  /** World War 1936: smallID → faction, for the relation matrix (decision 4). */
  private _worldWarTeams: Map<number, string> | null = null;
  private readonly _usernames: Map<ClientID, string>;
```

3. 생성자의 `this._map = this._mapData.gameMap;` 다음에:

```ts
    this._usernames = new Map(humans.map((h) => [h.clientID, h.username]));
    // World War 1936 (plan decision 5): the engine gave every land tile its
    // 1936 owner without tile updates; this view fills the same owners.
    const scenario = this.worldWarScenario();
    if (scenario !== null) {
      fillScenarioOwners(this._map, scenario);
      this._worldWarTeams = new Map();
      scenario.countries.forEach((tag, i) => {
        const faction = COUNTRIES[tag].faction;
        if (faction !== null) this._worldWarTeams!.set(i + 1, faction);
      });
    }
```

4. `update()`의 `spawnPhaseEndUpdate` 처리 다음에:

```ts
    const wwUpdates = gu.updates[GameUpdateType.WorldWar];
    if (wwUpdates.length > 0) {
      this._worldWarData = wwUpdates[wwUpdates.length - 1].data;
    }
```

5. 내 이름 바꿔치기 조건을 `if (pu.clientID !== undefined && pu.clientID === this._myClientID && this.worldWarScenario() === null) {`로 바꾼다(결정 사항 3).
6. `new PlayerView(` 호출의 꾸미기 인자를 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts

            this._cosmetics.get(pu.clientID ?? "") ??
            (pu.playerType === PlayerType.Nation && pu.nationFlag

```

7. `buildRelationMatrix(this._playerStates, this._teams)`를 `buildRelationMatrix(this._playerStates, this._worldWarTeams ?? this._teams)`로 바꾼다.
8. 클래스에 메서드:

```ts
  /** World War 1936: the static scenario; null when the mode is off. */
  worldWarScenario(): ScenarioIndex | null {
    return this._config.worldWar1936() ? (this._mapData.scenario ?? null) : null;
  }

  /** World War 1936: wars, capitulations, the preparation end. */
  worldWarData(): WorldWarData | null {
    return this._worldWarData;
  }

  /** The lobby username of a human player. */
  usernameOf(clientID: ClientID): string | null {
    return this._usernames.get(clientID) ?? null;
  }

  /** World War 1936 (decision 4): a country's flag and colour for everyone. */
  private countryCosmetics(smallID: number | undefined): PlayerCosmetics | null {
    const scenario = this.worldWarScenario();
    if (scenario === null || smallID === undefined) return null;
    const tag = scenario.tagOfSmallId(smallID);
    if (tag === undefined) return null;
    const def = COUNTRIES[tag];
    const hex = (n: number) => n.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
    return {
      flag: `/flags/${def.flag}.svg`,
      color: { color: `#${hex(def.color[0])}${hex(def.color[1])}${hex(def.color[2])}` },
    };
  }
```
