- [ ] **Step 13: 테스트 지도 스크립트와 출력**

`scripts/ww1936/make-test-map.ts`:

```ts
// Builds tests/testdata/maps/ww1936mini (plan decision 17): a 200x120 World
// War 1936 test map. Land rows 0..99, an island at x 20..49, y 106..115,
// sea elsewhere. Run: npx tsx scripts/ww1936/make-test-map.ts
import type { ScenarioData } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/ScenarioTypes";
import { encodeRegions } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Regions";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const W = 200;
const H = 120;
const OUT = path.join(process.cwd(), "tests/testdata/maps/ww1936mini");

const isLand = (x: number, y: number) =>
  y < 100 || (x >= 20 && x < 50 && y >= 106 && y < 116);

type Box = [number, number, number, number]; // x0, x1, y0, y1 (end exclusive)
const REGIONS: {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  nameKo: string;
  owner: "FRA" | "DEU" | "ITA" | "POL" | "SUN" | "GBR";
  vp: number;
  box: Box;
}[] = [
  { id: 1, name: "Paris", nameKo: "파리", owner: "FRA", vp: 21, box: [0, 40, 0, 50] },
  { id: 2, name: "Lyon", nameKo: "리옹", owner: "FRA", vp: 3, box: [0, 40, 50, 100] },
  { id: 3, name: "Berlin", nameKo: "베를린", owner: "DEU", vp: 21, box: [40, 90, 0, 35] },
  { id: 4, name: "Munich", nameKo: "뮌헨", owner: "DEU", vp: 4, box: [40, 90, 35, 70] },
  { id: 5, name: "Rome", nameKo: "로마", owner: "ITA", vp: 21, box: [40, 90, 70, 100] },
  { id: 6, name: "Warsaw", nameKo: "바르샤바", owner: "POL", vp: 21, box: [90, 120, 0, 50] },
  { id: 7, name: "Krakow", nameKo: "크라쿠프", owner: "POL", vp: 3, box: [90, 120, 50, 100] },
  { id: 8, name: "Moscow", nameKo: "모스크바", owner: "SUN", vp: 21, box: [120, 200, 0, 50] },
  { id: 9, name: "Kiev", nameKo: "키이우", owner: "SUN", vp: 6, box: [120, 200, 50, 100] },
  { id: 10, name: "London", nameKo: "런던", owner: "GBR", vp: 21, box: [20, 50, 106, 116] },
];
const SEAS = [
  { id: 40001, name: "North Sea", nameKo: "북해", x0: 0, x1: 100 },
  { id: 40002, name: "Black Sea", nameKo: "흑해", x0: 100, x1: 200 },
];
const CAPITALS: Record<string, [number, number]> = {
  FRA: [20, 25],
  DEU: [65, 17],
  ITA: [65, 85],
  POL: [105, 25],
  SUN: [160, 25],
  GBR: [35, 110],
};

function grid(w: number, h: number, land: (x: number, y: number) => boolean) {
  const g = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) g[y * w + x] = land(x, y) ? 1 : 0;
  return g;
}

/** Half size, water wins (map_generator.go createMiniMap). */
function half(g: Uint8Array, w: number, h: number) {
  const hw = w / 2;
  const hh = h / 2;
  const out = new Uint8Array(hw * hh).fill(1);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++)
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) if (g[y * w + x] === 0) out[(y >> 1) * hw + (x >> 1)] = 0;
  return out;
}

/** map.bin bytes: bit7 land, bit6 shoreline, bit5 ocean, bits0-4 magnitude. */
function pack(g: Uint8Array, w: number, h: number) {
  const n = w * h;
  const dist = new Int32Array(n).fill(-1);
  const queue: number[] = [];
  const nb = (i: number) => {
    const x = i % w;
    const out: number[] = [];
    if (i >= w) out.push(i - w);
    if (x > 0) out.push(i - 1);
    if (x < w - 1) out.push(i + 1);
    if (i < n - w) out.push(i + w);
    return out;
  };
  const shore = new Uint8Array(n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    shore[i] = nb(i).some((j) => g[j] !== g[i]) ? 1 : 0;
    if (g[i] === 0 && shore[i] === 1) {
      dist[i] = 0;
      queue.push(i);
    }
  }
  for (let q = 0; q < queue.length; q++) {
    const i = queue[q];
    for (const j of nb(i)) {
      if (g[j] === 0 && dist[j] === -1) {
        dist[j] = dist[i] + 1;
        queue.push(j);
      }
    }
  }
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(n);
  let land = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (g[i] === 1) {
      bytes[i] = 0x80 | (shore[i] ? 0x40 : 0);
      land++;
    } else {
      bytes[i] = (shore[i] ? 0x40 : 0) | 0x20 | Math.min(31, Math.ceil(Math.max(0, dist[i]) / 2));
    }
  }
  return { bytes, land };
}

const full = grid(W, H, isLand);
const g4 = half(full, W, H);
const g16 = half(g4, W / 2, H / 2);
const m1 = pack(full, W, H);
const m4 = pack(g4, W / 2, H / 2);
const m16 = pack(g16, W / 4, H / 4);

const ids = new Uint16Array(W * H);
const tiles = new Map<number, number>();
for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
    let id = 0;
    if (isLand(x, y)) {
      const r = REGIONS.find(({ box: [x0, x1, y0, y1] }) => x >= x0 && x < x1 && y >= y0 && y < y1);
      if (r === undefined) throw new Error(`land (${x},${y}) has no region`);
      id = r.id;
    } else {
      id = SEAS.find((s) => x >= s.x0 && x < s.x1)!.id;
    }
    ids[y * W + x] = id;
    tiles.set(id, (tiles.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
}

const scenario: ScenarioData = {
  version: 1,
  width: W,
  height: H,
  countries: Object.entries(CAPITALS).map(([tag, capital]) => ({
    tag: tag as ScenarioData["countries"][number]["tag"],
    capital,
  })),
  regions: REGIONS.map((r) => ({
    id: r.id,
    name: r.name,
    nameKo: r.nameKo,
    owner: r.owner,
    vp: r.vp,
    vpTile: [(r.box[0] + r.box[1]) >> 1, (r.box[2] + r.box[3]) >> 1] as [number, number],
    tiles: tiles.get(r.id)!,
    terrain: "plains" as const,
  })),
  seaZones: SEAS.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, name: s.name, nameKo: s.nameKo, tiles: tiles.get(s.id)! })),
};

const manifest = {
  name: "ww1936mini",
  map: { width: W, height: H, num_land_tiles: m1.land },
  map4x: { width: W / 2, height: H / 2, num_land_tiles: m4.land },
  map16x: { width: W / 4, height: H / 4, num_land_tiles: m16.land },
  nations: [],
  ww1936: true,
};

fs.mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, "map.bin"), m1.bytes);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, "map4x.bin"), m4.bytes);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, "map16x.bin"), m16.bytes);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, "regions.bin"), encodeRegions(ids, W, H));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, "scenario.json"), JSON.stringify(scenario, null, 2) + "\n");
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, "manifest.json"), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2) + "\n");
console.log(`ww1936mini: ${m1.land} land tiles, ${REGIONS.length} regions`);
```

Run:

```bash
npx tsx scripts/ww1936/make-test-map.ts
npx prettier --write tests/testdata/maps/ww1936mini/*.json scripts/ww1936/make-test-map.ts
```

Expected: `ww1936mini: 20300 land tiles, 10 regions`, 파일 6개가 생긴다.

- [ ] **Step 14: 테스트 유틸**

`tests/ww1936/util.ts`:

```ts
import {
  Difficulty,
  GameMapSize,
  GameMapType,
  GameMode,
  GameType,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import {
  ErrorUpdate,
  GameUpdateViewData,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameUpdates";
import {
  GameConfig,
  GameStartInfo,
  Intent,
  StampedIntent,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import type { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import type { Game, Player } from "@openfront/engine/game/Game";
import { createGameRunner, GameRunner } from "@openfront/engine/GameRunner";
import { loadMapFiles } from "@openfront/shared/GameMapLoader";
import { TestDataMapLoader } from "../util/ScriptedGame";

export const MINI = "ww1936mini";
export const HUMAN = "HUMAN001";

export interface WwHuman {
  clientID: string;
  country?: CountryTag;
}

export function wwGameStart(
  config: Partial<GameConfig> = {},
  humans: WwHuman[] = [],
): GameStartInfo {
  return {
    gameID: "WWTEST01",
    lobbyCreatedAt: 0,
    config: {
      gameMap: GameMapType.World1936,
      gameMapSize: GameMapSize.Normal,
      gameMode: GameMode.FFA,
      gameType: GameType.Private,
      difficulty: Difficulty.Medium,
      nations: "default",
      bots: 0,
      donateGold: true,
      donateTroops: true,
      infiniteGold: false,
      infiniteTroops: false,
      instantBuild: false,
      randomSpawn: false,
      worldWar1936: true,
      ...config,
    },
    players: humans.map((h, i) => ({
      clientID: h.clientID,
      username: `player${i}`,
      clanTag: null,
      isLobbyCreator: i === 0,
      ...(h.country === undefined ? {} : { country: h.country }),
    })),
  };
}

/** A runner on the test map; `updates` collects every tick's view data. */
export async function wwRunnerCapture(
  config: Partial<GameConfig> = {},
  humans: WwHuman[] = [],
): Promise<{ runner: GameRunner; updates: GameUpdateViewData[] }> {
  const start = wwGameStart(config, humans);
  const updates: GameUpdateViewData[] = [];
  const runner = await createGameRunner(
    start,
    undefined,
    await loadMapFiles(new TestDataMapLoader(MINI), GameMapType.World1936, GameMapSize.Normal),
    (gu: GameUpdateViewData | ErrorUpdate) => {
      if ("errMsg" in gu) throw new Error(gu.errMsg);
      updates.push(gu);
    },
  );
  return { runner, updates };
}

export async function wwRunner(
  config: Partial<GameConfig> = {},
  humans: WwHuman[] = [],
): Promise<GameRunner> {
  return (await wwRunnerCapture(config, humans)).runner;
}

/** Runs n ticks; the intents go into the first one. */
export function step(runner: GameRunner, n = 1, intents: StampedIntent[] = []): void {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    runner.addTurn({
      turnNumber: runner.game.ticks(),
      intents: i === 0 ? intents : [],
    });
    if (!runner.executeNextTick()) {
      throw new Error(`tick ${runner.game.ticks()} did not run`);
    }
  }
}

/** Runs until game.ticks() reaches `tick`. */
export function stepTo(runner: GameRunner, tick: number): void {
  while (runner.game.ticks() < tick) step(runner);
}

export function stamped(clientID: string, intent: Intent): StampedIntent {
  return { ...intent, clientID } as StampedIntent;
}

export function byTag(game: Game, tag: CountryTag): Player {
  const p = game.worldWar()?.playerOfTag(tag) ?? null;
  if (p === null) throw new Error(`no ${tag} in this game`);
  return p;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 15: 실패하는 시작·전쟁 판정 테스트**

`tests/ww1936/ScenarioStart.test.ts`:

```ts
import { GameMapSize, GameMapType, PlayerType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { GameUpdateType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameUpdates";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { loadTerrainMap } from "@openfront/engine-lib/game/TerrainMapLoader";
import { fillScenarioOwners } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";
import { loadMapFiles } from "@openfront/shared/GameMapLoader";
import { TestDataMapLoader } from "../util/ScriptedGame";
import { byTag, HUMAN, MINI, step, wwRunner, wwRunnerCapture } from "./util";

describe("World War 1936 start", () => {
  test("every scenario country is a player, in COUNTRY_TAGS order", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({}, [{ clientID: HUMAN, country: "POL" }]);
    const g = runner.game;
    expect(g.worldWar()!.scenario.countries).toEqual(["DEU", "ITA", "GBR", "FRA", "SUN", "POL"]);
    const players = g.allPlayers();
    expect(players.map((p) => p.smallID())).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]);
    expect(players.map((p) => p.name())).toEqual([
      "Germany",
      "Italy",
      "United Kingdom",
      "France",
      "Soviet Union",
      "Poland",
    ]);
    expect(players.map((p) => p.type())).toEqual([
      PlayerType.Nation,
      PlayerType.Nation,
      PlayerType.Nation,
      PlayerType.Nation,
      PlayerType.Nation,
      PlayerType.Human,
    ]);
    expect(g.playerByClientID(HUMAN)!.name()).toBe("Poland");
  });

  test("duplicate and unknown picks fall back to the next free country", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({}, [
      { clientID: "HUMANAAA", country: "DEU" },
      { clientID: "HUMANBBB", country: "DEU" },
      { clientID: "HUMANCCC", country: "USA" },
      { clientID: "HUMANDDD" },
    ]);
    const name = (id: string) => runner.game.playerByClientID(id)!.name();
    expect(name("HUMANAAA")).toBe("Germany");
    expect(name("HUMANBBB")).toBe("United Kingdom");
    expect(name("HUMANCCC")).toBe("Soviet Union");
    expect(name("HUMANDDD")).toBe("France");
  });

  test("every land tile starts with its 1936 owner", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const scenario = g.worldWar()!.scenario;
    const owners = scenario.ownerByRegion();
    let land = 0;
    g.forEachTile((t) => {
      if (!g.isLand(t)) {
        expect(g.ownerID(t)).toBe(0);
        return;
      }
      land++;
      expect(g.ownerID(t)).toBe(owners[scenario.regionAt(t)]);
    });
    expect(g.allPlayers().reduce((n, p) => n + p.numTilesOwned(), 0)).toBe(land);
    expect(byTag(g, "GBR").numTilesOwned()).toBe(300);
  });

  test("the view fills the same owners as the engine", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const view = await loadTerrainMap(
      await loadMapFiles(new TestDataMapLoader(MINI), GameMapType.World1936, GameMapSize.Normal),
    );
    fillScenarioOwners(view.gameMap, view.scenario!);
    runner.game.forEachTile((t) => {
      expect(view.gameMap.ownerID(t)).toBe(runner.game.ownerID(t));
    });
  });

  test("no tile updates on the opening ticks", async () => {
    const { runner, updates } = await wwRunnerCapture();
    step(runner, 3);
    expect(updates.map((u) => u.packedTileUpdates.length)).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);
  });

  test("the spawn phase ends on tick 0; capitals and half troops", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    for (const p of g.allPlayers()) {
      expect(p.spawnTile()).toBe(ww.scenario.capitalTile(ww.tagOf(p.smallID())));
      expect(p.troops()).toBe(Math.floor((g.config().maxTroops(p) * 50) / 100));
    }
    step(runner);
    expect(g.inSpawnPhase()).toBe(false);
    expect(ww.prepEndTick()).toBe(WW_PREP_TICKS);
  });

  test("the first WorldWar update tells the preparation end", async () => {
    const { runner, updates } = await wwRunnerCapture();
    step(runner, 3);
    const ww = updates.flatMap((u) => u.updates[GameUpdateType.WorldWar]);
    expect(ww).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(ww[0].data).toEqual({ prepEndTick: WW_PREP_TICKS, wars: [], capitulated: [] });
  });
});
```

`tests/ww1936/WarGate.test.ts`:

```ts
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { AttackExecution } from "@openfront/engine/execution/AttackExecution";
import { byTag, step, stepTo, wwRunner } from "./util";

describe("only enemies fight (spec §7.2)", () => {
  test("faction members are friends, neutrals are not", async () => {
    const g = (await wwRunner()).game;
    const deu = byTag(g, "DEU");
    expect(deu.isFriendly(byTag(g, "ITA"))).toBe(true);
    expect(deu.isFriendly(byTag(g, "FRA"))).toBe(false);
    expect(byTag(g, "GBR").isFriendly(byTag(g, "FRA"))).toBe(true);
  });

  test("a war cannot be fought during the preparation minute", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const deu = byTag(g, "DEU");
    const pol = byTag(g, "POL");
    g.worldWar()!.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), 0);
    step(runner, 2);
    expect(deu.canAttackPlayer(pol)).toBe(false);
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    expect(deu.canAttackPlayer(pol)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("neutrals and faction members cannot be attacked, enemies can", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const deu = byTag(g, "DEU");
    expect(deu.canAttackPlayer(byTag(g, "FRA"))).toBe(false);
    expect(deu.canAttackPlayer(byTag(g, "ITA"))).toBe(false);
    g.worldWar()!.setWar(deu.smallID(), byTag(g, "FRA").smallID(), g.ticks());
    expect(deu.canAttackPlayer(byTag(g, "FRA"))).toBe(true);
    expect(byTag(g, "FRA").canAttackPlayer(deu)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("an attack on a neutral never starts; on an enemy it does", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const deu = byTag(g, "DEU");
    const pol = byTag(g, "POL");
    g.addExecution(new AttackExecution(1000, deu, pol.id(), null));
    step(runner, 2);
    expect(deu.outgoingAttacks().filter((a) => a.target() === pol)).toHaveLength(0);
    g.worldWar()!.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    g.addExecution(new AttackExecution(1000, deu, pol.id(), null));
    step(runner, 2);
    expect(
      deu.outgoingAttacks().filter((a) => a.target() === pol).length,
    ).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe("World War 1936 in a host's patch", () => {

    expect(target.strategicWeapons).toBeUndefined();
```

### Task 2: 지도 생성기 확장 — 주 번호 확정, 조각 합치기, `regions.bin`, `scenario.json`

**Files:**
- Create: `map-generator/regions.go`, `map-generator/regions_test.go`
- Modify: `map-generator/main.go` (`processMap`이 쓴 JSON 파일 목록을 돌려주고, `"ww1936": true` 지도에서 `writeScenarioFiles`를 부른다), `map-generator/README.md` (절 하나)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `packTerrain`이 만든 `result.Map.Data`(행 우선, bit7 땅, bit0-4 높이, `0b10011111` 지나갈 수 없음), `LoggerFromContext`
- Produces: `resources/maps/<지도>/regions.bin`(spec §4.7 RGN1, engine-lib `decodeRegions`와 같은 형식), `resources/maps/<지도>/scenario.json`(engine-api `ScenarioDataSchema`). 입력은 `map-generator/scenarios/<지도>/regions.png`(R<<8|G)와 `scenario.src.json`(Task 3이 쓴다: `{version, countries: [{tag, capital: [x, y]}], regions: [{id, name, nameKo, owner, vp, vpTile?}], seaZones: [{id, name, nameKo}]}`).

이 Task의 코드는 이 계획을 쓰면서 Go 1.27로 빌드·`go vet`·`go test`를 통과시킨 것이다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 Go 테스트**

`map-generator/regions_test.go`:


```go
package main

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// grid builds packed terrain from rows of 'L' (land, plains), 'M' (land,
// magnitude 25), '~' (water) and '#' (impassable).
func grid(rows ...string) ([]byte, int, int) {
	h := len(rows)
	w := len(rows[0])
	packed := make([]byte, w*h)
	for y, row := range rows {
		for x, c := range row {
			switch c {
			case 'L':
				packed[y*w+x] = 0x80
			case 'M':
				packed[y*w+x] = 0x80 | 25
			case '#':
				packed[y*w+x] = impassableByte
			default:
				packed[y*w+x] = 0x20
			}
		}
	}
	return packed, w, h
}

func decodeRLE(t *testing.T, bin []byte, w, h int) []uint16 {
	t.Helper()
	if string(bin[:4]) != "RGN1" {
		t.Fatalf("magic %q", bin[:4])
	}
	if int(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(bin[4:])) != w || int(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(bin[8:])) != h {
		t.Fatalf("size")
	}
	runs := int(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(bin[12:]))
	out := make([]uint16, 0, w*h)
	pos := 16
	for r := 0; r < runs; r++ {
		l, k := binary.Uvarint(bin[pos:])
		pos += k
		id, k2 := binary.Uvarint(bin[pos:])
		pos += k2
		for i := uint64(0); i < l; i++ {
			out = append(out, uint16(id))
		}
	}
	if pos != len(bin) || len(out) != w*h {
		t.Fatalf("runs cover %d of %d tiles", len(out), w*h)
	}
	return out
}

func TestFinalizeFillsLandAndWaterFromNeighbours(t *testing.T) {
	packed, w, h := grid(
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL~~~~",
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL~~~~",
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL~~~~",
	)
	raw := make([]uint16, w*h)
	raw[0] = 1      // region 1 seed at the left
	raw[19] = 2     // region 2 seed at x=19
	raw[23] = 40001 // a sea zone seed
	raw[20] = 1     // a land id on water: dropped
	ids, err := finalizeRegions(packed, raw, w, h)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for i := 0; i < w*h; i++ {
		if packedIsWater(packed[i]) && ids[i] != 40001 {
			t.Fatalf("water tile %d has %d", i, ids[i])
		}
		if packedIsLand(packed[i]) && ids[i] != 1 && ids[i] != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("land tile %d has %d", i, ids[i])
		}
	}
	if ids[0] != 1 || ids[19] != 2 || ids[20] != 40001 {
		t.Fatalf("seeds moved: %v", ids)
	}
}

func TestFinalizeGivesUnseededLakesNewZones(t *testing.T) {
	packed, w, h := grid(
		"~~LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL~~",
		"~~LLLLLL~~~~~LLLLLLLLLLL~~",
		"~~LLLLLL~~~~~LLLLLLLLLLL~~",
		"~~LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL~~",
	)
	raw := make([]uint16, w*h)
	raw[2] = 3
	raw[0] = 40005
	ids, err := finalizeRegions(packed, raw, w, h)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// New zones are numbered in tile order: the right-hand strip (row 0)
	// comes before the lake (row 1).
	if ids[25] != 40006 {
		t.Fatalf("right-hand water %d, want 40006", ids[25])
	}
	if lake := ids[1*w+8]; lake != 40007 {
		t.Fatalf("lake id %d, want 40007", lake)
	}
}

func TestSmallPiecesJoinTheirLongestNeighbour(t *testing.T) {
	packed, w, h := grid(
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL",
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL",
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL",
		"LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL",
	)
	raw := make([]uint16, w*h)
	for y := 0; y < h; y++ {
		for x := 0; x < w; x++ {
			switch {
			case x < 10:
				raw[y*w+x] = 1
			default:
				raw[y*w+x] = 2
			}
		}
	}
	// A 2x2 piece of region 5 inside region 2, and one tile of region 1
	// stranded inside region 2.
	raw[1*w+14], raw[1*w+15], raw[2*w+14], raw[2*w+15] = 5, 5, 5, 5
	raw[0*w+18] = 1
	ids, err := finalizeRegions(packed, raw, w, h)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, i := range []int{1*w + 14, 1*w + 15, 2*w + 14, 2*w + 15, 0*w + 18} {
		if ids[i] != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("tile %d is %d, want 2", i, ids[i])
		}
	}
}

func TestLandWithoutAnyRegionIsAnError(t *testing.T) {
	packed, w, h := grid("LL~~LL")
	raw := []uint16{1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0}
	if _, err := finalizeRegions(packed, raw, w, h); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "no region") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRegionsRLERoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	ids := []uint16{1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 40001, 40001, 0, 300, 300, 300, 300}
	bin := encodeRegionsRLE(ids, 4, 3)
	got := decodeRLE(t, bin, 4, 3)
	for i := range ids {
		if got[i] != ids[i] {
			t.Fatalf("tile %d: %d != %d", i, got[i], ids[i])
		}
	}
}

func TestBuildScenarioCountsTilesAndChecksCapitals(t *testing.T) {
	packed, w, h := grid(
		"LLLLMMMM~~",
		"LLLLMMMM~~",
		"LLLLMMMM~~",
	)
	ids := make([]uint16, w*h)
	for y := 0; y < h; y++ {
		for x := 0; x < w; x++ {
			switch {
			case x < 4:
				ids[y*w+x] = 1
			case x < 8:
				ids[y*w+x] = 2
			default:
				ids[y*w+x] = 40001
			}
		}
	}
	src := scenarioSrc{
		Version:   1,
		Countries: []srcCountry{{Tag: "DEU", Capital: [2]int{1, 1}}, {Tag: "POL", Capital: [2]int{5, 1}}},
		Regions: []srcRegion{
			{ID: 1, Name: "Berlin", NameKo: "베를린", Owner: "DEU", VP: 21, VPTile: &[2]int{1, 1}},
			{ID: 2, Name: "Warsaw", NameKo: "바르샤바", Owner: "POL", VP: 3, VPTile: &[2]int{0, 0}},
			{ID: 3, Name: "Gone", NameKo: "사라짐", Owner: "POL", VP: 1},
		},
		SeaZones: []srcSea{{ID: 40001, Name: "Baltic Sea", NameKo: "발트해"}},
	}
	out, notes, err := buildScenario(src, ids, packed, w, h)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(out.Regions) != 2 || len(notes) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("regions %v notes %v", out.Regions, notes)
	}
	if out.Regions[0].Tiles != 12 || out.Regions[0].Terrain != "plains" || out.Regions[0].VPTile != [2]int{1, 1} {
		t.Fatalf("region 1: %+v", out.Regions[0])
	}
	// Warsaw's given VP tile is in region 1, so it gets its centre instead.
	if out.Regions[1].Terrain != "mountain" || ids[out.Regions[1].VPTile[1]*w+out.Regions[1].VPTile[0]] != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("region 2: %+v", out.Regions[1])
	}
	if len(out.SeaZones) != 1 || out.SeaZones[0].Tiles != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("seas %v", out.SeaZones)
	}

	src.Countries[1].Capital = [2]int{1, 2}
	if _, _, err := buildScenario(src, ids, packed, w, h); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "own land") {
		t.Fatalf("capital check: %v", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `regions.go`**

`map-generator/regions.go`:


```go
package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"image/png"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
)

// World War 1936 scenario maps (info.json "ww1936": true). The map's region
// raster and region list come from scenarios/<map>/ (drawn by
// cmd/ww1936build); this file fixes them to the terrain the generator kept
// and writes regions.bin and scenario.json next to map.bin. See
// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-world-war-1936-design.md §4.

const (
	// Land regions are 1..seaZoneBase-1, sea zones seaZoneBase+1..65535.
	seaZoneBase = 40000
	// Pieces of a region smaller than this many tiles join a neighbour.
	minRegionFragment = 20
	// packTerrain's byte for impassable terrain.
	impassableByte = 0b10011111
)

func packedIsLand(b byte) bool  { return b&0x80 != 0 && b != impassableByte }
func packedIsWater(b byte) bool { return b&0x80 == 0 }

// forNeighbours4 calls fn for the orthogonal neighbours of tile i of a
// row-major grid of width w and n tiles: up, left, right, down.
func forNeighbours4(i, w, n int, fn func(j int)) {
	x := i % w
	if i >= w {
		fn(i - w)
	}
	if x > 0 {
		fn(i - 1)
	}
	if x < w-1 {
		fn(i + 1)
	}
	if i+w < n {
		fn(i + w)
	}
}

// decodeRegionIDs reads a region raster (id = R<<8 | G; 0 = none) cropped to
// width x height like the terrain.
func decodeRegionIDs(path string, width, height int) ([]uint16, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	img, err := png.Decode(f)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
	}
	b := img.Bounds()
	if b.Dx() < width || b.Dy() < height {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s is %dx%d, the map %dx%d", path, b.Dx(), b.Dy(), width, height)
	}
	ids := make([]uint16, width*height)
	for y := 0; y < height; y++ {
		for x := 0; x < width; x++ {
			r, g, _, _ := img.At(b.Min.X+x, b.Min.Y+y).RGBA()
			ids[y*width+x] = uint16((r>>8)<<8 | (g >> 8))
		}
	}
	return ids, nil
}

// finalizeRegions gives every land tile a land region and every water tile a
// sea zone on the terrain as packed (after small islands and lakes went):
// tiles without one take their nearest neighbour's (4-neighbour BFS, seeds in
// tile order), water bodies no zone reaches get new zones in tile order, and
// pieces under minRegionFragment tiles join the neighbour they share the
// longest edge with.
func finalizeRegions(packed []byte, raw []uint16, w, h int) ([]uint16, error) {
	n := w * h
	if len(packed) != n || len(raw) != n {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("regions: %d terrain bytes, %d ids, %dx%d map", len(packed), len(raw), w, h)
	}
	isLand := func(i int) bool { return packedIsLand(packed[i]) }
	isWater := func(i int) bool { return packedIsWater(packed[i]) }
	ids := make([]uint16, n)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		id := raw[i]
		switch {
		case packed[i] == impassableByte:
		case isLand(i):
			if id >= 1 && id < seaZoneBase {
				ids[i] = id
			}
		default:
			if id > seaZoneBase {
				ids[i] = id
			}
		}
	}
	floodFill(ids, w, n, isLand)
	floodFill(ids, w, n, isWater)

	next := seaZoneBase + 1
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if isWater(i) && int(ids[i]) >= next {
			next = int(ids[i]) + 1
		}
	}
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if !isWater(i) || ids[i] != 0 {
			continue
		}
		if next > 0xffff {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("regions: more than %d sea zones", 0xffff-seaZoneBase)
		}
		fillComponent(ids, w, n, i, uint16(next), isWater)
		next++
	}
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if isLand(i) && ids[i] == 0 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("regions: land tile (%d,%d) has no region and touches none", i%w, i/w)
		}
	}
	for pass := 0; pass < 4; pass++ {
		changed := mergeFragments(ids, w, n, isLand)
		changed = mergeFragments(ids, w, n, isWater) || changed
		if !changed {
			break
		}
	}
	return ids, nil
}

// floodFill spreads ids into tiles of the same kind that have none.
func floodFill(ids []uint16, w, n int, kind func(int) bool) {
	queue := make([]int32, 0, 1024)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if kind(i) && ids[i] != 0 {
			queue = append(queue, int32(i))
		}
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := int(queue[head])
		forNeighbours4(i, w, n, func(j int) {
			if ids[j] == 0 && kind(j) {
				ids[j] = ids[i]
				queue = append(queue, int32(j))
			}
		})
	}
}

// fillComponent gives id to the id-less tiles of one kind connected to start.
func fillComponent(ids []uint16, w, n, start int, id uint16, kind func(int) bool) {
	ids[start] = id
	queue := []int32{int32(start)}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		forNeighbours4(int(queue[head]), w, n, func(j int) {
			if ids[j] == 0 && kind(j) {
				ids[j] = id
				queue = append(queue, int32(j))
			}
		})
	}
}

// mergeFragments hands every connected piece of a region smaller than
// minRegionFragment tiles to the neighbouring region of the same kind it
// shares the longest edge with (ties: the smaller id). A piece with no such
// neighbour (a small island) keeps its id. Reports whether anything changed.
func mergeFragments(ids []uint16, w, n int, kind func(int) bool) bool {
	comp := make([]int32, n)
	var pieces [][]int32
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if comp[i] != 0 || ids[i] == 0 || !kind(i) {
			continue
		}
		c := int32(len(pieces) + 1)
		comp[i] = c
		tiles := []int32{int32(i)}
		for head := 0; head < len(tiles); head++ {
			t := int(tiles[head])
			forNeighbours4(t, w, n, func(j int) {
				if comp[j] == 0 && ids[j] == ids[t] && kind(j) {
					comp[j] = c
					tiles = append(tiles, int32(j))
				}
			})
		}
		pieces = append(pieces, tiles)
	}
	changed := false
	for _, tiles := range pieces {
		if len(tiles) >= minRegionFragment {
			continue
		}
		own := ids[tiles[0]]
		edges := map[uint16]int{}
		for _, t := range tiles {
			forNeighbours4(int(t), w, n, func(j int) {
				if ids[j] != own && ids[j] != 0 && kind(j) {
					edges[ids[j]]++
				}
			})
		}
		best, bestN := uint16(0), 0
		for id, k := range edges {
			if k > bestN || (k == bestN && id < best) {
				best, bestN = id, k
			}
		}
		if best == 0 {
			continue
		}
		for _, t := range tiles {
			ids[t] = best
		}
		changed = true
	}
	return changed
}

// encodeRegionsRLE writes regions.bin (spec §4.7): "RGN1", u32 width, u32
// height, u32 run count, then (uvarint length, uvarint id) runs.
func encodeRegionsRLE(ids []uint16, w, h int) []byte {
	var body []byte
	runs := 0
	for i := 0; i < len(ids); {
		j := i + 1
		for j < len(ids) && ids[j] == ids[i] {
			j++
		}
		body = binary.AppendUvarint(body, uint64(j-i))
		body = binary.AppendUvarint(body, uint64(ids[i]))
		runs++
		i = j
	}
	out := make([]byte, 16, 16+len(body))
	copy(out, "RGN1")
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(out[4:], uint32(w))
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(out[8:], uint32(h))
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(out[12:], uint32(runs))
	return append(out, body...)
}

// scenario.src.json as cmd/ww1936build writes it.
type srcCountry struct {
	Tag     string `json:"tag"`
	Capital [2]int `json:"capital"`
}

type srcRegion struct {
	ID     int     `json:"id"`
	Name   string  `json:"name"`
	NameKo string  `json:"nameKo"`
	Owner  string  `json:"owner"`
	VP     int     `json:"vp"`
	VPTile *[2]int `json:"vpTile,omitempty"`
}

type srcSea struct {
	ID     int    `json:"id"`
	Name   string `json:"name"`
	NameKo string `json:"nameKo"`
}

type scenarioSrc struct {
	Version   int          `json:"version"`
	Countries []srcCountry `json:"countries"`
	Regions   []srcRegion  `json:"regions"`
	SeaZones  []srcSea     `json:"seaZones"`
}

// scenario.json (engine-api ScenarioDataSchema).
type outRegion struct {
	ID      int    `json:"id"`
	Name    string `json:"name"`
	NameKo  string `json:"nameKo"`
	Owner   string `json:"owner"`
	VP      int    `json:"vp"`
	VPTile  [2]int `json:"vpTile"`
	Tiles   int    `json:"tiles"`
	Terrain string `json:"terrain"`
}

type outSea struct {
	ID     int    `json:"id"`
	Name   string `json:"name"`
	NameKo string `json:"nameKo"`
	Tiles  int    `json:"tiles"`
}

type scenarioOut struct {
	Version   int          `json:"version"`
	Width     int          `json:"width"`
	Height    int          `json:"height"`
	Countries []srcCountry `json:"countries"`
	Regions   []outRegion  `json:"regions"`
	SeaZones  []outSea     `json:"seaZones"`
}

var terrainClasses = [3]string{"plains", "highland", "mountain"}

// regionCenters returns, per land region id, the tile farthest (in 4-steps)
// from the region's edge; ties go to the smaller tile index.
func regionCenters(ids []uint16, w, h int) map[int]int {
	n := w * h
	dist := make([]int32, n)
	queue := make([]int32, 0, 1024)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		dist[i] = -1
		id := ids[i]
		if id == 0 || id >= seaZoneBase {
			continue
		}
		x := i % w
		edge := x == 0 || x == w-1 || i < w || i >= n-w
		if !edge {
			forNeighbours4(i, w, n, func(j int) {
				if ids[j] != id {
					edge = true
				}
			})
		}
		if edge {
			dist[i] = 0
			queue = append(queue, int32(i))
		}
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := int(queue[head])
		forNeighbours4(i, w, n, func(j int) {
			if dist[j] == -1 && ids[j] == ids[i] {
				dist[j] = dist[i] + 1
				queue = append(queue, int32(j))
			}
		})
	}
	best := map[int]int{}
	bestD := map[int]int32{}
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		id := int(ids[i])
		if id == 0 || id >= seaZoneBase {
			continue
		}
		if d, ok := bestD[id]; !ok || dist[i] > d {
			bestD[id] = dist[i]
			best[id] = i
		}
	}
	return best
}

// buildScenario fills in tile counts, terrain classes and victory-point
// tiles, drops regions that lost every tile, and checks that every country
// keeps a region and that its capital is on its own land.
func buildScenario(src scenarioSrc, ids []uint16, packed []byte, w, h int) (scenarioOut, []string, error) {
	n := w * h
	tiles := map[int]int{}
	terrain := map[int]*[3]int{}
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		id := int(ids[i])
		if id == 0 {
			continue
		}
		tiles[id]++
		if id < seaZoneBase {
			mag := int(packed[i] & 0x1f)
			class := 0
			if mag >= 20 {
				class = 2
			} else if mag >= 10 {
				class = 1
			}
			if terrain[id] == nil {
				terrain[id] = &[3]int{}
			}
			terrain[id][class]++
		}
	}
	centers := regionCenters(ids, w, h)
	out := scenarioOut{Version: 1, Width: w, Height: h, Countries: src.Countries}
	var notes []string
	known := map[int]bool{}
	regionCount := map[string]int{}
	byID := map[int]outRegion{}
	for _, r := range src.Regions {
		known[r.ID] = true
		if tiles[r.ID] == 0 {
			notes = append(notes, fmt.Sprintf("region %d %q (%s) lost every tile", r.ID, r.Name, r.Owner))
			continue
		}
		var vpTile [2]int
		if v := r.VPTile; v != nil && v[0] >= 0 && v[0] < w && v[1] >= 0 && v[1] < h && int(ids[v[1]*w+v[0]]) == r.ID {
			vpTile = *v
		} else {
			c := centers[r.ID]
			vpTile = [2]int{c % w, c / w}
		}
		t := terrain[r.ID]
		class := 0
		for k := 1; k < 3; k++ {
			if t[k] > t[class] {
				class = k
			}
		}
		o := outRegion{ID: r.ID, Name: r.Name, NameKo: r.NameKo, Owner: r.Owner, VP: r.VP, VPTile: vpTile, Tiles: tiles[r.ID], Terrain: terrainClasses[class]}
		out.Regions = append(out.Regions, o)
		byID[r.ID] = o
		regionCount[r.Owner]++
	}
	for id := range tiles {
		if id < seaZoneBase && !known[id] {
			return out, notes, fmt.Errorf("region id %d is on the map but not in scenario.src.json", id)
		}
	}
	for _, c := range src.Countries {
		if regionCount[c.Tag] == 0 {
			return out, notes, fmt.Errorf("country %s has no region left", c.Tag)
		}
		x, y := c.Capital[0], c.Capital[1]
		if x < 0 || x >= w || y < 0 || y >= h {
			return out, notes, fmt.Errorf("capital of %s is off the map", c.Tag)
		}
		r, ok := byID[int(ids[y*w+x])]
		if !ok || r.Owner != c.Tag {
			return out, notes, fmt.Errorf("capital of %s at (%d,%d) is not on its own land", c.Tag, x, y)
		}
	}
	seaKnown := map[int]bool{}
	for _, s := range src.SeaZones {
		seaKnown[s.ID] = true
		if tiles[s.ID] == 0 {
			continue
		}
		out.SeaZones = append(out.SeaZones, outSea{ID: s.ID, Name: s.Name, NameKo: s.NameKo, Tiles: tiles[s.ID]})
	}
	for id, k := range tiles {
		if id > seaZoneBase && !seaKnown[id] {
			out.SeaZones = append(out.SeaZones, outSea{ID: id, Name: "Lake", NameKo: "호수", Tiles: k})
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out.Regions, func(a, b int) bool { return out.Regions[a].ID < out.Regions[b].ID })
	sort.Slice(out.SeaZones, func(a, b int) bool { return out.SeaZones[a].ID < out.SeaZones[b].ID })
	return out, notes, nil
}

// writeScenarioFiles reads scenarios/<name>/regions.png and scenario.src.json
// and writes regions.bin and scenario.json into mapDir. Returns the files to
// format with Prettier.
func writeScenarioFiles(name, mapDir string, packed []byte, w, h int) ([]string, []string, error) {
	srcDir := filepath.Join("scenarios", name)
	raw, err := decodeRegionIDs(filepath.Join(srcDir, "regions.png"), w, h)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	ids, err := finalizeRegions(packed, raw, w, h)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	srcBytes, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(srcDir, "scenario.src.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	var src scenarioSrc
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(srcBytes))
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	if err := dec.Decode(&src); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("scenario.src.json: %w", err)
	}
	out, notes, err := buildScenario(src, ids, packed, w, h)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(mapDir, "regions.bin"), encodeRegionsRLE(ids, w, h), 0644); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	js, err := json.MarshalIndent(out, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	scenarioPath := filepath.Join(mapDir, "scenario.json")
	if err := os.WriteFile(scenarioPath, append(js, '\n'), 0644); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return []string{scenarioPath}, notes, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `processMap`에 연결**

`map-generator/main.go`:
1. `processMap`의 반환 타입을 `([]string, error)`로 바꾸고, 함수 안의 모든 `return "", ` 를 `return nil, `로 바꾼다(다른 함수의 `return "", `는 그대로). 주석 셋째 줄을 `// On success it returns the JSON files it wrote (manifest.json, and for a World War 1936 map scenario.json), so callers can format them with Prettier.`로 바꾼다.
2. `thumbnail.webp`를 쓴 `if` 블록 다음에:

```go
	// World War 1936 scenario maps (regions.go): regions.bin and scenario.json
	// from scenarios/<map>/, fixed to the terrain kept above.
	var written []string
	if ww, _ := manifest["ww1936"].(bool); ww && !isTest {
		paths, notes, err := writeScenarioFiles(name, mapDir, result.Map.Data, result.Map.Width, result.Map.Height)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("map %s: %w", name, err)
		}
		for _, note := range notes {
			LoggerFromContext(ctx).Info(note)
		}
		written = append(written, paths...)
	}
```

3. 마지막 `return manifestOutPath, nil`을 `return append([]string{manifestOutPath}, written...), nil`로.
4. `loadTerrainMaps`의 `type result struct { manifestPath string; err error }`를 `type result struct { paths []string; err error }`로, `manifestPath, err := processMap(…)` / `result{manifestPath, err}`를 `paths, err := processMap(…)` / `result{paths, err}`로, `manifestPaths = append(manifestPaths, r.manifestPath)`를 `manifestPaths = append(manifestPaths, r.paths...)`로 바꾼다.

- [ ] **Step 4: README 절**

`map-generator/README.md`의 "## Notes" 앞에:

```markdown
## World War 1936 scenario maps

A map whose `info.json` has `"ww1936": true` also gets `regions.bin` (a region or sea-zone id per tile, run-length coded) and `scenario.json` (the regions, sea zones and countries). Their sources are `scenarios/<map>/regions.png` and `scenarios/<map>/scenario.src.json`, drawn by `go run ./cmd/ww1936build` (see `scenarios/world1936/README.md`). The generator fixes the ids to the terrain it keeps: land without a region takes its nearest region, pieces under 20 tiles join a neighbour, and regions that lost every tile (removed islets) are dropped and logged.
```

### Task 3: 원본 그리기 도구 `cmd/ww1936build`

**Files:**
- Create: `map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/{geo,owners,regions,seas,cities,terrain,output,crosscheck,debugpng,main}.go`, `map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/build_test.go`
- Create: `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/fetch.sh`, `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/README.md`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (`map-generator/.cache/`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 받은 자료(Natural Earth 10m GeoJSON 5개, NASA `gebco_08_rev_elev_21600x10800.png`), Task 4의 입력 표(`countries.json`, `owners1936.json`, `vp_cities.json`, 선택 `region_names.json`)
- Produces: `map-generator/assets/maps/world1936/image.png`(생성기 입력 지형: 물 `(0,0,106)`, 땅 회색, 파랑 = 140 + 2×높이단계), `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/regions.png`(주·해역 번호 R<<8|G), `scenario.src.json`(Task 2가 읽는 형식). 기본 매개변수로 땅 주 약 1,300개(생성기가 사라진 작은 섬을 빼면 약 1,230개), 해역 149개가 나온다(이 계획을 쓰면서 실제 자료로 돌린 결과).

이 Task의 코드는 이 계획을 쓰면서 실제 자료로 끝까지 돌려 본 것이다(전 과정 약 20초, 메모리 약 3GB).

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/build_test.go`:


```go
package main

import (
	"math"
	"testing"
)

func TestProjectionSizeAndRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	p := NewProjection(4000, -60, 84)
	if p.Width != 4000 || p.Height != 2036 {
		t.Fatalf("size %dx%d, want 4000x2036", p.Width, p.Height)
	}
	for _, c := range [][2]float64{{13.4, 52.52}, {-77.04, 38.91}, {139.69, 35.69}, {0, 0}, {174.78, -41.29}} {
		px, ok := p.Pixel(c[0], c[1])
		if !ok {
			t.Fatalf("%v is off the map", c)
		}
		lon, lat := p.LonLat(px%p.Width, px/p.Width)
		if math.Abs(lon-c[0]) > 0.1 || math.Abs(lat-c[1]) > 0.1 {
			t.Fatalf("%v came back as %.3f, %.3f", c, lon, lat)
		}
	}
	if _, ok := p.Pixel(0, -70); ok {
		t.Fatal("Antarctica is on the map")
	}
}

func TestFillPolygonEvenOdd(t *testing.T) {
	p := NewProjection(360, -60, 84)
	square := Ring{{0, 0}, {10, 0}, {10, 10}, {0, 10}, {0, 0}}
	hole := Ring{{2, 2}, {8, 2}, {8, 8}, {2, 8}, {2, 2}}
	full, holed := 0, 0
	fillPolygon(p, Polygon{square}, func(int) { full++ })
	fillPolygon(p, Polygon{square, hole}, func(int) { holed++ })
	if full == 0 || holed == 0 || holed >= full {
		t.Fatalf("square %d pixels, with a hole %d", full, holed)
	}
	// 10 degrees of longitude are 10 pixels at this width.
	x0, _ := p.ToPixel(0, 5)
	x1, _ := p.ToPixel(10, 5)
	if math.Abs((x1-x0)-10) > 1e-9 {
		t.Fatalf("10 degrees are %.3f pixels", x1-x0)
	}
}

func TestTerrainBlueBands(t *testing.T) {
	cases := map[int]uint8{0: 140, 19: 158, 20: 160, 59: 178, 60: 180, 255: 200}
	for e, want := range cases {
		if got := terrainBlue(e); got != want {
			t.Fatalf("elevation %d: blue %d, want %d", e, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestSplitPixelsCoversEverything(t *testing.T) {
	w, h := 60, 20
	n := w * h
	var pix []int32
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		pix = append(pix, int32(i))
	}
	mark := make([]int32, n)
	parts := splitPixels(pix, 3, w, n, mark, 1)
	if len(parts) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("%d parts", len(parts))
	}
	total := 0
	for _, part := range parts {
		if len(part) < n/6 {
			t.Fatalf("a part of %d pixels", len(part))
		}
		total += len(part)
	}
	if total != n {
		t.Fatalf("parts cover %d of %d pixels", total, n)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 도구 코드**


`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/geo.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"os"
	"sort"
)

// Ring is a closed ring of [lon, lat] points; Polygon is an outer ring and
// its holes (filled with the even-odd rule, so holes need no special case).
type Ring [][2]float64
type Polygon []Ring

// Feature is a GeoJSON feature reduced to what the tool reads.
type Feature struct {
	Props    map[string]any
	Polygons []Polygon
	Point    *[2]float64
}

func (f Feature) Str(key string) string {
	v, _ := f.Props[key].(string)
	return v
}

func (f Feature) Num(key string) float64 {
	v, _ := f.Props[key].(float64)
	return v
}

func readFeatures(path string) ([]Feature, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var fc struct {
		Features []struct {
			Properties map[string]any `json:"properties"`
			Geometry   *struct {
				Type        string          `json:"type"`
				Coordinates json.RawMessage `json:"coordinates"`
			} `json:"geometry"`
		} `json:"features"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &fc); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
	}
	out := make([]Feature, 0, len(fc.Features))
	for _, f := range fc.Features {
		feat := Feature{Props: f.Properties}
		if g := f.Geometry; g != nil {
			switch g.Type {
			case "Polygon":
				var c [][][]float64
				if err := json.Unmarshal(g.Coordinates, &c); err != nil {
					return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
				}
				feat.Polygons = []Polygon{toPolygon(c)}
			case "MultiPolygon":
				var c [][][][]float64
				if err := json.Unmarshal(g.Coordinates, &c); err != nil {
					return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
				}
				for _, p := range c {
					feat.Polygons = append(feat.Polygons, toPolygon(p))
				}
			case "Point":
				var c []float64
				if err := json.Unmarshal(g.Coordinates, &c); err != nil {
					return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
				}
				feat.Point = &[2]float64{c[0], c[1]}
			}
		}
		out = append(out, feat)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func toPolygon(c [][][]float64) Polygon {
	p := make(Polygon, 0, len(c))
	for _, ring := range c {
		r := make(Ring, 0, len(ring))
		for _, pt := range ring {
			r = append(r, [2]float64{pt[0], pt[1]})
		}
		p = append(p, r)
	}
	return p
}

// Projection is the Miller cylindrical projection of spec §4.1.
type Projection struct {
	Width, Height  int
	LatMin, LatMax float64
	yTop           float64
	scale          float64 // pixels per radian
}

func millerY(latDeg float64) float64 {
	phi := latDeg * math.Pi / 180
	return 1.25 * math.Log(math.Tan(math.Pi/4+0.4*phi))
}

func millerLat(y float64) float64 {
	return (2.5*math.Atan(math.Exp(0.8*y)) - 0.625*math.Pi) * 180 / math.Pi
}

func NewProjection(width int, latMin, latMax float64) Projection {
	p := Projection{Width: width, LatMin: latMin, LatMax: latMax}
	p.scale = float64(width) / (2 * math.Pi)
	p.yTop = millerY(latMax)
	h := int(math.Floor((p.yTop - millerY(latMin)) * p.scale))
	p.Height = h - h%4
	return p
}

// ToPixel gives fractional pixel coordinates (x right, y down).
func (p Projection) ToPixel(lon, lat float64) (float64, float64) {
	lat = math.Max(-89.5, math.Min(89.5, lat))
	return (lon + 180) / 360 * float64(p.Width), (p.yTop - millerY(lat)) * p.scale
}

// Pixel is the pixel a point falls in, and whether it is on the map.
func (p Projection) Pixel(lon, lat float64) (int, bool) {
	fx, fy := p.ToPixel(lon, lat)
	x, y := int(math.Floor(fx)), int(math.Floor(fy))
	if x < 0 || x >= p.Width || y < 0 || y >= p.Height {
		return 0, false
	}
	return y*p.Width + x, true
}

// LonLat of a pixel's centre.
func (p Projection) LonLat(px, py int) (float64, float64) {
	lon := (float64(px)+0.5)/float64(p.Width)*360 - 180
	lat := millerLat(p.yTop - (float64(py)+0.5)/p.scale)
	return lon, lat
}

type edge struct {
	x0, y0, x1, y1 float64
	ymin, ymax     float64
}

// fillPolygon calls set for every pixel whose centre lies inside the polygon
// (even-odd over all its rings), row by row.
func fillPolygon(p Projection, poly Polygon, set func(i int)) {
	var edges []edge
	for _, ring := range poly {
		for k := range ring {
			a := ring[k]
			b := ring[(k+1)%len(ring)]
			ax, ay := p.ToPixel(a[0], a[1])
			bx, by := p.ToPixel(b[0], b[1])
			if ay == by {
				continue
			}
			edges = append(edges, edge{ax, ay, bx, by, math.Min(ay, by), math.Max(ay, by)})
		}
	}
	if len(edges) == 0 {
		return
	}
	sort.Slice(edges, func(i, j int) bool { return edges[i].ymin < edges[j].ymin })
	ymin, ymax := edges[0].ymin, edges[0].ymax
	for _, e := range edges {
		ymax = math.Max(ymax, e.ymax)
	}
	y0 := int(math.Max(0, math.Floor(ymin-0.5)))
	y1 := int(math.Min(float64(p.Height-1), math.Ceil(ymax)))
	var active []int
	next := 0
	xs := make([]float64, 0, 64)
	for py := y0; py <= y1; py++ {
		cy := float64(py) + 0.5
		for next < len(edges) && edges[next].ymin <= cy {
			active = append(active, next)
			next++
		}
		w := 0
		for _, k := range active {
			if edges[k].ymax > cy {
				active[w] = k
				w++
			}
		}
		active = active[:w]
		xs = xs[:0]
		for _, k := range active {
			e := edges[k]
			if e.ymin <= cy && cy < e.ymax {
				t := (cy - e.y0) / (e.y1 - e.y0)
				xs = append(xs, e.x0+t*(e.x1-e.x0))
			}
		}
		sort.Float64s(xs)
		for k := 0; k+1 < len(xs); k += 2 {
			xa := int(math.Ceil(xs[k] - 0.5))
			xb := int(math.Ceil(xs[k+1]-0.5)) - 1
			if xa < 0 {
				xa = 0
			}
			if xb >= p.Width {
				xb = p.Width - 1
			}
			for px := xa; px <= xb; px++ {
				set(py*p.Width + px)
			}
		}
	}
}

// forNeighbours4 calls fn for the orthogonal neighbours of i: up, left,
// right, down.
func forNeighbours4(i, w, n int, fn func(j int)) {
	x := i % w
	if i >= w {
		fn(i - w)
	}
	if x > 0 {
		fn(i - 1)
	}
	if x < w-1 {
		fn(i + 1)
	}
	if i+w < n {
		fn(i + w)
	}
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/owners.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
)

// CountryRow is one line of src/countries.json: a tag and its capital.
type CountryRow struct {
	Tag     string     `json:"tag"`
	Capital [2]float64 `json:"capital"` // lon, lat
}

// Patch gives the land inside a polygon to a 1936 owner (spec §4.4), only on
// the listed modern countries / admin-1 units when those lists are set.
type Patch struct {
	Owner   string       `json:"owner"`
	Note    string       `json:"note"`
	Adm0    []string     `json:"adm0,omitempty"`
	Adm1    []string     `json:"adm1,omitempty"`
	Polygon [][2]float64 `json:"polygon"`
}

// OwnerTables is src/owners1936.json.
type OwnerTables struct {
	// Modern country (Natural Earth adm0_a3) → 1936 tag; null: no owner,
	// the land takes its neighbours'.
	Adm0 map[string]*string `json:"adm0"`
	// Modern countries drawn with fewer, larger regions (colonies).
	LowDetail []string `json:"lowDetail"`
	// "ADM0:Natural Earth admin-1 name" → 1936 tag.
	Adm1    map[string]string `json:"adm1"`
	Patches []Patch           `json:"patches"`
}

// City is one line of src/vp_cities.json.
type City struct {
	Name string  `json:"name"`
	Lon  float64 `json:"lon"`
	Lat  float64 `json:"lat"`
	Tier int     `json:"tier"`
}

type Tables struct {
	Countries []CountryRow
	Owners    OwnerTables
	Cities    []City
	// Optional src/region_names.json: "TAG:generated name" → [name, nameKo].
	RegionNames map[string][2]string
	tagIndex    map[string]int // 1-based
	lowDetail   map[string]bool
}

func readJSON(path string, v any) error {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, v); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
	}
	return nil
}

func loadTables(dir string) (*Tables, error) {
	t := &Tables{tagIndex: map[string]int{}, lowDetail: map[string]bool{}}
	if err := readJSON(filepath.Join(dir, "countries.json"), &t.Countries); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := readJSON(filepath.Join(dir, "owners1936.json"), &t.Owners); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := readJSON(filepath.Join(dir, "vp_cities.json"), &t.Cities); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "region_names.json")); err == nil {
		if err := readJSON(filepath.Join(dir, "region_names.json"), &t.RegionNames); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	if len(t.Countries) > 254 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("too many countries for a byte: %d", len(t.Countries))
	}
	for i, c := range t.Countries {
		if _, dup := t.tagIndex[c.Tag]; dup {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("countries.json: %s twice", c.Tag)
		}
		t.tagIndex[c.Tag] = i + 1
	}
	var bad []string
	check := func(where, tag string) {
		if _, ok := t.tagIndex[tag]; !ok {
			bad = append(bad, where+" → "+tag)
		}
	}
	for k, v := range t.Owners.Adm0 {
		if v != nil {
			check("adm0 "+k, *v)
		}
	}
	for k, v := range t.Owners.Adm1 {
		check("adm1 "+k, v)
	}
	for _, p := range t.Owners.Patches {
		check("patch "+p.Note, p.Owner)
		if len(p.Polygon) < 3 {
			bad = append(bad, "patch "+p.Note+" has fewer than 3 points")
		}
	}
	for _, a := range t.Owners.LowDetail {
		t.lowDetail[a] = true
	}
	if len(bad) > 0 {
		sort.Strings(bad)
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown tags in the tables:\n  %v", bad)
	}
	return t, nil
}

func adm1Key(f Feature) string { return f.Str("adm0_a3") + ":" + f.Str("name") }

// assignOwners rasterizes the admin-1 units and gives every land pixel its
// 1936 owner (1-based index into countries.json). adm holds the admin-1
// feature index + 1 per pixel (0 = none).
func assignOwners(p Projection, land []bool, admin []Feature, t *Tables) ([]uint8, []int32, error) {
	n := p.Width * p.Height
	adm := make([]int32, n)
	for i, f := range admin {
		id := int32(i + 1)
		for _, poly := range f.Polygons {
			fillPolygon(p, poly, func(px int) { adm[px] = id })
		}
	}
	featOwner := make([]uint8, len(admin)+1)
	usedAdm1 := map[string]bool{}
	missing := map[string]bool{}
	for i, f := range admin {
		key := adm1Key(f)
		if tag, ok := t.Owners.Adm1[key]; ok {
			featOwner[i+1] = uint8(t.tagIndex[tag])
			usedAdm1[key] = true
			continue
		}
		a0 := f.Str("adm0_a3")
		o, ok := t.Owners.Adm0[a0]
		if !ok {
			missing[a0] = true
			continue
		}
		if o != nil {
			featOwner[i+1] = uint8(t.tagIndex[*o])
		}
	}
	var errs []string
	for a0 := range missing {
		errs = append(errs, "adm0 "+a0+" is not in owners1936.json")
	}
	for key := range t.Owners.Adm1 {
		if !usedAdm1[key] {
			errs = append(errs, "adm1 "+key+" matches no Natural Earth unit")
		}
	}
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		sort.Strings(errs)
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("owner tables:\n  %v", errs)
	}
	owner := make([]uint8, n)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if land[i] && adm[i] != 0 {
			owner[i] = featOwner[adm[i]]
		}
	}
	for _, pt := range t.Owners.Patches {
		allow0 := map[string]bool{}
		for _, a := range pt.Adm0 {
			allow0[a] = true
		}
		allow1 := map[string]bool{}
		for _, a := range pt.Adm1 {
			allow1[a] = true
		}
		tag := uint8(t.tagIndex[pt.Owner])
		ring := make(Ring, len(pt.Polygon))
		copy(ring, pt.Polygon)
		fillPolygon(p, Polygon{ring}, func(px int) {
			if !land[px] || adm[px] == 0 {
				return
			}
			f := admin[adm[px]-1]
			if len(allow0) > 0 && !allow0[f.Str("adm0_a3")] {
				return
			}
			if len(allow1) > 0 && !allow1[adm1Key(f)] {
				return
			}
			owner[px] = tag
		})
	}
	// Land Natural Earth's admin-1 layer misses (coast mismatches) or that
	// has no owner: its nearest owned land's owner and unit.
	queue := make([]int32, 0, 1<<16)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if land[i] && owner[i] != 0 {
			queue = append(queue, int32(i))
		}
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := int(queue[head])
		forNeighbours4(i, p.Width, n, func(j int) {
			if land[j] && owner[j] == 0 {
				owner[j] = owner[i]
				adm[j] = adm[i]
				queue = append(queue, int32(j))
			}
		})
	}
	// Islands no owned land reaches over land: the nearest owned land across
	// the water.
	origin := make([]int32, n)
	for i := range origin {
		origin[i] = -1
	}
	queue = queue[:0]
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if land[i] && owner[i] != 0 {
			origin[i] = int32(i)
			queue = append(queue, int32(i))
		}
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := int(queue[head])
		forNeighbours4(i, p.Width, n, func(j int) {
			if origin[j] != -1 {
				return
			}
			origin[j] = origin[i]
			if land[j] && owner[j] == 0 {
				owner[j] = owner[origin[i]]
				adm[j] = adm[origin[i]]
			}
			queue = append(queue, int32(j))
		})
	}
	return owner, adm, nil
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/regions.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"sort"
)

// Params are the region and sea-zone sizes (spec §4.5, §4.6).
type Params struct {
	MinCore     int // pieces of home countries smaller than this join a neighbour
	MinLow      int // the same for low-detail (colonial) land
	MaxRegion   int // regions larger than this are split
	MinSea      int // sea zones smaller than this join a neighbour
	MaxSea      int // sea zones larger than this are split
	IslandReach int // a small island joins same-owner land this many pixels away
}

// Region is one land region or sea zone as the tool builds it.
type Region struct {
	ID       int
	Owner    int // 1-based country index; 0 for sea zones
	Pixels   []int32
	Name     string
	NameKo   string
	VP       int
	VPTile   int // pixel, -1 when none
	vpWeight int
	suffixEn string // " (N)" … for a part of a split group
	suffixKo string
}

type group struct {
	owner   int
	pixels  []int32
	mainAdm int32 // admin-1 feature of the largest atom, + 1
	mainLen int
}

// buildRegions cuts the land into regions: pieces of one admin-1 unit with
// one 1936 owner, small pieces joined to their same-owner neighbour, large
// ones split. Returns a region id per pixel (0 on water) and the regions,
// numbered from 1 in the order of their first pixel.
func buildRegions(p Projection, land []bool, owner []uint8, adm []int32, admin []Feature, t *Tables, prm Params) ([]uint16, []*Region, error) {
	w := p.Width
	n := w * p.Height
	atom := make([]int32, n)
	var groups []*group
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if !land[i] || atom[i] != 0 || owner[i] == 0 {
			continue
		}
		id := int32(len(groups) + 1)
		atom[i] = id
		pix := []int32{int32(i)}
		for head := 0; head < len(pix); head++ {
			k := int(pix[head])
			forNeighbours4(k, w, n, func(j int) {
				if atom[j] == 0 && land[j] && owner[j] == owner[k] && adm[j] == adm[k] {
					atom[j] = id
					pix = append(pix, int32(j))
				}
			})
		}
		groups = append(groups, &group{owner: int(owner[i]), pixels: pix, mainAdm: adm[i], mainLen: len(pix)})
	}
	parent := make([]int32, len(groups)+1)
	for i := range parent {
		parent[i] = int32(i)
	}
	var find func(int32) int32
	find = func(a int32) int32 {
		for parent[a] != a {
			parent[a] = parent[parent[a]]
			a = parent[a]
		}
		return a
	}
	threshold := func(g *group) int {
		if g.mainAdm > 0 && t.lowDetail[admin[g.mainAdm-1].Str("adm0_a3")] {
			return prm.MinLow
		}
		return prm.MinCore
	}
	for {
		roots := make([]int32, 0, len(groups))
		for i := range groups {
			if find(int32(i+1)) == int32(i+1) {
				roots = append(roots, int32(i+1))
			}
		}
		sort.Slice(roots, func(a, b int) bool {
			la, lb := len(groups[roots[a]-1].pixels), len(groups[roots[b]-1].pixels)
			if la != lb {
				return la < lb
			}
			return roots[a] < roots[b]
		})
		merged := false
		for _, r := range roots {
			if find(r) != r {
				continue
			}
			g := groups[r-1]
			if len(g.pixels) >= threshold(g) {
				continue
			}
			edges := map[int32]int{}
			for _, px := range g.pixels {
				forNeighbours4(int(px), w, n, func(j int) {
					if atom[j] == 0 {
						return
					}
					o := find(atom[j])
					if o != r && groups[o-1].owner == g.owner {
						edges[o]++
					}
				})
			}
			// The smallest same-owner neighbour (then the longest shared
			// edge, then the smaller id), so small pieces gather into
			// regions of about the threshold instead of feeding one giant.
			best, bestN, bestLen := int32(0), 0, 0
			for o, k := range edges {
				l := len(groups[o-1].pixels)
				if best == 0 || l < bestLen || (l == bestLen && (k > bestN || (k == bestN && o < best))) {
					best, bestN, bestLen = o, k, l
				}
			}
			if best == 0 {
				// An island: the nearest same-owner land within reach.
				best = nearestGroup(g.pixels, w, n, prm.IslandReach, func(j int) int32 {
					if atom[j] == 0 {
						return 0
					}
					o := find(atom[j])
					if o == r || groups[o-1].owner != g.owner {
						return 0
					}
					return o
				})
			}
			if best == 0 {
				continue
			}
			tg := groups[best-1]
			tg.pixels = append(tg.pixels, g.pixels...)
			if g.mainLen > tg.mainLen {
				tg.mainAdm, tg.mainLen = g.mainAdm, g.mainLen
			}
			g.pixels = nil
			parent[r] = best
			merged = true
		}
		if !merged {
			break
		}
	}

	type piece struct {
		owner  int
		pixels []int32
		parent []int32 // the pixels of the group it was split from
		split  bool
	}
	var pieces []piece
	mark := make([]int32, n)
	for i, g := range groups {
		if find(int32(i+1)) != int32(i+1) {
			continue
		}
		if len(g.pixels) <= prm.MaxRegion {
			pieces = append(pieces, piece{owner: g.owner, pixels: g.pixels})
			continue
		}
		k := (len(g.pixels) + prm.MaxRegion - 1) / prm.MaxRegion
		for _, part := range splitPixels(g.pixels, k, w, n, mark, int32(i+1)) {
			pieces = append(pieces, piece{owner: g.owner, pixels: part, parent: g.pixels, split: true})
		}
	}
	for _, pc := range pieces {
		sort.Slice(pc.pixels, func(a, b int) bool { return pc.pixels[a] < pc.pixels[b] })
	}
	sort.Slice(pieces, func(a, b int) bool { return pieces[a].pixels[0] < pieces[b].pixels[0] })
	if len(pieces) >= 40000 {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%d land regions do not fit below the sea-zone ids", len(pieces))
	}
	ids := make([]uint16, n)
	regions := make([]*Region, len(pieces))
	for k, pc := range pieces {
		id := k + 1
		for _, px := range pc.pixels {
			ids[px] = uint16(id)
		}
		counts := map[int32]int{}
		for _, px := range pc.pixels {
			counts[adm[px]]++
		}
		var main int32
		for a, c := range counts {
			if c > counts[main] || (c == counts[main] && a < main) {
				main = a
			}
		}
		r := &Region{ID: id, Owner: pc.owner, Pixels: pc.pixels, VP: 1, VPTile: -1}
		setAdminName(r, admin, main)
		if pc.split {
			en, ko := direction(pc.pixels, pc.parent, w)
			r.suffixEn, r.suffixKo = " ("+en+")", " "+ko
		}
		regions[k] = r
	}
	return ids, regions, nil
}

// setAdminName names a region after an admin-1 unit (feature index + 1).
func setAdminName(r *Region, admin []Feature, unit int32) {
	r.Name, r.NameKo = "", ""
	if unit > 0 {
		f := admin[unit-1]
		r.Name = f.Str("name")
		r.NameKo = f.Str("name_ko")
	}
	if r.Name == "" {
		r.Name = "Unnamed"
	}
	if r.NameKo == "" {
		r.NameKo = r.Name
	}
}

// finishNames names each region after the admin-1 unit of its victory-point
// tile (its capital or biggest city) when it has one, applies the hand
// renames (keyed "TAG:name"), adds the split suffixes and numbers repeats.
// Fails on a rename that matches no region.
func finishNames(regions []*Region, adm []int32, admin []Feature, t *Tables) error {
	used := map[string]bool{}
	for _, r := range regions {
		if r.VPTile >= 0 && adm[r.VPTile] > 0 {
			setAdminName(r, admin, adm[r.VPTile])
		}
		key := t.Countries[r.Owner-1].Tag + ":" + r.Name
		if names, ok := t.RegionNames[key]; ok {
			r.Name, r.NameKo = names[0], names[1]
			used[key] = true
		}
		r.Name += r.suffixEn
		r.NameKo += r.suffixKo
	}
	for key := range t.RegionNames {
		if !used[key] {
			return fmt.Errorf("region_names.json: %q matches no region", key)
		}
	}
	dedupeNames(regions)
	return nil
}

// splitPixels cuts a connected set of pixels into k parts around k seeds
// picked farthest-first (ties: the smaller pixel), by multi-source BFS.
func splitPixels(pixels []int32, k, w, n int, mark []int32, tag int32) [][]int32 {
	for _, px := range pixels {
		mark[px] = tag
	}
	in := func(j int) bool { return mark[j] == tag }
	bfs := func(seeds []int32) map[int32]int32 {
		dist := make(map[int32]int32, len(pixels))
		queue := append([]int32(nil), seeds...)
		for _, s := range seeds {
			dist[s] = 0
		}
		for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
			i := queue[head]
			forNeighbours4(int(i), w, n, func(j int) {
				if !in(j) {
					return
				}
				if _, seen := dist[int32(j)]; !seen {
					dist[int32(j)] = dist[i] + 1
					queue = append(queue, int32(j))
				}
			})
		}
		return dist
	}
	first := pixels[0]
	for _, px := range pixels {
		if px < first {
			first = px
		}
	}
	seeds := []int32{first}
	for len(seeds) < k {
		dist := bfs(seeds)
		best, bestD := int32(-1), int32(-1)
		for _, px := range pixels {
			d, ok := dist[px]
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			if d > bestD || (d == bestD && px < best) {
				best, bestD = px, d
			}
		}
		if bestD <= 0 {
			break
		}
		seeds = append(seeds, best)
	}
	owner := make(map[int32]int, len(pixels))
	queue := append([]int32(nil), seeds...)
	for s, px := range seeds {
		owner[px] = s
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := queue[head]
		forNeighbours4(int(i), w, n, func(j int) {
			if !in(j) {
				return
			}
			if _, seen := owner[int32(j)]; !seen {
				owner[int32(j)] = owner[i]
				queue = append(queue, int32(j))
			}
		})
	}
	parts := make([][]int32, len(seeds))
	for _, px := range pixels {
		s, ok := owner[px]
		if !ok {
			// A part of the set the seeds cannot reach (an island joined
			// across the water): the nearest seed as the crow flies.
			x, y := int(px)%w, int(px)/w
			bestD := -1
			for k, sd := range seeds {
				dx, dy := int(sd)%w-x, int(sd)/w-y
				if d := dx*dx + dy*dy; bestD < 0 || d < bestD {
					s, bestD = k, d
				}
			}
		}
		parts[s] = append(parts[s], px)
	}
	return parts
}

var directionNames = map[string][2]string{
	"N": {"N", "북부"}, "NE": {"NE", "북동부"}, "E": {"E", "동부"}, "SE": {"SE", "남동부"},
	"S": {"S", "남부"}, "SW": {"SW", "남서부"}, "W": {"W", "서부"}, "NW": {"NW", "북서부"}, "C": {"C", "중부"},
}

// direction names where a part lies in the group it was split from.
func direction(part, whole []int32, w int) (string, string) {
	cx, cy := centroid(part, w)
	gx, gy := centroid(whole, w)
	minX, minY, maxX, maxY := bbox(whole, w)
	dx := (cx - gx) / float64(maxX-minX+1)
	dy := (cy - gy) / float64(maxY-minY+1)
	key := ""
	if dy < -1.0/6 {
		key = "N"
	} else if dy > 1.0/6 {
		key = "S"
	}
	if dx < -1.0/6 {
		key += "W"
	} else if dx > 1.0/6 {
		key += "E"
	}
	if key == "" {
		key = "C"
	}
	d := directionNames[key]
	return d[0], d[1]
}

func centroid(pix []int32, w int) (float64, float64) {
	var sx, sy float64
	for _, px := range pix {
		sx += float64(int(px) % w)
		sy += float64(int(px) / w)
	}
	return sx / float64(len(pix)), sy / float64(len(pix))
}

func bbox(pix []int32, w int) (int, int, int, int) {
	minX, minY, maxX, maxY := 1<<30, 1<<30, -1, -1
	for _, px := range pix {
		x, y := int(px)%w, int(px)/w
		minX, maxX = min(minX, x), max(maxX, x)
		minY, maxY = min(minY, y), max(maxY, y)
	}
	return minX, minY, maxX, maxY
}

// dedupeNames numbers repeated names of one owner ("X", "X 2", …) in id order.
func dedupeNames(regions []*Region) {
	seen := map[string]int{}
	for _, r := range regions {
		key := fmt.Sprintf("%d|%s", r.Owner, r.Name)
		seen[key]++
		if c := seen[key]; c > 1 {
			r.Name = fmt.Sprintf("%s %d", r.Name, c)
			r.NameKo = fmt.Sprintf("%s %d", r.NameKo, c)
		}
	}
}

// nearestGroup searches outwards from pixels (4-steps over land and water,
// at most reach steps) for the first pixel whose groupOf is not 0.
func nearestGroup(pixels []int32, w, n, reach int, groupOf func(j int) int32) int32 {
	dist := make(map[int32]int, len(pixels)*4)
	queue := make([]int32, 0, len(pixels))
	for _, px := range pixels {
		dist[px] = 0
		queue = append(queue, px)
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := queue[head]
		if dist[i] >= reach {
			continue
		}
		var found int32
		forNeighbours4(int(i), w, n, func(j int) {
			if found != 0 {
				return
			}
			if _, seen := dist[int32(j)]; seen {
				return
			}
			if g := groupOf(j); g != 0 {
				found = g
				return
			}
			dist[int32(j)] = dist[i] + 1
			queue = append(queue, int32(j))
		})
		if found != 0 {
			return found
		}
	}
	return 0
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/seas.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"sort"
)

const seaZoneBase = 40000

type seaPart struct {
	label   int32
	pixels  []int32
	mainLen int
}

// buildSeas cuts the water into sea zones (spec §4.6): Natural Earth marine
// areas (larger first, so smaller ones inside them win) and big named lakes;
// the rest of the water takes its nearest area. Zones over MaxSea are split,
// and parts under MinSea join the water zone they share the longest edge
// with (or the nearest zone when they touch none). Ids run from
// seaZoneBase+1 in the order of each zone's first pixel.
func buildSeas(p Projection, land []bool, marine, lakes []Feature, prm Params) ([]uint16, []*Region, error) {
	w := p.Width
	n := w * p.Height
	type named struct{ name, nameKo string }
	var names []named
	label := make([]int32, n)
	type area struct{ idx, pixels int }
	var order []area
	for i, f := range marine {
		count := 0
		for _, poly := range f.Polygons {
			fillPolygon(p, poly, func(px int) {
				if !land[px] {
					count++
				}
			})
		}
		if count > 0 {
			order = append(order, area{i, count})
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(order, func(a, b int) bool {
		if order[a].pixels != order[b].pixels {
			return order[a].pixels > order[b].pixels
		}
		return order[a].idx < order[b].idx
	})
	paint := func(f Feature) {
		names = append(names, named{f.Str("name"), f.Str("name_ko")})
		id := int32(len(names))
		for _, poly := range f.Polygons {
			fillPolygon(p, poly, func(px int) {
				if !land[px] {
					label[px] = id
				}
			})
		}
	}
	for _, a := range order {
		paint(marine[a.idx])
	}
	for _, f := range lakes {
		if f.Num("scalerank") <= 2 && f.Str("name") != "" {
			paint(f)
		}
	}
	// Water the areas miss: first along the water, then (lakes no area
	// reaches) the nearest labelled water across land.
	queue := make([]int32, 0, 1<<16)
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if !land[i] && label[i] != 0 {
			queue = append(queue, int32(i))
		}
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := int(queue[head])
		forNeighbours4(i, w, n, func(j int) {
			if !land[j] && label[j] == 0 {
				label[j] = label[i]
				queue = append(queue, int32(j))
			}
		})
	}
	reach := make([]int32, n)
	queue = queue[:0]
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if !land[i] && label[i] != 0 {
			reach[i] = label[i]
			queue = append(queue, int32(i))
		}
	}
	for head := 0; head < len(queue); head++ {
		i := int(queue[head])
		forNeighbours4(i, w, n, func(j int) {
			if reach[j] != 0 {
				return
			}
			reach[j] = reach[i]
			if !land[j] && label[j] == 0 {
				label[j] = reach[i]
			}
			queue = append(queue, int32(j))
		})
	}

	// Connected parts of each label, the big ones split.
	seen := make([]bool, n)
	mark := make([]int32, n)
	var parts []*seaPart
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if land[i] || label[i] == 0 || seen[i] {
			continue
		}
		seen[i] = true
		pix := []int32{int32(i)}
		for head := 0; head < len(pix); head++ {
			k := int(pix[head])
			forNeighbours4(k, w, n, func(j int) {
				if !seen[j] && !land[j] && label[j] == label[k] {
					seen[j] = true
					pix = append(pix, int32(j))
				}
			})
		}
		if len(pix) <= prm.MaxSea {
			parts = append(parts, &seaPart{label: label[i], pixels: pix, mainLen: len(pix)})
			continue
		}
		k := (len(pix) + prm.MaxSea - 1) / prm.MaxSea
		for _, sp := range splitPixels(pix, k, w, n, mark, int32(len(parts)+1)) {
			parts = append(parts, &seaPart{label: label[i], pixels: sp, mainLen: len(sp)})
		}
	}

	// Small parts join a neighbour, smallest first, until none is left.
	partOf := make([]int32, n)
	for k, pt := range parts {
		for _, px := range pt.pixels {
			partOf[px] = int32(k + 1)
		}
	}
	alive := make([]bool, len(parts))
	for k := range alive {
		alive[k] = true
	}
	for {
		idx := make([]int, 0, len(parts))
		for k := range parts {
			if alive[k] && len(parts[k].pixels) < prm.MinSea {
				idx = append(idx, k)
			}
		}
		sort.Slice(idx, func(a, b int) bool {
			la, lb := len(parts[idx[a]].pixels), len(parts[idx[b]].pixels)
			if la != lb {
				return la < lb
			}
			return idx[a] < idx[b]
		})
		merged := false
		for _, k := range idx {
			if !alive[k] || len(parts[k].pixels) >= prm.MinSea {
				continue
			}
			own := int32(k + 1)
			edges := map[int32]int{}
			for _, px := range parts[k].pixels {
				forNeighbours4(int(px), w, n, func(j int) {
					if !land[j] && partOf[j] != own && partOf[j] != 0 {
						edges[partOf[j]]++
					}
				})
			}
			best, bestN := int32(0), 0
			for o, c := range edges {
				if c > bestN || (c == bestN && o < best) {
					best, bestN = o, c
				}
			}
			if best == 0 {
				best = nearestGroup(parts[k].pixels, w, n, w, func(j int) int32 {
					if land[j] || partOf[j] == own || partOf[j] == 0 {
						return 0
					}
					return partOf[j]
				})
			}
			if best == 0 {
				continue
			}
			tg := parts[best-1]
			for _, px := range parts[k].pixels {
				partOf[px] = best
			}
			tg.pixels = append(tg.pixels, parts[k].pixels...)
			if parts[k].mainLen > tg.mainLen {
				tg.label, tg.mainLen = parts[k].label, parts[k].mainLen
			}
			parts[k].pixels = nil
			alive[k] = false
			merged = true
		}
		if !merged {
			break
		}
	}
	var final []*seaPart
	for k, pt := range parts {
		if alive[k] {
			sort.Slice(pt.pixels, func(a, b int) bool { return pt.pixels[a] < pt.pixels[b] })
			final = append(final, pt)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(final, func(a, b int) bool { return final[a].pixels[0] < final[b].pixels[0] })
	if seaZoneBase+len(final) > 0xffff {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%d sea zones do not fit in 16 bits", len(final))
	}
	ids := make([]uint16, n)
	zones := make([]*Region, len(final))
	for k, pt := range final {
		id := seaZoneBase + 1 + k
		for _, px := range pt.pixels {
			ids[px] = uint16(id)
		}
		nm := names[pt.label-1]
		z := &Region{ID: id, Pixels: pt.pixels, Name: nm.name, NameKo: nm.nameKo, VPTile: -1}
		if z.Name == "" {
			z.Name = "Sea"
		}
		if z.NameKo == "" {
			z.NameKo = z.Name
		}
		zones[k] = z
	}
	dedupeNames(zones)
	return ids, zones, nil
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/cities.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"sort"
	"strings"
)

// 1936 city weights (spec §7.5), keyed by Natural Earth nameascii|adm0_a3.
// Listed cities are taken whatever their Natural Earth scale rank; other
// capitals and rank 0..2 cities get 5 (rank 0..1) or 3.
var cityTiers = map[string]int{
	"London|GBR": 20, "Paris|FRA": 20, "Berlin|DEU": 20, "Moscow|RUS": 20, "Tokyo|JPN": 20, "New York|USA": 20,

	"Rome|ITA": 10, "Vienna|AUT": 10, "St. Petersburg|RUS": 10, "Chicago|USA": 10, "Osaka|JPN": 10,
	"Shanghai|CHN": 10, "Buenos Aires|ARG": 10, "Kolkata|IND": 10, "Mumbai|IND": 10, "Warsaw|POL": 10,
	"Budapest|HUN": 10, "Madrid|ESP": 10, "Barcelona|ESP": 10, "Hamburg|DEU": 10, "Detroit|USA": 10,
	"Philadelphia|USA": 10, "Beijing|CHN": 10, "Tianjin|CHN": 10, "Istanbul|TUR": 10, "Cairo|EGY": 10,
	"Sydney|AUS": 10, "Rio de Janeiro|BRA": 10, "Sao Paulo|BRA": 10, "Mexico City|MEX": 10, "Los Angeles|USA": 10,

	"Milan|ITA": 5, "Munich|DEU": 5, "Leipzig|DEU": 5, "Prague|CZE": 5, "Bucharest|ROU": 5, "Kiev|UKR": 5,
	"Kharkiv|UKR": 5, "Baku|AZE": 5, "Tashkent|UZB": 5, "Montreal|CAN": 5, "Toronto|CAN": 5, "Melbourne|AUS": 5,
	"Johannesburg|ZAF": 5, "Alexandria|EGY": 5, "Tehran|IRN": 5, "Delhi|IND": 5, "Chennai|IND": 5,
	"Lahore|PAK": 5, "Nanjing|CHN": 5, "Wuhan|CHN": 5, "Guangzhou|CHN": 5, "Seoul|KOR": 5, "Lisbon|PRT": 5,
	"Brussels|BEL": 5, "Amsterdam|NLD": 5, "Kobenhavn|DNK": 5, "Stockholm|SWE": 5, "Athens|GRC": 5,
	"Belgrade|SRB": 5, "Havana|CUB": 5, "Lima|PER": 5, "Montevideo|URY": 5, "San Francisco|USA": 5,
	"Boston|USA": 5, "Glasgow|GBR": 5, "Birmingham|GBR": 5, "Manchester|GBR": 5, "Marseille|FRA": 5,
	"Lyon|FRA": 5, "Naples|ITA": 5, "Turin|ITA": 5, "Manila|PHL": 5, "Singapore|SGP": 5, "Jakarta|IDN": 5,
	"Hong Kong|HKG": 5, "Cape Town|ZAF": 5, "Santiago|CHL": 5, "Bogota|COL": 5,
}

// Places that were no city of note in 1936 (later capitals, new cities).
var cityDrop = map[string]bool{
	"Brasilia|BRA": true, "Islamabad|PAK": true, "Abuja|NGA": true, "Dodoma|TZA": true, "Gaborone|BWA": true,
	"Nouakchott|MRT": true, "Belmopan|BLZ": true, "Putrajaya|MYS": true, "Yamoussoukro|CIV": true,
	"Lilongwe|MWI": true, "Dubai|ARE": true, "Abu Dhabi|ARE": true, "Doha|QAT": true, "Shenzhen|CHN": true,
	"Dongguan|CHN": true, "Chandigarh|IND": true,
}

func cityKey(f Feature) string {
	return strings.Join(strings.Fields(f.Str("nameascii")), " ") + "|" + f.Str("adm0_a3")
}

// bootstrapCities writes vp_cities.json from Natural Earth populated places
// and the lists above; it fails when a listed city is not found.
func bootstrapCities(places []Feature, path string) error {
	var rows []City
	found := map[string]bool{}
	for _, f := range places {
		if f.Point == nil {
			continue
		}
		key := cityKey(f)
		if cityDrop[key] {
			found[key] = true
			continue
		}
		rank := int(f.Num("scalerank"))
		tier, listed := cityTiers[key]
		if !listed {
			if rank > 2 && f.Num("adm0cap") != 1 {
				continue
			}
			tier = 3
			if rank <= 1 {
				tier = 5
			}
		}
		if listed && found[key] {
			continue // a second place of the same name and country
		}
		found[key] = true
		rows = append(rows, City{Name: f.Str("name"), Lon: f.Point[0], Lat: f.Point[1], Tier: tier})
	}
	var missing []string
	for key := range cityTiers {
		if !found[key] {
			missing = append(missing, key)
		}
	}
	for key := range cityDrop {
		if !found[key] {
			missing = append(missing, key)
		}
	}
	if len(missing) > 0 {
		sort.Strings(missing)
		return fmt.Errorf("cities not in Natural Earth (fix the key): %v", missing)
	}
	sort.Slice(rows, func(a, b int) bool {
		if rows[a].Tier != rows[b].Tier {
			return rows[a].Tier > rows[b].Tier
		}
		if rows[a].Name != rows[b].Name {
			return rows[a].Name < rows[b].Name
		}
		return rows[a].Lon < rows[b].Lon
	})
	js, err := json.MarshalIndent(rows, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	fmt.Printf("%d cities written to %s\n", len(rows), path)
	return os.WriteFile(path, append(js, '\n'), 0644)
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/terrain.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"image"
	"image/color"
	"image/png"
	"os"
)

// buildLand marks land pixels: Natural Earth land polygons minus lakes.
func buildLand(p Projection, landFeats, lakeFeats []Feature) []bool {
	land := make([]bool, p.Width*p.Height)
	for _, f := range landFeats {
		for _, poly := range f.Polygons {
			fillPolygon(p, poly, func(i int) { land[i] = true })
		}
	}
	for _, f := range lakeFeats {
		for _, poly := range f.Polygons {
			fillPolygon(p, poly, func(i int) { land[i] = false })
		}
	}
	return land
}

func loadGray(path string) (*image.Gray, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	img, err := png.Decode(f)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", path, err)
	}
	g, ok := img.(*image.Gray)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s is not 8-bit grayscale", path)
	}
	return g, nil
}

// elevationAt averages the 3x3 block of the equirectangular elevation
// raster (0..255, about 25 m per step) around lon, lat.
func elevationAt(g *image.Gray, lon, lat float64) int {
	w := g.Rect.Dx()
	h := g.Rect.Dy()
	cx := int((lon + 180) / 360 * float64(w))
	cy := int((90 - lat) / 180 * float64(h))
	sum, k := 0, 0
	for dy := -1; dy <= 1; dy++ {
		y := cy + dy
		if y < 0 || y >= h {
			continue
		}
		for dx := -1; dx <= 1; dx++ {
			x := ((cx+dx)%w + w) % w
			sum += int(g.Pix[y*g.Stride+x])
			k++
		}
	}
	return sum / k
}

// terrainBlue maps elevation to the generator's blue channel (map_generator.go
// GenerateMap): plains under ~500 m, highland to ~1500 m, mountains above.
func terrainBlue(e int) uint8 {
	var mag int
	switch {
	case e < 20:
		mag = e / 2
	case e < 60:
		mag = 10 + (e-20)/4
	default:
		mag = 20 + min(10, (e-60)/15)
	}
	return uint8(140 + 2*mag)
}

// writeTerrain writes the generator's image.png: water (0, 0, 106), land a
// grey whose blue channel is its terrain.
func writeTerrain(path string, p Projection, land []bool, elev *image.Gray) error {
	img := image.NewNRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, p.Width, p.Height))
	for y := 0; y < p.Height; y++ {
		for x := 0; x < p.Width; x++ {
			if !land[y*p.Width+x] {
				img.SetNRGBA(x, y, color.NRGBA{0, 0, 106, 255})
				continue
			}
			lon, lat := p.LonLat(x, y)
			b := terrainBlue(elevationAt(elev, lon, lat))
			img.SetNRGBA(x, y, color.NRGBA{b, b, b, 255})
		}
	}
	f, err := os.Create(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	return png.Encode(f, img)
}
```
