`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/output.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"image"
	"image/color"
	"image/png"
	"os"
	"sort"
)

// capitalVP is what a capital adds to its region (WW_CAPITAL_VP).
const capitalVP = 15

// nearestLand finds a land pixel within r pixels of px (cities on the coast
// can fall in the sea), scanning rings outwards, row by row.
func nearestLand(p Projection, land []bool, px, r int) (int, bool) {
	if land[px] {
		return px, true
	}
	x0, y0 := px%p.Width, px/p.Width
	for d := 1; d <= r; d++ {
		for y := y0 - d; y <= y0+d; y++ {
			for x := x0 - d; x <= x0+d; x++ {
				if x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= p.Width || y >= p.Height {
					continue
				}
				if max(abs(x-x0), abs(y-y0)) != d {
					continue
				}
				if land[y*p.Width+x] {
					return y*p.Width + x, true
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return 0, false
}

func abs(v int) int {
	if v < 0 {
		return -v
	}
	return v
}

// assignVP gives every region 1 victory point, adds each city's tier to the
// region it is in and +capitalVP to each capital's (spec §7.5). A region's VP
// tile is its capital, else its highest-tier city (first listed on ties).
// Returns each country's capital pixel.
func assignVP(p Projection, land []bool, ids []uint16, regions []*Region, t *Tables) ([]int, error) {
	byID := map[int]*Region{}
	for _, r := range regions {
		byID[r.ID] = r
	}
	for _, c := range t.Cities {
		px, ok := p.Pixel(c.Lon, c.Lat)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		px, ok = nearestLand(p, land, px, 3)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		r := byID[int(ids[px])]
		if r == nil {
			continue
		}
		r.VP += c.Tier
		if c.Tier > r.vpWeight {
			r.vpWeight = c.Tier
			r.VPTile = px
		}
	}
	capitals := make([]int, len(t.Countries))
	var errs []string
	for i, c := range t.Countries {
		px, ok := p.Pixel(c.Capital[0], c.Capital[1])
		if ok {
			px, ok = nearestLand(p, land, px, 3)
		}
		if !ok {
			errs = append(errs, c.Tag+": capital is not on land")
			continue
		}
		r := byID[int(ids[px])]
		if r == nil || r.Owner != i+1 {
			errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("%s: capital (%.2f, %.2f) is not on its own land", c.Tag, c.Capital[0], c.Capital[1]))
			continue
		}
		r.VP += capitalVP
		r.vpWeight = 1 << 20
		r.VPTile = px
		capitals[i] = px
	}
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("capitals:\n  %v", errs)
	}
	return capitals, nil
}

// writeRegionsPNG stores ids as R = id >> 8, G = id & 255.
func writeRegionsPNG(path string, p Projection, land, sea []uint16) error {
	img := image.NewNRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, p.Width, p.Height))
	for i := 0; i < p.Width*p.Height; i++ {
		id := land[i]
		if id == 0 {
			id = sea[i]
		}
		img.SetNRGBA(i%p.Width, i/p.Width, color.NRGBA{uint8(id >> 8), uint8(id), 0, 255})
	}
	f, err := os.Create(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	return png.Encode(f, img)
}

type srcCountryOut struct {
	Tag     string `json:"tag"`
	Capital [2]int `json:"capital"`
}

type srcRegionOut struct {
	ID     int     `json:"id"`
	Name   string  `json:"name"`
	NameKo string  `json:"nameKo"`
	Owner  string  `json:"owner"`
	VP     int     `json:"vp"`
	VPTile *[2]int `json:"vpTile,omitempty"`
}

type srcSeaOut struct {
	ID     int    `json:"id"`
	Name   string `json:"name"`
	NameKo string `json:"nameKo"`
}

// writeScenarioSrc writes scenario.src.json (read by the map generator,
// map-generator/regions.go).
func writeScenarioSrc(path string, p Projection, t *Tables, capitals []int, regions, seas []*Region) error {
	out := struct {
		Version   int             `json:"version"`
		Countries []srcCountryOut `json:"countries"`
		Regions   []srcRegionOut  `json:"regions"`
		SeaZones  []srcSeaOut     `json:"seaZones"`
	}{Version: 1}
	for i, c := range t.Countries {
		out.Countries = append(out.Countries, srcCountryOut{c.Tag, [2]int{capitals[i] % p.Width, capitals[i] / p.Width}})
	}
	for _, r := range regions {
		row := srcRegionOut{ID: r.ID, Name: r.Name, NameKo: r.NameKo, Owner: t.Countries[r.Owner-1].Tag, VP: r.VP}
		if r.VPTile >= 0 {
			row.VPTile = &[2]int{r.VPTile % p.Width, r.VPTile / p.Width}
		}
		out.Regions = append(out.Regions, row)
	}
	for _, z := range seas {
		out.SeaZones = append(out.SeaZones, srcSeaOut{z.ID, z.Name, z.NameKo})
	}
	js, err := json.MarshalIndent(out, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.WriteFile(path, append(js, '\n'), 0644)
}

// summary prints region counts per country, smallest first.
func summary(t *Tables, regions []*Region) {
	per := map[string]int{}
	for _, r := range regions {
		per[t.Countries[r.Owner-1].Tag]++
	}
	tags := make([]string, 0, len(per))
	for tag := range per {
		tags = append(tags, tag)
	}
	sort.Slice(tags, func(a, b int) bool {
		if per[tags[a]] != per[tags[b]] {
			return per[tags[a]] < per[tags[b]]
		}
		return tags[a] < tags[b]
	})
	for _, tag := range tags {
		fmt.Printf("  %s %d\n", tag, per[tag])
	}
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/crosscheck.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"sort"
)

// crossCheck compares the 1936 owners with a historical-basemaps file
// (aourednik, GPL-3.0; spec §4.2: compared only, nothing derived is
// written). For each country it prints the share of its land that falls in
// the file's best-matching NAME. Prints only; returns read errors.
func crossCheck(p Projection, land []bool, owner []uint8, t *Tables, path string) error {
	feats, err := readFeatures(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	n := p.Width * p.Height
	label := make([]int32, n)
	for i, f := range feats {
		id := int32(i + 1)
		for _, poly := range f.Polygons {
			fillPolygon(p, poly, func(px int) { label[px] = id })
		}
	}
	ours := make([]int, len(t.Countries)+1)
	overlap := make([]map[string]int, len(t.Countries)+1)
	for i := range overlap {
		overlap[i] = map[string]int{}
	}
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		if !land[i] || owner[i] == 0 {
			continue
		}
		o := owner[i]
		ours[o]++
		if label[i] != 0 {
			overlap[o][matchName(feats[label[i]-1])]++
		}
	}
	type row struct {
		tag    string
		name   string
		share  float64
		others string
	}
	var rows []row
	for i, c := range t.Countries {
		o := i + 1
		best, bestN := "", 0
		for name, k := range overlap[o] {
			if k > bestN || (k == bestN && name < best) {
				best, bestN = name, k
			}
		}
		share := 0.0
		if ours[o] > 0 {
			share = float64(bestN) / float64(ours[o])
		}
		others := ""
		names := make([]string, 0, len(overlap[o]))
		for name := range overlap[o] {
			if name != best {
				names = append(names, name)
			}
		}
		sort.Slice(names, func(a, b int) bool {
			return overlap[o][names[a]] > overlap[o][names[b]] || (overlap[o][names[a]] == overlap[o][names[b]] && names[a] < names[b])
		})
		for k, name := range names {
			if k == 2 {
				break
			}
			others += fmt.Sprintf("; %s %.1f%%", name, 100*float64(overlap[o][name])/float64(ours[o]))
		}
		rows = append(rows, row{c.Tag, best, share, others})
	}
	sort.Slice(rows, func(a, b int) bool { return rows[a].share < rows[b].share })
	fmt.Println("share of each country's 1936 land inside its best match in", path)
	for _, r := range rows {
		fmt.Printf("  %s %5.1f%%  %s%s\n", r.tag, 100*r.share, r.name, r.others)
	}
	return nil
}

// matchName groups a basemap feature under its colonial power when it has
// one (SUBJECTO), else under its own NAME.
func matchName(f Feature) string {
	if s := f.Str("SUBJECTO"); s != "" {
		return s
	}
	return f.Str("NAME")
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/debugpng.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"image"
	"image/color"
	"image/png"
	"os"
)

// writeOwnerPNG draws each pixel's owner (a colour per country, water dark)
// and the region edges, for checking the tables by eye.
func writeOwnerPNG(path string, p Projection, land []bool, owner []uint8, ids []uint16) error {
	img := image.NewNRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, p.Width, p.Height))
	n := p.Width * p.Height
	for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
		x, y := i%p.Width, i/p.Width
		if !land[i] {
			img.SetNRGBA(x, y, color.NRGBA{20, 30, 60, 255})
			continue
		}
		o := uint32(owner[i])
		h := o*2654435761 + 12345
		c := color.NRGBA{uint8(64 + h%160), uint8(64 + (h>>8)%160), uint8(64 + (h>>16)%160), 255}
		if ids != nil && x+1 < p.Width && y+1 < p.Height && (ids[i] != ids[i+1] || ids[i] != ids[i+p.Width]) {
			c = color.NRGBA{c.R / 2, c.G / 2, c.B / 2, 255}
		}
		if x+1 < p.Width && y+1 < p.Height && (owner[i] != owner[i+1] || owner[i] != owner[i+p.Width]) {
			c = color.NRGBA{0, 0, 0, 255}
		}
		img.SetNRGBA(x, y, c)
	}
	f, err := os.Create(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	return png.Encode(f, img)
}
```

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/main.go`:

```go
// Command ww1936build draws the World War 1936 map sources (spec §4.3):
//
//	assets/maps/world1936/image.png        terrain, read by the map generator
//	scenarios/world1936/regions.png        region and sea-zone ids (R<<8 | G)
//	scenarios/world1936/scenario.src.json  regions, sea zones, countries
//
// from Natural Earth and NASA files fetched by scenarios/world1936/fetch.sh
// and the tables in scenarios/world1936/src. Run from map-generator/:
//
//	go run ./cmd/ww1936build
//
// then `go run -tags nodynamic . --maps=world1936` to make the map files.
package main

import (
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"
)

func main() {
	cache := flag.String("cache", ".cache/ww1936", "folder with the fetched Natural Earth and NASA files")
	src := flag.String("src", "scenarios/world1936/src", "folder with countries.json, owners1936.json, vp_cities.json")
	outImage := flag.String("out-image", "assets/maps/world1936/image.png", "terrain image")
	outDir := flag.String("out-dir", "scenarios/world1936", "folder for regions.png and scenario.src.json")
	width := flag.Int("width", 4000, "map width in pixels")
	minCore := flag.Int("min-core", 200, "smallest region of a home country, in pixels")
	minLow := flag.Int("min-low", 3000, "smallest region of low-detail land, in pixels")
	maxRegion := flag.Int("max-region", 12000, "largest land region, in pixels")
	minSea := flag.Int("min-sea", 3000, "smallest sea zone, in pixels")
	maxSea := flag.Int("max-sea", 60000, "largest sea zone, in pixels")
	islandReach := flag.Int("island-reach", 40, "a small island joins same-owner land this many pixels away")
	crosscheck := flag.String("crosscheck", "", "a historical-basemaps GeoJSON to compare the owners with (prints a report, writes nothing)")
	bootstrap := flag.Bool("bootstrap-cities", false, "write src/vp_cities.json from Natural Earth populated places and exit")
	debugPNG := flag.String("debug-png", "", "also draw owners and region edges to this PNG")
	flag.Parse()

	start := time.Now()
	step := func(what string) { log.Printf("%-28s %6.1fs", what, time.Since(start).Seconds()) }
	ne := func(name string) []Feature {
		f, err := readFeatures(filepath.Join(*cache, name+".geojson"))
		if err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
		return f
	}

	if *bootstrap {
		if err := bootstrapCities(ne("ne_10m_populated_places_simple"), filepath.Join(*src, "vp_cities.json")); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
		return
	}

	t, err := loadTables(*src)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	p := NewProjection(*width, -60, 84)
	log.Printf("map %dx%d", p.Width, p.Height)
	prm := Params{MinCore: *minCore, MinLow: *minLow, MaxRegion: *maxRegion, MinSea: *minSea, MaxSea: *maxSea, IslandReach: *islandReach}

	lakes := ne("ne_10m_lakes")
	land := buildLand(p, ne("ne_10m_land"), lakes)
	step("land")
	elev, err := loadGray(filepath.Join(*cache, "gebco_08_rev_elev_21600x10800.png"))
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := writeTerrain(*outImage, p, land, elev); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	step("terrain")
	admin := ne("ne_10m_admin_1_states_provinces")
	owner, adm, err := assignOwners(p, land, admin, t)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	step("owners")
	if *crosscheck != "" {
		if err := crossCheck(p, land, owner, t, *crosscheck); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
		return
	}
	landIDs, regions, err := buildRegions(p, land, owner, adm, admin, t, prm)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	step("regions")
	if *debugPNG != "" {
		if err := writeOwnerPNG(*debugPNG, p, land, owner, landIDs); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	seaIDs, seas, err := buildSeas(p, land, ne("ne_10m_geography_marine_polys"), lakes, prm)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	step("sea zones")
	capitals, err := assignVP(p, land, landIDs, regions, t)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := finishNames(regions, adm, admin, t); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(*outDir, 0755); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := writeRegionsPNG(filepath.Join(*outDir, "regions.png"), p, landIDs, seaIDs); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := writeScenarioSrc(filepath.Join(*outDir, "scenario.src.json"), p, t, capitals, regions, seas); err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
	step("written")
	fmt.Printf("%d land regions, %d sea zones; regions per country:\n", len(regions), len(seas))
	summary(t, regions)
	if len(regions) < 1000 || len(regions) > 1800 {
		log.Fatalf("land regions %d are outside 1000..1800: change -min-core/-min-low/-max-region", len(regions))
	}
	if len(seas) < 120 || len(seas) > 300 {
		log.Fatalf("sea zones %d are outside 120..300: change -max-sea", len(seas))
	}
}
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Fetches the World War 1936 map sources into map-generator/.cache/ww1936
# (git-ignored) and checks them. Public domain: Natural Earth (pinned
# commit), NASA Earth Observatory / GEBCO topography. Run from map-generator/.
set -euo pipefail
dir=.cache/ww1936
mkdir -p "$dir"
ne=https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvkelso/natural-earth-vector/ca96624a56bd078437bca8184e78163e5039ad19/geojson
for f in ne_10m_land ne_10m_lakes ne_10m_admin_1_states_provinces ne_10m_geography_marine_polys ne_10m_populated_places_simple; do
  [ -f "$dir/$f.geojson" ] || curl -fsSL -o "$dir/$f.geojson" "$ne/$f.geojson"
done
nasa=https://eoimages.gsfc.nasa.gov/images/imagerecords/73000/73934/gebco_08_rev_elev_21600x10800.png
[ -f "$dir/gebco_08_rev_elev_21600x10800.png" ] || curl -fsSL -o "$dir/gebco_08_rev_elev_21600x10800.png" "$nasa"
(cd "$dir" && sha256sum -c - <<'SUMS'
1ac90796408bc6ad6911d69448485d3c4dbf2190370080368a09976e1c9f7416  ne_10m_land.geojson
2d036f53dedec578001c5c30c2959ee7d4eebc1306900fa4367c49929ec8f2d9  ne_10m_lakes.geojson
22d0e3ad85eb3e27f17cabf8ba2d50e554fbc27a87796ff891d958185da62fb5  ne_10m_admin_1_states_provinces.geojson
53f865e8ffa966cdd402145c82c5cd14ee7ce974cd0eb9a3f59f03a4cfd2d66c  ne_10m_geography_marine_polys.geojson
fd3fa867a320cbd5c5b6bb5bc550afeec2939fb2cef688e508007282a55ac42f  ne_10m_populated_places_simple.geojson
4a70590d40dd7b9c69b9ab359d1dc4475ce97c1d2d13625223b248364112c699  gebco_08_rev_elev_21600x10800.png
SUMS
)
```

`map-generator/scenarios/world1936/README.md`:

```markdown
# World War 1936 map sources

See docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-world-war-1936-design.md §4. From map-generator/:

1. `bash scenarios/world1936/fetch.sh` — Natural Earth and NASA files into `.cache/ww1936` (not committed).
2. `go run ./cmd/ww1936build -bootstrap-cities` — only when the city lists in `cmd/ww1936build/cities.go` change; writes `src/vp_cities.json`.
3. `go run ./cmd/ww1936build` — writes `../assets/maps/world1936/image.png`, `regions.png` and `scenario.src.json` here. `-debug-png .cache/ww1936/owners.png` also draws the owners for checking by eye.
4. `go run -tags nodynamic . --maps=world1936` — the map files in `resources/maps/world1936`.

`src/owners1936.json` gives every modern country (Natural Earth `adm0_a3`) its 1936 owner, overrides admin-1 units (`"ADM0:name"`), and patches the borders that cut through modern units with hand-drawn polygons (each patch limited to the listed modern countries or units). `src/region_names.json` renames a generated region (`"TAG:name": [name, nameKo]`).

Cross-check (optional): with aourednik/historical-basemaps' `world_1938.geojson` (GPL-3.0) in `.cache/ww1936`, `go run ./cmd/ww1936build -crosscheck .cache/ww1936/world_1938.geojson` prints how much of each country lies in its best match. It writes no file; nothing derived from that data is committed.
```

`map-generator/scenarios/world1936/src/region_names.json`:

```json
{
  "GBR:Wandsworth": ["London", "런던"]
}
```

(런던 시가 든 Natural Earth 1급 단위 이름이 "Wandsworth"라서 바꾼다. Step 5의 목록을 보고 이상한 이름이 더 있으면 여기에 더한다.)

- [ ] **Step 2: 자료 받기와 도시 목록**

```bash
cd /home/eser/dev/OpenFrontIO-ww/map-generator
bash scenarios/world1936/fetch.sh
go run ./cmd/ww1936build -bootstrap-cities
```

Expected: 체크섬 6개 `OK`, `314 cities written to scenarios/world1936/src/vp_cities.json`(Natural Earth의 수도와 축척 0~2 도시, 그리고 `cities.go`의 1936년 가중치 목록. 목록의 도시가 하나라도 없으면 도구가 멈춘다).

- [ ] **Step 3: 대조 보고서 (선택, GPL 자료는 넣지 않는다)**

```bash
curl -fsSL -o .cache/ww1936/world_1938.geojson https://raw.githubusercontent.com/aourednik/historical-basemaps/master/geojson/world_1938.geojson
go run ./cmd/ww1936build -crosscheck .cache/ww1936/world_1938.geojson
```

`tests/ww1936/World1936Data.test.ts`:

```ts
import { GameMapSize, GameMapType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { COUNTRY_TAGS, CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { SEA_ZONE_BASE } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { loadTerrainMap, TerrainMapData } from "@openfront/engine-lib/game/TerrainMapLoader";
import { loadMapFiles } from "@openfront/shared/GameMapLoader";
import path from "path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";
import { NodeGameMapLoader } from "../perf/fullgame/NodeGameMapLoader";

const maps = new NodeGameMapLoader(
  path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../resources/maps"),
);

// Miller cylindrical, -60..84 degrees, as cmd/ww1936build draws it.
const W = 4000;
const millerY = (lat: number) =>
  1.25 * Math.log(Math.tan(Math.PI / 4 + 0.4 * ((lat * Math.PI) / 180)));
const SCALE = W / (2 * Math.PI);
const TOP = millerY(84);

let data: TerrainMapData;
beforeAll(async () => {
  data = await loadTerrainMap(
    await loadMapFiles(maps, GameMapType.World1936, GameMapSize.Normal),
  );
}, 60_000);

/** The owner tag at a lon/lat (the nearest land within 3 tiles). */
function ownerAt(lon: number, lat: number): CountryTag | undefined {
  const s = data.scenario!;
  const map = data.gameMap;
  const x0 = Math.floor(((lon + 180) / 360) * W);
  const y0 = Math.floor((TOP - millerY(lat)) * SCALE);
  for (let d = 0; d <= 3; d++) {
    for (let y = y0 - d; y <= y0 + d; y++) {
      for (let x = x0 - d; x <= x0 + d; x++) {
        if (Math.max(Math.abs(x - x0), Math.abs(y - y0)) !== d) continue;
        const t = map.ref(x, y);
        if (map.isLand(t)) return s.region(s.regionAt(t))?.owner;
      }
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

describe("the world1936 map", () => {
  test("every 1936 country, about 1,200 regions and 150 sea zones", () => {
    const s = data.scenario!;
    expect([...s.countries]).toEqual([...COUNTRY_TAGS]);
    expect(s.landRegions.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1000);
    expect(s.landRegions.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1800);
    expect(s.data.seaZones.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(120);
    expect(s.data.seaZones.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(300);
    const owners = new Set(s.landRegions.map((r) => r.owner));
    expect(COUNTRY_TAGS.filter((t) => !owners.has(t))).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("every land tile has a land region and every water tile a sea zone", () => {
    const s = data.scenario!;
    const map = data.gameMap;
    let bad = 0;
    map.forEachTile((t) => {
      const id = s.regionAt(t);
      if (map.isLand(t) && !map.isImpassable(t)) {
        if (s.region(id) === undefined) bad++;
      } else if (!map.isLand(t) && (id <= SEA_ZONE_BASE || s.seaZone(id) === undefined)) {
        bad++;
      }
    });
    expect(bad).toBe(0);
  });

  test("capitals and VP tiles sit on their own land", () => {
    const s = data.scenario!;
    for (const tag of s.countries) {
      expect(s.region(s.regionAt(s.capitalTile(tag)))?.owner, tag).toBe(tag);
    }
    for (const r of s.landRegions) {
      expect(s.regionAt(s.vpTile(r)), r.name).toBe(r.id);
    }
  });

  test.each([
    ["Berlin", 13.4, 52.52, "DEU"],
    ["Danzig", 18.65, 54.35, "DZG"],
    ["Gdynia", 18.53, 54.52, "POL"],
    ["Gleiwitz", 18.67, 50.29, "DEU"],
    ["Kattowitz", 19.02, 50.26, "POL"],
    ["Wilno", 25.28, 54.69, "POL"],
    ["Lwów", 24.03, 49.84, "POL"],
    ["Königsberg", 20.5, 54.7, "DEU"],
    ["Memel", 21.13, 55.7, "LTU"],
    ["Viipuri", 28.75, 60.71, "FIN"],
    ["Chişinău", 28.86, 47.01, "ROU"],
    ["Trieste", 13.77, 45.65, "ITA"],
    ["Antakya", 36.16, 36.2, "FRA"],
    ["Madrid", -3.7, 40.42, "ESR"],
    ["Burgos", -3.7, 42.34, "ESN"],
    ["Tétouan", -5.37, 35.57, "ESN"],
    ["Cairo", 31.24, 30.04, "GBR"],
    ["Addis Ababa", 38.75, 9.03, "ETH"],
    ["Tripoli", 13.19, 32.89, "ITA"],
    ["Hanoi", 105.85, 21.03, "FRA"],
    ["Batavia", 106.85, -6.21, "NLD"],
    ["Manila", 120.98, 14.6, "USA"],
    ["Karachi", 67.0, 24.86, "RAJ"],
    ["Rangoon", 96.16, 16.87, "RAJ"],
    ["Keijō (Seoul)", 126.98, 37.57, "JPN"],
    ["Taihoku", 121.56, 25.04, "JPN"],
    ["Toyohara", 142.7, 46.95, "JPN"],
    ["Dairen", 121.6, 38.92, "JPN"],
    ["Hsinking", 125.32, 43.88, "MCK"],
    ["Chengde", 117.9, 40.97, "MCK"],
    ["Hohhot", 111.7, 40.8, "SXI"],
    ["Yan'an", 109.5, 36.6, "CCP"],
    ["Xi'an", 108.9, 34.3, "CHN"],
    ["Kyzyl", 94.45, 51.72, "TTU"],
    ["St. John's", -52.7, 47.56, "GBR"],
    ["Windhoek", 17.08, -22.56, "ZAF"],
  ] as const)("%s is %s's", (_name, lon, lat, tag) => {
    expect(ownerAt(lon, lat)).toBe(tag);
  });
});
```

### Task 5: 선전포고·참전·역사 달력

**Files:**
- Modify (스텁 본문 채우기): `packages/engine/src/ww1936/Diplomacy.ts`, `packages/engine/src/ww1936/HistoryRun.ts`
- Test: `tests/ww1936/Diplomacy.test.ts`, `tests/ww1936/History.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `WorldWar`(`inPrep`, `isAtWar`, `setWar`, `sameFaction`, `factionOf`, `factionMembers`, `isCapitulated`, `tagOf`, `playerOfTag`, `history`, `setHistory`, `markHistoryDone`), `HISTORY`, `HISTORY_SKIP_ODDS`, `HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS`, `dayOfDate`, `dayStartTick`, 테스트 유틸
- Produces: Task 1의 시그니처 그대로(본문만). 메시지 키 `events_display.ww1936_war_declared {attackerTag, targetTag, allies}`, `events_display.ww1936_war_joined {actorTag, enemyTag}`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트**

`tests/ww1936/Diplomacy.test.ts`:

```ts
import { GameUpdateType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameUpdates";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import {
  declareWar,
  declareWarBlock,
  joinWar,
} from "@openfront/engine/ww1936/Diplomacy";
import {
  byTag,
  HUMAN,
  stamped,
  step,
  stepTo,
  wwRunner,
  wwRunnerCapture,
} from "./util";

describe("declarations of war (spec §7.3)", () => {
  test("no declaration during the preparation minute", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    step(runner);
    expect(declareWarBlock(g, ww, byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL"), g.ticks())).toBe("prep");
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    expect(declareWarBlock(g, ww, byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL"), g.ticks())).toBeNull();
  });

  test("not on oneself, a faction member, an enemy or a capitulated country", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const [deu, ita, pol, sun] = ["DEU", "ITA", "POL", "SUN"].map((t) =>
      byTag(g, t as "DEU"),
    );
    const now = g.ticks();
    expect(declareWarBlock(g, ww, deu, deu, now)).toBe("self");
    expect(declareWarBlock(g, ww, deu, ita, now)).toBe("faction");
    declareWar(g, ww, deu, pol, now);
    expect(declareWarBlock(g, ww, deu, pol, now)).toBe("at_war");
    ww.addCapitulation(sun.smallID(), 0, now);
    expect(declareWarBlock(g, ww, deu, sun, now)).toBe("capitulated");
  });

  test("a declaration brings in the target's faction, never the attacker's", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const [deu, ita, fra, gbr] = ["DEU", "ITA", "FRA", "GBR"].map((t) =>
      byTag(g, t as "DEU"),
    );
    const joined = declareWar(g, ww, deu, fra, g.ticks());
    expect(joined).toEqual([fra.smallID(), gbr.smallID()]);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(deu.smallID(), gbr.smallID())).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(ita.smallID(), fra.smallID())).toBe(false);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(ita.smallID(), gbr.smallID())).toBe(false);
  });

  test("a join stays between the two", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const [deu, ita, fra, gbr] = ["DEU", "ITA", "FRA", "GBR"].map((t) =>
      byTag(g, t as "DEU"),
    );
    expect(joinWar(g, ww, gbr, deu, g.ticks())).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(gbr.smallID(), deu.smallID())).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(gbr.smallID(), ita.smallID())).toBe(false);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(fra.smallID(), deu.smallID())).toBe(false);
    expect(joinWar(g, ww, gbr, deu, g.ticks())).toBe(false);
  });

  test("a human's declare_war intent starts the war and everyone hears of it", async () => {
    const { runner, updates } = await wwRunnerCapture({}, [
      { clientID: HUMAN, country: "DEU" },
    ]);
    const g = runner.game;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const fra = byTag(g, "FRA");
    step(runner, 2, [stamped(HUMAN, { type: "declare_war", target: fra.id() })]);
    expect(g.worldWar()!.isAtWar(byTag(g, "DEU").smallID(), fra.smallID())).toBe(true);
    const messages = updates.flatMap((u) => u.updates[GameUpdateType.DisplayEvent]);
    const declared = messages.find((m) => m.message === "events_display.ww1936_war_declared");
    expect(declared?.params).toEqual({ attackerTag: "DEU", targetTag: "FRA", allies: 1 });
    expect(declared?.playerID).toBeNull();
  });

  test("the player actions tell whether war can be declared", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({}, [{ clientID: HUMAN, country: "DEU" }]);
    const g = runner.game;
    const deu = byTag(g, "DEU");
    const at = (tag: "POL" | "ITA") => {
      const tile = byTag(g, tag).spawnTile()!;
      return runner.playerActions(deu.id(), g.x(tile), g.y(tile)).interaction?.canDeclareWar;
    };
    step(runner);
    expect(at("POL")).toBe(false);
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    expect(at("POL")).toBe(true);
    expect(at("ITA")).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/ww1936/History.test.ts`:

```ts
import { PseudoRandom } from "@openfront/engine-lib/PseudoRandom";
import { dayOfDate, dayStartTick } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import {
  HISTORY,
  HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/History";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { rollHistory } from "@openfront/engine/ww1936/HistoryRun";
import { byTag, HUMAN, step, stepTo, wwRunner } from "./util";

const DEU_POL = HISTORY.findIndex((e) => e.actor === "DEU" && e.target === "POL");
const GBR_DEU = HISTORY.findIndex((e) => e.actor === "GBR" && e.target === "DEU");

/** Only the given entries, at the given days. */
function only(entries: Record<number, number>): [number, boolean][] {
  return HISTORY.map((_, i) => [entries[i] ?? -1, false]);
}

describe("the historical calendar (spec §7.4)", () => {
  test("the table is sorted by date and names two different countries", () => {
    for (let i = 1; i < HISTORY.length; i++) {
      expect(dayOfDate(HISTORY[i].date)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(
        dayOfDate(HISTORY[i - 1].date),
      );
    }
    for (const e of HISTORY) expect(e.actor).not.toBe(e.target);
  });

  test("each entry is skipped or happens 0..20 days late, the same way every time", async () => {
    const a = await wwRunner();
    const b = await wwRunner();
    step(a);
    step(b);
    const plan = a.game.worldWar()!.history();
    expect(plan).toHaveLength(HISTORY.length);
    expect(b.game.worldWar()!.history()).toEqual(plan);
    let skipped = 0;
    plan.forEach(([fire], i) => {
      if (fire === -1) {
        skipped++;
        return;
      }
      const day = dayOfDate(HISTORY[i].date);
      expect(fire).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(day);
      expect(fire).toBeLessThanOrEqual(day + HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS);
    });
    expect(skipped).toBeLessThan(HISTORY.length / 3);
  });

  test("rolling uses one chance and one delay per entry", () => {
    const fake = { plan: [] as [number, boolean][] };
    const ww = {
      setHistory: (p: [number, boolean][]) => (fake.plan = p),
    } as unknown as Parameters<typeof rollHistory>[0];
    rollHistory(ww, new PseudoRandom(42), true);
    const replay = new PseudoRandom(42);
    HISTORY.forEach((e, i) => {
      const skip = replay.chance(10);
      const delay = replay.nextInt(0, HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS + 1);
      expect(fake.plan[i][0]).toBe(skip ? -1 : dayOfDate(e.date) + delay);
    });
    rollHistory(ww, new PseudoRandom(42), false);
    expect(fake.plan).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("historical AI off: no calendar", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({ historicalAi: false });
    step(runner);
    expect(runner.game.worldWar()!.history()).toEqual([]);
  });

  test("a due entry fires for a nation: a declaration, then a join", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    step(runner);
    ww.setHistory(only({ [DEU_POL]: 2, [GBR_DEU]: 3 }));
    stepTo(runner, dayStartTick(2, WW_PREP_TICKS) + 1);
    const [deu, pol, gbr, ita] = ["DEU", "POL", "GBR", "ITA"].map((t) => byTag(g, t as "DEU"));
    expect(ww.isAtWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID())).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(gbr.smallID(), deu.smallID())).toBe(false);
    stepTo(runner, dayStartTick(3, WW_PREP_TICKS) + 1);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(gbr.smallID(), deu.smallID())).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(gbr.smallID(), ita.smallID())).toBe(false);
    expect(ww.history()[DEU_POL][1]).toBe(true);
  });

  test("a human country is never moved by the calendar", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({}, [{ clientID: HUMAN, country: "DEU" }]);
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    step(runner);
    ww.setHistory(only({ [DEU_POL]: 1 }));
    stepTo(runner, dayStartTick(2, WW_PREP_TICKS));
    expect(ww.isAtWar(byTag(g, "DEU").smallID(), byTag(g, "POL").smallID())).toBe(false);
    expect(ww.history()[DEU_POL][1]).toBe(true);
  });

  test("an entry with a country the scenario lacks passes quietly", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const ww = runner.game.worldWar()!;
    step(runner);
    ww.setHistory(only({ 0: 1 })); // ESN → ESR: neither is on the test map
    stepTo(runner, dayStartTick(2, WW_PREP_TICKS));
    expect(ww.history()[0][1]).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.data().wars).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `Diplomacy.ts` 본문**

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/Diplomacy.ts` 전체를 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts
import { MessageType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { DeclareWarBlock } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import type { Game, Player } from "../game/Game";
import type { WorldWar } from "./WorldWar";

/** Why `attacker` may not declare war on `target` now (spec §7.3); null if it may. */
export function declareWarBlock(
  _mg: Game,
  ww: WorldWar,
  attacker: Player,
  target: Player,
  tick: number,
): DeclareWarBlock | null {
  if (ww.inPrep(tick)) return "prep";
  if (attacker === target) return "self";
  if (!attacker.isAlive() || !target.isAlive()) return "dead";
  const a = attacker.smallID();
  const b = target.smallID();
  if (ww.isCapitulated(a) || ww.isCapitulated(b)) return "capitulated";
  if (ww.sameFaction(a, b)) return "faction";
  if (ww.isAtWar(a, b)) return "at_war";
  return null;
}

/**
 * Spec §7.3: the attacker goes to war with the target and with every living,
 * uncapitulated member of the target's faction; never with members of its
 * own faction. Returns the smallIDs newly at war with it, the target first,
 * and tells everyone.
 */
export function declareWar(
  mg: Game,
  ww: WorldWar,
  attacker: Player,
  target: Player,
  tick: number,
): number[] {
  if (declareWarBlock(mg, ww, attacker, target, tick) !== null) return [];
  const a = attacker.smallID();
  const joined: number[] = [];
  if (ww.setWar(a, target.smallID(), tick)) joined.push(target.smallID());
  const faction = ww.factionOf(target.smallID());
  if (faction !== null) {
    for (const m of ww.factionMembers(faction)) {
      if (m === target.smallID() || m === a || ww.isCapitulated(m)) continue;
      const p = mg.playerBySmallID(m);
      if (!p.isPlayer() || !p.isAlive()) continue;
      if (ww.setWar(a, m, tick)) joined.push(m);
    }
  }
  mg.displayMessage(
    "events_display.ww1936_war_declared",
    MessageType.WORLD_WAR,
    null,
    undefined,
    {
      attackerTag: ww.tagOf(a),
      targetTag: ww.tagOf(target.smallID()),
      allies: joined.length - 1,
    },
  );
  return joined;
}

/** Spec §7.3: a war between the two only (the calendar's "join"). */
export function joinWar(
  mg: Game,
  ww: WorldWar,
  actor: Player,
  enemy: Player,
  tick: number,
): boolean {
  if (declareWarBlock(mg, ww, actor, enemy, tick) !== null) return false;
  ww.setWar(actor.smallID(), enemy.smallID(), tick);
  mg.displayMessage(
    "events_display.ww1936_war_joined",
    MessageType.WORLD_WAR,
    null,
    undefined,
    { actorTag: ww.tagOf(actor.smallID()), enemyTag: ww.tagOf(enemy.smallID()) },
  );
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `HistoryRun.ts` 본문**

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/HistoryRun.ts` 전체를 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts
import { PlayerType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { PseudoRandom } from "@openfront/engine-lib/PseudoRandom";
import { dayOfDate } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import {
  HISTORY,
  HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS,
  HISTORY_SKIP_ODDS,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/History";
import type { Game } from "../game/Game";
import { declareWar, joinWar } from "./Diplomacy";
import type { WorldWar } from "./WorldWar";

/**
 * Spec §7.4, plan decision 10: once per game, in table order, each entry is
 * skipped with odds 1 in HISTORY_SKIP_ODDS and otherwise happens 0..20 days
 * late. Both values are rolled for every entry. Off: no calendar at all.
 */
export function rollHistory(
  ww: WorldWar,
  random: PseudoRandom,
  enabled: boolean,
): void {
  if (!enabled) {
    ww.setHistory([]);
    return;
  }
  ww.setHistory(
    HISTORY.map((e): [number, boolean] => {
      const skip = random.chance(HISTORY_SKIP_ODDS);
      const delay = random.nextInt(0, HISTORY_MAX_DELAY_DAYS + 1);
      return [skip ? -1 : dayOfDate(e.date) + delay, false];
    }),
  );
}

/**
 * Fires every entry due by `day`, in table order. Only a nation (AI) acts on
 * the calendar; a human decides for themselves. An entry is used up whether
 * or not it could fire.
 */
export function processHistory(
  mg: Game,
  ww: WorldWar,
  day: number,
  tick: number,
): void {
  const plan = ww.history();
  for (let i = 0; i < plan.length; i++) {
    const [fireDay, done] = plan[i];
    if (done || fireDay < 0 || fireDay > day) continue;
    ww.markHistoryDone(i);
    const e = HISTORY[i];
    const actor = ww.playerOfTag(e.actor);
    const target = ww.playerOfTag(e.target);
    if (actor === null || target === null) continue;
    if (actor.type() !== PlayerType.Nation) continue;
    if (e.kind === "declare") declareWar(mg, ww, actor, target, tick);
    else joinWar(mg, ww, actor, target, tick);
  }
}
```

```ts
import {
  campScores,
  CountryScoreInput,
  lastCampStanding,
  rankMembers,
  scoreBp,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Score";

const c = (
  smallID: number,
  faction: CountryScoreInput["faction"],
  vp: number,
  tiles: number,
  major = false,
  alive = true,
): CountryScoreInput => ({ smallID, faction, vp, tiles, major, alive });

describe("the 1936 score (spec §7.6)", () => {
  test("70% victory points and 30% land, in whole basis points", () => {
    expect(scoreBp(46, 5000, 142, 20300)).toBe(2267 + 738);
    expect(scoreBp(0, 0, 0, 0)).toBe(0);
  });

  test("factions add up their members; unaligned countries stand alone", () => {
    const camps = campScores(
      [
        c(1, "axis", 25, 3500, true),
        c(2, "axis", 21, 1500, true),
        c(3, "allies", 21, 300, true),
        c(4, "allies", 24, 4000, true),
        c(5, "comintern", 27, 8000, true),
        c(6, null, 24, 3000),
      ],
      142,
      20300,
    );
    expect(camps.map((x) => x.camp)).toEqual(["axis", "allies", "comintern", 6]);
    expect(camps[0]).toMatchObject({ members: [1, 2], vp: 46, tiles: 5000, scoreBp: 3005, livingMajors: 2 });
  });

  test("ties go to more victory points, then the fixed camp order; dead camps drop out", () => {
    const camps = campScores(
      [c(1, "axis", 10, 100), c(2, null, 10, 100), c(3, "allies", 10, 100, false, false)],
      100,
      1000,
    );
    expect(camps.map((x) => x.camp)).toEqual(["axis", 2]);
  });

  test("the last camp with a living major", () => {
    const camps = campScores(
      [c(1, "axis", 10, 100, true), c(2, "allies", 10, 100, true, false), c(3, null, 5, 50)],
      100,
      1000,
    );
    expect(lastCampStanding(camps)?.camp).toBe("axis");
    expect(lastCampStanding(campScores([c(1, "axis", 1, 1, true), c(2, null, 1, 1, true)], 2, 2))).toBeNull();
  });

  test("MVP order inside a camp", () => {
    const inputs = [c(1, "axis", 5, 100), c(2, "axis", 20, 100), c(3, "allies", 50, 100)];
    expect(rankMembers(inputs, [1, 2], 100, 1000).map((r) => r.smallID)).toEqual([2, 1]);
  });
});
```

`tests/ww1936/Capitulation.test.ts`:

```ts
import { GameUpdateType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameUpdates";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { dayStartTick } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import type { Game } from "@openfront/engine/game/Game";
import {
  controlledVp,
  heldStartVp,
  pickVictor,
} from "@openfront/engine/ww1936/Capitulation";
import { byTag, HUMAN, step, stepTo, wwRunner, wwRunnerCapture } from "./util";

/** The VP tile of the named region on the test map. */
function vpTileOf(g: Game, name: string): number {
  const s = g.worldWar()!.scenario;
  const r = s.landRegions.find((x) => x.name === name)!;
  return s.vpTile(r);
}

/** Runs to the start of the next day after now (the daily check runs then). */
function nextDay(runner: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof wwRunner>>): void {
  const ww = runner.game.worldWar()!;
  stepTo(runner, dayStartTick(ww.day(runner.game.ticks()) + 1, WW_PREP_TICKS) + 1);
}

describe("victory points and capitulation (spec §7.5)", () => {
  test("at the start everyone controls exactly its own points", async () => {
    const g = (await wwRunner()).game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const vp = controlledVp(g, ww);
    for (const p of g.allPlayers()) {
      expect(vp[p.smallID()]).toBe(ww.startVp(p.smallID()));
      expect(heldStartVp(g, ww, p.smallID())).toBe(ww.startVp(p.smallID()));
    }
    expect(ww.startVp(byTag(g, "POL").smallID())).toBe(24);
  });

  test("losing 80% of its starting points makes a country capitulate", async () => {
    const { runner, updates } = await wwRunnerCapture();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const [deu, pol] = [byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL")];
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
    ww.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    deu.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Krakow"));
    nextDay(runner);
    expect(ww.isCapitulated(pol.smallID())).toBe(false); // 3 of 24 lost
    deu.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Warsaw"));
    nextDay(runner);
    expect(ww.isCapitulated(pol.smallID())).toBe(true);
    expect(ww.isAtWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID())).toBe(false);
    expect(pol.outgoingAttacks()).toEqual([]);
    expect(ww.data().capitulated).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ smallID: pol.smallID(), victor: deu.smallID() }),
    ]);
    const capitulated = updates
      .flatMap((u) => u.updates[GameUpdateType.DisplayEvent])
      .find((m) => m.message === "events_display.ww1936_capitulated");
    expect(capitulated?.params).toEqual({ loserTag: "POL", victorTag: "DEU" });
  });

  test("the land moves to the victor; the conquest is settled once, when the last tile moves", async () => {
    const { runner, updates } = await wwRunnerCapture();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const [deu, pol] = [byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL")];
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
    ww.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    deu.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Warsaw"));
    const warsaw = vpTileOf(g, "Warsaw");
    const krakow = vpTileOf(g, "Krakow");
    nextDay(runner);
    step(runner, 3);
    expect(pol.isAlive()).toBe(false);
    expect(pol.numTilesOwned()).toBe(0);
    expect(g.owner(warsaw)).toBe(deu);
    expect(g.owner(krakow)).toBe(deu);
    const conquests = updates
      .flatMap((u) => u.updates[GameUpdateType.ConquestEvent])
      .filter((c) => c.conqueredId === pol.id());
    expect(conquests).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  test("tiles someone else took are skipped", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const [deu, pol, sun] = [byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL"), byTag(g, "SUN")];
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
    ww.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    ww.setWar(sun.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    const krakow = vpTileOf(g, "Krakow");
    sun.conquer(krakow);
    deu.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Warsaw"));
    nextDay(runner);
    step(runner, 3);
    expect(g.owner(krakow)).toBe(sun);
    expect(pol.isAlive()).toBe(false);
  });

  test("the victor is the enemy holding most of its points", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const [deu, pol, sun] = [byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL"), byTag(g, "SUN")];
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
    ww.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    ww.setWar(sun.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    sun.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Krakow"));
    expect(pickVictor(g, ww, pol)).toBe(sun);
    deu.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Warsaw"));
    expect(pickVictor(g, ww, pol)).toBe(deu);
  });

  test("a human whose country capitulates is left watching", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({}, [{ clientID: HUMAN, country: "POL" }]);
    const g = runner.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const [deu, pol] = [byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL")];
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
    ww.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    deu.conquer(vpTileOf(g, "Warsaw"));
    nextDay(runner);
    step(runner, 3);
    expect(g.playerByClientID(HUMAN)!.isAlive()).toBe(false);
    expect(g.getWinner()).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `Capitulation.ts` 본문**

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/Capitulation.ts` 전체를 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts
import { MessageType, UnitType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { COUNTRIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { WW_TRANSFER_TILES_PER_TICK } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import type { Game, Player } from "../game/Game";
import type { WorldWar } from "./WorldWar";

/** Spec §7.5: a region's points go to the owner of its VP tile. Indexed by smallID. */
export function controlledVp(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar): number[] {
  const out = new Array<number>(ww.scenario.countries.length + 1).fill(0);
  for (const r of ww.scenario.landRegions) {
    const owner = mg.ownerID(ww.scenario.vpTile(r));
    if (owner > 0 && owner < out.length) out[owner] += r.vp;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Of `smallID`'s starting regions, the victory points it still controls. */
export function heldStartVp(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar, smallID: number): number {
  const owners = ww.scenario.ownerByRegion();
  let held = 0;
  for (const r of ww.scenario.landRegions) {
    if (owners[r.id] !== smallID) continue;
    if (mg.ownerID(ww.scenario.vpTile(r)) === smallID) held += r.vp;
  }
  return held;
}

/** Points of `loser`'s starting regions each other country controls. */
function takenFrom(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar, loser: number): Map<number, number> {
  const owners = ww.scenario.ownerByRegion();
  const out = new Map<number, number>();
  for (const r of ww.scenario.landRegions) {
    if (owners[r.id] !== loser) continue;
    const holder = mg.ownerID(ww.scenario.vpTile(r));
    if (holder === 0 || holder === loser) continue;
    out.set(holder, (out.get(holder) ?? 0) + r.vp);
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * The enemy holding most of the loser's starting points (ties: the smaller
 * smallID); when no enemy holds any, whoever holds most; else null.
 */
export function pickVictor(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar, loser: Player): Player | null {
  const taken = [...takenFrom(mg, ww, loser.smallID())].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
  const enemies = new Set(ww.enemiesOf(loser.smallID()));
  for (const enemiesOnly of [true, false]) {
    let best = 0;
    let bestVp = 0;
    for (const [id, vp] of taken) {
      if (enemiesOnly && !enemies.has(id)) continue;
      if (vp > bestVp) {
        best = id;
        bestVp = vp;
      }
    }
    if (best !== 0) {
      const p = mg.playerBySmallID(best);
      return p.isPlayer() ? p : null;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/** Once a day (WorldWarExecution): every country past its surrender line capitulates. */
export function checkCapitulations(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar, tick: number): void {
  for (let id = 1; id <= ww.scenario.countries.length; id++) {
    if (ww.isCapitulated(id)) continue;
    const p = mg.playerBySmallID(id);
    if (!p.isPlayer() || !p.isAlive()) continue;
    const start = ww.startVp(id);
    if (start === 0) continue;
    const lost = start - heldStartVp(mg, ww, id);
    if (lost * 100 < start * COUNTRIES[ww.tagOf(id)].surrenderPct) continue;
    capitulate(mg, ww, p, tick);
  }
}

/**
 * Spec §7.5: the country leaves every war, its army is gone, and the rest of
 * its land goes to the victor over the next ticks.
 */
function capitulate(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar, loser: Player, tick: number): void {
  const victor = pickVictor(mg, ww, loser);
  ww.addCapitulation(loser.smallID(), victor?.smallID() ?? 0, tick);
  ww.endWarsOf(loser.smallID());
  // Its army is gone: the troops at home, its running attacks and the
  // troops at sea.
  loser.setTroops(0);
  for (const a of [...loser.outgoingAttacks()]) a.delete();
  for (const u of loser.units(UnitType.TransportShip)) u.delete(false);
  if (victor !== null) {
    ww.queueTransfer(loser.smallID(), victor.smallID(), Uint32Array.from(loser.tiles()));
  }
  mg.displayMessage(
    "events_display.ww1936_capitulated",
    MessageType.WORLD_WAR,
    null,
    undefined,
    {
      loserTag: ww.tagOf(loser.smallID()),
      victorTag: victor === null ? "" : ww.tagOf(victor.smallID()),
    },
  );
}

/**
 * Every tick: moves up to WW_TRANSFER_TILES_PER_TICK tiles of the oldest
 * transfer, skipping tiles the loser no longer owns. When a transfer ends
 * and the loser has no land left, the conquest is settled (gold, stats).
 */
export function stepTransfers(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar): void {
  const list = ww.transfers();
  let budget = WW_TRANSFER_TILES_PER_TICK;
  while (budget > 0 && list.length > 0) {
    const t = list[0];
    const to = mg.playerBySmallID(t.to);
    while (budget > 0 && t.next < t.tiles.length) {
      const tile = t.tiles[t.next++];
      if (to.isPlayer() && mg.ownerID(tile) === t.from) {
        to.conquer(tile);
        budget--;
      }
    }
    if (t.next < t.tiles.length) return;
    list.shift();
    const from = mg.playerBySmallID(t.from);
    if (from.isPlayer() && to.isPlayer() && !from.isAlive()) {
      mg.conquerPlayer(to, from);
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `Victory.ts` 본문**

`packages/engine/src/ww1936/Victory.ts` 전체를 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts
import { COUNTRIES, FactionId } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { WW_GAME_MINUTES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import {
  CampScore,
  campScores,
  CountryScoreInput,
  lastCampStanding,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Score";
import type { Game, Player } from "../game/Game";
import { controlledVp } from "./Capitulation";
import type { WorldWar } from "./WorldWar";

export interface WorldWarWin {
  winner: Player | FactionId;
  reason: "ww_score" | "ww_last_camp";
  camp: CampScore;
}

/** Each country's numbers now (spec §7.6). Capitulated countries are not alive. */
export function scoreInputs(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar): CountryScoreInput[] {
  const vp = controlledVp(mg, ww);
  return ww.scenario.countries.map((tag, i) => {
    const id = i + 1;
    const p = mg.playerBySmallID(id);
    const alive = p.isPlayer() && p.isAlive() && !ww.isCapitulated(id);
    return {
      smallID: id,
      faction: COUNTRIES[tag].faction,
      major: COUNTRIES[tag].major,
      alive,
      vp: vp[id],
      tiles: p.isPlayer() ? p.numTilesOwned() : 0,
    };
  });
}

export function worldWarCamps(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar): CampScore[] {
  return campScores(scoreInputs(mg, ww), ww.scenario.totalVp(), mg.numLandTiles());
}

/**
 * Spec §7.6: the only camp left with a living major wins at once; at the
 * time limit the best camp wins on score. Null while the game goes on.
 */
export function worldWarVerdict(
  mg: Game,
  ww: WorldWar,
  elapsedSeconds: number,
): WorldWarWin | null {
  const camps = worldWarCamps(mg, ww);
  const last = lastCampStanding(camps);
  if (last !== null) {
    return { winner: campWinner(mg, last), reason: "ww_last_camp", camp: last };
  }
  const minutes = mg.config().maxTimerMinutes() ?? WW_GAME_MINUTES;
  if (elapsedSeconds < minutes * 60) return null;
  const best = camps[0];
  if (best === undefined) return null;
  return { winner: campWinner(mg, best), reason: "ww_score", camp: best };
}

export function checkWorldWarWin(mg: Game, ww: WorldWar): WorldWarWin | null {
  return worldWarVerdict(mg, ww, mg.elapsedGameSeconds());
}

function campWinner(mg: Game, camp: CampScore): Player | FactionId {
  if (typeof camp.camp !== "number") return camp.camp;
  const p = mg.playerBySmallID(camp.camp);
  if (!p.isPlayer()) throw new Error(`camp ${camp.camp} has no player`);
  return p;
}
```

### Task 7: AI — 전쟁 중인 상대만 노린다

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/engine/src/execution/utils/AiAttackBehavior.ts`
- Test: `tests/ww1936/NationAi.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Player.canAttackPlayer`(Task 1이 전쟁 판정을 넣었다), `Game.worldWar()`, `WorldWar.setWar`, 테스트 유틸
- Produces: 없음(행동 변경). 모드가 꺼진 게임에서는 PRNG 호출·분기가 하나도 바뀌지 않는다(모든 새 조건이 `worldWar() !== null` 뒤에 있다).

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트**

`tests/ww1936/NationAi.test.ts`:

```ts
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import type { Game, Player } from "@openfront/engine/game/Game";
import { byTag, step, stepTo, wwRunner } from "./util";

/** Every player-on-player attack running now, as "ATT>DEF" tags. */
function attacks(g: Game): string[] {
  const ww = g.worldWar()!;
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const p of g.allPlayers()) {
    for (const a of p.outgoingAttacks()) {
      const t = a.target();
      if (t.isPlayer()) out.push(`${ww.tagOf(p.smallID())}>${ww.tagOf((t as Player).smallID())}`);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

describe("nation AI in World War 1936 (spec §7.7)", () => {
  test("nations at peace attack nobody", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({ historicalAi: false });
    for (let t = 0; t < 1500; t += 50) {
      step(runner, 50);
      expect(attacks(runner.game)).toEqual([]);
    }
  }, 60_000);

  test("a nation at war attacks its enemy and only its enemy", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner({ historicalAi: false });
    const g = runner.game;
    stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS);
    const deu = byTag(g, "DEU");
    const pol = byTag(g, "POL");
    g.worldWar()!.setWar(deu.smallID(), pol.smallID(), g.ticks());
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (let t = 0; t < 3000 && !seen.has("DEU>POL") && !seen.has("POL>DEU"); t += 20) {
      step(runner, 20);
      for (const a of attacks(g)) seen.add(a);
    }
    expect(seen.has("DEU>POL") || seen.has("POL>DEU")).toBe(true);
    for (const a of seen) expect(["DEU>POL", "POL>DEU"]).toContain(a);
  }, 60_000);
});
```

Run: `npx vitest tests/ww1936/NationAi.test.ts --run`

`tests/server/CountryPick.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { GameType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import type { GameConfig } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import { ServerStartGameMessage } from "@openfront/shared/WireSchemas";
import type { Client } from "../../src/server/Client";
import {
  cid,
  makeClient,
  makeGame,
  mockWsOf,
  startGame,
} from "../util/GameServerHarness";

const player = (id: string): Client =>
  makeClient({ clientID: cid(id), persistentID: `${id}-pid`, publicId: `${id}-pub` });

const pick = (c: Client, tag: "DEU" | "GBR" | null) =>
  mockWsOf(c).emit({ type: "pick_country", tag });

describe("World War 1936 country picks (spec §8.1)", () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllTimers();
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  const lobby = (config: Partial<GameConfig> = { worldWar1936: true }) => {
    const game = makeGame({ config: { gameType: GameType.Private, ...config } });
    const a = player("a");
    const b = player("b");
    game.joinClient(a);
    game.joinClient(b);
    return { game, a, b };
  };

  it("a taken country is refused", async () => {
    const { game, a, b } = lobby();
    await pick(a, "DEU");
    await pick(b, "DEU");
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toEqual([{ clientID: a.clientID, tag: "DEU" }]);
    await pick(b, "GBR");
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toEqual([
      { clientID: a.clientID, tag: "DEU" },
      { clientID: b.clientID, tag: "GBR" },
    ]);
  });

  it("giving a country up, or turning spectator, frees it", async () => {
    const { game, a, b } = lobby();
    await pick(a, "DEU");
    await pick(a, null);
    await pick(b, "DEU");
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toEqual([{ clientID: b.clientID, tag: "DEU" }]);
    await mockWsOf(b).emit({ type: "spectate", spectator: true });
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toEqual([]);
    await pick(a, "DEU");
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toEqual([{ clientID: a.clientID, tag: "DEU" }]);
  });

  it("the start gives each player the country it picked", async () => {
    const { game, a, b } = lobby();
    await pick(a, "DEU");
    startGame(game);
    const start = mockWsOf(a)
      .sent()
      .find((m): m is ServerStartGameMessage => m.type === "start")!;
    const players = start.gameStartInfo.players;
    expect(players.find((p) => p.clientID === a.clientID)?.country).toBe("DEU");
    expect(players.find((p) => p.clientID === b.clientID)?.country).toBeUndefined();
    await pick(b, "GBR"); // too late
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toEqual([{ clientID: a.clientID, tag: "DEU" }]);
  });

  it("outside the mode there are no picks", async () => {
    const { game, a } = lobby({});
    await pick(a, "DEU");
    expect(game.gameInfo().countryPicks).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

`src/server/GameServer.ts`:
1. import에 `import type { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";`
2. 필드(`private stage: …` 근처)에:

```ts
  /** World War 1936 lobbies (spec §8.1): clientID → the country it picked. */
  private countryPicks = new Map<ClientID, CountryTag>();
```

3. 클라이언트 메시지 `switch`의 `case "spectate": { … }` 다음에:

```ts
      case "pick_country": {
        this.pickCountry(client, clientMsg.tag);
        break;
      }
```

4. `setSpectator` 다음에 새 메서드 둘:

```ts
  /**
   * World War 1936 (spec §8.1): a lobby player picks a country, or gives its
   * pick up with null. A country another player holds is refused: the
   * message is dropped, and every lobby list shows who holds what.
   */
  private pickCountry(client: Client, tag: CountryTag | null): void {
    if (this.gameConfig.worldWar1936 !== true) return;
    if (this.stage !== "lobby" || this.ended || client.spectator === true) {
      return;
    }
    if (tag === null) {
      if (this.countryPicks.delete(client.clientID)) this.broadcastLobbyInfo();
      return;
    }
    if (
      this.livePicks().some(
        (p) => p.tag === tag && p.clientID !== client.clientID,
      )
    ) {
      return;
    }
    // A player who left or turned spectator may still be listed for it.
    for (const [id, held] of this.countryPicks) {
      if (held === tag && id !== client.clientID) this.countryPicks.delete(id);
    }
    this.countryPicks.set(client.clientID, tag);
    this.broadcastLobbyInfo();
  }

  /** Picks of the players still in the lobby (not spectators), in pick order. */
  private livePicks(): { clientID: ClientID; tag: CountryTag }[] {
    const playing = new Set(this.clients.players().map((c) => c.clientID));
    return [...this.countryPicks]
      .filter(([id]) => playing.has(id))
      .map(([clientID, tag]) => ({ clientID, tag }));
  }
```

5. `gameInfo()`가 돌려주는 객체의 마지막 줄(`queued: …,`) 다음에:

```ts
      ...(this.gameConfig.worldWar1936 === true
        ? { countryPicks: this.livePicks() }
        : {}),
```

6. `start()`의 `players: this.clients.players().map((c) => ({ … }))` 객체 마지막(`teamIndex: …,`) 다음에:

```ts
        // World War 1936: the country this player picked (the engine gives
        // the rest the next free one, spec §7.1).
        ...(this.gameConfig.worldWar1936 === true &&
        this.countryPicks.has(c.clientID)
          ? { country: this.countryPicks.get(c.clientID)! }
          : {}),
```

```ts
import { GameMapType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { MAJOR_TAGS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "../../src/client/components/ww1936/CountryPicker";
import type { CountryPicker } from "../../src/client/components/ww1936/CountryPicker";
import { getRandomMapType } from "../../src/client/utilities/GameConfigHelpers";

async function picker(): Promise<CountryPicker> {
  const el = document.createElement("ww-country-picker") as CountryPicker;
  el.myClientID = "A";
  el.picks = [{ clientID: "B", tag: "DEU" }];
  el.usernames = new Map([["B", "bob"]]);
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return el;
}

describe("<ww-country-picker> (spec §8.1)", () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    document.body.innerHTML = "";
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it("shows the seven majors first, then every country by region", async () => {
    const el = await picker();
    const tags = [...el.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("button[data-tag]")].map(
      (b) => b.dataset.tag,
    );
    expect(tags.slice(0, 7)).toEqual([...MAJOR_TAGS]);
    // Every country once: the majors as cards, the rest in their region.
    expect(tags).toHaveLength(79);
    expect(new Set(tags).size).toBe(79);
    expect(el.querySelectorAll("[data-group]")).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it("a country someone else holds is greyed out with their name", async () => {
    const el = await picker();
    const deu = el.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-tag="DEU"]')!;
    expect(deu.disabled).toBe(true);
    expect(deu.textContent).toContain("bob");
  });

  it("tapping a free country picks it, tapping it again gives it up", async () => {
    const el = await picker();
    const seen: (string | null)[] = [];
    el.addEventListener("country-picked", (e) =>
      seen.push((e as CustomEvent<{ tag: string | null }>).detail.tag),
    );
    el.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-tag="GBR"]')!.click();
    el.selected = "GBR";
    await el.updateComplete;
    el.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-tag="GBR"]')!.click();
    expect(seen).toEqual(["GBR", null]);
  });
});

describe("the World 1936 map outside its mode", () => {
  it("is never the random map", () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
      vi.spyOn(Math, "random").mockReturnValueOnce(i / 200);
      expect(getRandomMapType()).not.toBe(GameMapType.World1936);
    }
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 나라 고르기 컴포넌트**

`src/client/components/ww1936/CountryPicker.ts`:

```ts
import {
  COUNTRIES,
  COUNTRY_GROUPS,
  COUNTRY_TAGS,
  CountryTag,
  MAJOR_TAGS,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { assetUrl } from "@openfront/shared/AssetUrls";
import { html, LitElement, nothing, TemplateResult } from "lit";
import { customElement, property } from "lit/decorators.js";
import { translateText } from "../../Utils";

const FACTION_DOT: Record<string, string> = {
  axis: "bg-zinc-400",
  allies: "bg-sky-400",
  comintern: "bg-red-500",
};

/**
 * World War 1936 lobby (spec §8.1): the seven majors as cards, then every
 * country by region. A country another player holds is greyed out with
 * their name. Tapping a free country picks it; tapping my own gives it up.
 */
@customElement("ww-country-picker")
export class CountryPicker extends LitElement {
  @property({ attribute: false }) picks: { clientID: string; tag: CountryTag }[] = [];
  @property({ attribute: false }) myClientID = "";
  @property({ attribute: false }) usernames: Map<string, string> = new Map();
  @property({ attribute: false }) selected: CountryTag | null = null;

  createRenderRoot() {
    return this;
  }

  private holderOf(tag: CountryTag): string | null {
    const p = this.picks.find((x) => x.tag === tag && x.clientID !== this.myClientID);
    return p === undefined ? null : (this.usernames.get(p.clientID) ?? "?");
  }

  private onPick(tag: CountryTag) {
    const next = this.selected === tag ? null : tag;
    this.dispatchEvent(
      new CustomEvent("country-picked", {
        detail: { tag: next },
        bubbles: true,
        composed: true,
      }),
    );
  }

  private flag(tag: CountryTag, size: string): TemplateResult {
    return html`<img
      src=${assetUrl(`flags/${COUNTRIES[tag].flag}.svg`)}
      alt=""
      class="${size} shrink-0 rounded-sm object-cover"
    />`;
  }

  private card(tag: CountryTag): TemplateResult {
    const holder = this.holderOf(tag);
    const mine = this.selected === tag;
    const faction = COUNTRIES[tag].faction;
    return html`<button
      data-tag=${tag}
      ?disabled=${holder !== null}
      @click=${() => this.onPick(tag)}
      class="flex flex-col items-start gap-1 rounded-xl border p-3 text-left transition-colors ${mine
        ? "border-amber-400 bg-amber-400/15"
        : "border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10"} ${holder !== null
        ? "opacity-40"
        : ""}"
    >
      <span class="flex items-center gap-2">
        ${this.flag(tag, "h-5 w-8")}
        <span class="text-sm font-bold text-white">
          ${translateText(`ww1936.country.${tag}`)}
        </span>
      </span>
      <span class="text-xs text-white/70">
        ${translateText(`ww1936.leader.${tag}`)}
      </span>
      ${faction === null
        ? nothing
        : html`<span class="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-white/70">
            <span class="size-2 rounded-full ${FACTION_DOT[faction]}"></span>
            ${translateText(`ww1936.faction.${faction}`)}
          </span>`}
      ${holder === null
        ? nothing
        : html`<span class="text-xs text-white/60">
            ${translateText("ww1936.lobby.taken_by", { name: holder })}
          </span>`}
    </button>`;
  }

  private row(tag: CountryTag): TemplateResult {
    const holder = this.holderOf(tag);
    const mine = this.selected === tag;
    return html`<button
      data-tag=${tag}
      ?disabled=${holder !== null}
      @click=${() => this.onPick(tag)}
      class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-xs ${mine
        ? "border-amber-400 bg-amber-400/15"
        : "border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10"} ${holder !== null
        ? "opacity-40"
        : ""}"
    >
      ${this.flag(tag, "h-3.5 w-5")}
      <span class="font-semibold text-white">
        ${translateText(`ww1936.country.${tag}`)}
      </span>
      ${holder === null
        ? nothing
        : html`<span class="ml-auto text-white/60">${holder}</span>`}
    </button>`;
  }

  render() {
    return html`
      <div class="space-y-4">
        <p class="text-sm text-white/70">${translateText("ww1936.lobby.pick_hint")}</p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 md:grid-cols-4">
          ${MAJOR_TAGS.map((t) => this.card(t))}
        </div>
        ${COUNTRY_GROUPS.map(
          (g) => html`<section data-group=${g} class="space-y-2">
            <div class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40">
              ${translateText(`ww1936.group.${g}`)}
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 md:grid-cols-4">
              ${COUNTRY_TAGS.filter(
                (t) => COUNTRIES[t].group === g && !COUNTRIES[t].major,
              ).map((t) => this.row(t))}
            </div>
          </section>`,
        )}
      </div>
    `;
  }
}

declare global {
  interface HTMLElementTagNameMap {
    "ww-country-picker": CountryPicker;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 지도 고르기에서 빼기, 무작위 지도에서 빼기**

`src/client/utilities/GameConfigHelpers.ts`의 `getRandomMapType`을 다음으로 바꾼다:

```ts
export function getRandomMapType(): GameMapType {
  // World 1936 belongs to its mode (spec §2.2) and is never a random pick.
  const maps = Object.values(GameMapType).filter(
    (m) => m !== GameMapType.World1936,
  );
  const randIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * maps.length);
  return maps[randIdx] as GameMapType;
}
```

```ts
              "text-aquarius",
              "bg-malibu-blue/20",
              "ww1936.lobby.pick_country",
              settings.worldWar.picker,
            )
          : renderSection(
              MAP_ICON,
              …(지금 인자 그대로)…
            )}
```

3. 방식(`MODE_ICON`) 절과 팀 수 절을 `${settings.worldWar ? nothing : html`…`}`로 감싼다(팀 수 절은 이미 있는 FFA 조건 바깥에 둔다).
4. 옵션 절 안의 봇 슬라이더 `<div class="col-span-2 …"><fluent-slider … bots …></div>`를 `${settings.worldWar ? nothing : html`…`}`로 감싼다.
5. 단위 절(`ENABLES_ICON`)을 `${settings.worldWar ? nothing : renderSection(ENABLES_ICON, …)}`로 감싼다.


```ts
      case "game_settings.world_war_1936":

          this.warfareExpansion = false;
```

```ts
      const baseConfig = { …(지금 config 객체 그대로)…,
        ...(this.worldWar1936
          ? { worldWar1936: true, historicalAi: this.historicalAi }
          : {}),
      };
      // World War 1936 (spec §2.2): the client loads the map named here, so
      // the mode's fixed map and rules are applied before the start.
      const config = normalizeWorldWar1936Config(baseConfig);
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 화면 테스트**

`tests/client/WorldWarHud.test.ts`:

```ts
import { COUNTRIES, CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { ScenarioDataSchema } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/ScenarioTypes";
import { dayStartTick } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import { ScenarioIndex } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

vi.mock("../../src/client/Utils", async (importOriginal) => ({
  ...(await importOriginal<typeof import("../../src/client/Utils")>()),
  translateText: (key: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>) =>
    params
      ? `[${key}] ${Object.entries(params)
          .map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`)
          .join(",")}`
      : `[${key}]`,
}));

import {
  bandModel,
  callIns,
  countryLabel,
  diplomacyModel,
  regionInfo,
  translateCountryParams,
  TwoTap,
  wwWinView,
  type WwGame,
} from "../../src/client/hud/ww1936/WorldWarModel";
import {
  ToggleWwDiplomacyEvent,
  WwTopBand,
} from "../../src/client/hud/ww1936/WwTopBand";

const MINI = path.join(__dirname, "../testdata/maps/ww1936mini");
const scenario = ScenarioIndex.fromFiles(
  ScenarioDataSchema.parse(
    JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(MINI, "scenario.json"), "utf8")),
  ),
  new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(path.join(MINI, "regions.bin"))),
);
const ID = (tag: CountryTag) => scenario.smallIdOf(tag);

interface Fake {
  ticks?: number;
  me?: CountryTag;
  wars?: [CountryTag, CountryTag][];
  capitulated?: CountryTag[];
  humans?: Partial<Record<CountryTag, [string, string]>>;
  /** Tiles that changed hands: tile → new owner. */
  taken?: Map<number, CountryTag>;
}

function fakeGame(o: Fake = {}): WwGame {
  const owners = scenario.ownerByRegion();
  const ownerID = (t: number) =>
    o.taken?.has(t) ? ID(o.taken.get(t)!) : (owners[scenario.regionAt(t)] ?? 0);
  const tiles = new Array<number>(scenario.countries.length + 1).fill(0);
  for (let t = 0; t < scenario.regionIds.length; t++) tiles[ownerID(t)]++;
  const players = scenario.countries.map((tag, i) => ({
    id: () => `P${i + 1}`,
    smallID: () => i + 1,
    countryTag: () => tag,
    clientID: () => o.humans?.[tag]?.[0] ?? null,
    displayName: () => COUNTRIES[tag].name,
    isAlive: () => true,
    isPlayer: () => true,
    numTilesOwned: () => tiles[i + 1],
  }));
  return {
    ticks: () => o.ticks ?? 700,
    worldWarScenario: () => scenario,
    worldWarData: () => ({
      prepEndTick: 600,
      wars: (o.wars ?? []).map(([a, b]) => ({ a: ID(a), b: ID(b), sinceTick: 600 })),
      capitulated: (o.capitulated ?? []).map((t) => ({
        smallID: ID(t),
        victor: 0,
        tick: 600,
      })),
    }),
    playerBySmallID: (id: number) => players[id - 1],
    playerByClientID: (cid: string) =>
      players.find((p) => p.clientID() === cid) ?? null,
    myPlayer: () => (o.me === undefined ? null : players[ID(o.me) - 1]),
    ownerID,
    usernameOf: (cid: string) =>
      Object.values(o.humans ?? {}).find((h) => h?.[0] === cid)?.[1] ?? null,
    numLandTiles: () => 20300,
  } as unknown as WwGame;
}

describe("the World War 1936 screens (spec §8.2)", () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    document.body.innerHTML = "";
  });

  it("the band counts the preparation down, then shows the date", () => {
    expect(bandModel(fakeGame({ ticks: 180 })).label).toBe("[ww1936.band.prep] time=0:42");
    expect(bandModel(fakeGame({ ticks: 600 })).label).toBe(
      "[ww1936.date] year=1936,month=[ww1936.month.01],day=1",
    );
    expect(bandModel(fakeGame({ ticks: dayStartTick(366, 600) })).label).toBe(
      "[ww1936.date] year=1937,month=[ww1936.month.01],day=1",
    );
  });

  it("the band names my country, my faction and my wars", () => {
    const m = bandModel(fakeGame({ me: "DEU", wars: [["DEU", "POL"]] }));
    expect(m).toMatchObject({ myTag: "DEU", myFaction: "axis", wars: 1, inPrep: false });
  });

  it("the drawer lists the factions, my wars, the camp scores and the next date", () => {
    const m = diplomacyModel(
      fakeGame({ me: "DEU", wars: [["DEU", "POL"]], capitulated: ["FRA"], ticks: 600 }),
    );
    expect(m.factions.map((f) => [f.id, f.members.map((x) => x.tag)])).toEqual([
      ["axis", ["DEU", "ITA"]],
      ["allies", ["GBR", "FRA"]],
      ["comintern", ["SUN"]],
    ]);
    expect(m.factions[1].members[1].capitulated).toBe(true);
    expect(m.myWars).toEqual(["POL"]);
    expect(m.camps[0]).toMatchObject({ mine: true, scoreBp: 3005 });
    // The first entry whose two countries are on this map: Germany → Poland.
    expect(m.nextHistory).toBe("[ww1936.date] year=1939,month=[ww1936.month.09],day=1");
  });

  it("the region line shows the name, the points and the 1936 owner when it changed", () => {
    const krakow = scenario.landRegions.find((r) => r.name === "Krakow")!;
    const tile = scenario.vpTile(krakow);
    expect(regionInfo(fakeGame(), tile, true)).toEqual({
      name: "크라쿠프",
      vp: 3,
      originalOwner: null,
    });
    const taken = new Map([[tile, "DEU" as CountryTag]]);
    expect(regionInfo(fakeGame({ taken }), tile, false)).toEqual({
      name: "Krakow",
      vp: 3,
      originalOwner: "POL",
    });
  });

  it("names: the country, and the username for a human", () => {
    const g = fakeGame({ humans: { DEU: ["C1", "minsu"] } });
    expect(countryLabel(g, g.playerBySmallID(ID("DEU")) as never)).toBe(
      "[ww1936.country.DEU] · minsu",
    );
    expect(countryLabel(g, g.playerBySmallID(ID("ITA")) as never)).toBe(
      "[ww1936.country.ITA]",
    );
    expect(translateCountryParams({ attackerTag: "DEU", targetTag: "FRA", allies: 1 })).toEqual({
      attackerTag: "DEU",
      targetTag: "FRA",
      allies: 1,
      attacker: "[ww1936.country.DEU]",
      target: "[ww1936.country.FRA]",
    });
  });

  it("a declaration needs a second tap within three seconds", () => {
    const t = new TwoTap();
    expect(t.tap("x", 0)).toBe(false);
    expect(t.tap("x", 2999)).toBe(true);
    expect(t.tap("x", 5000)).toBe(false);
    expect(t.tap("x", 8001)).toBe(false);
    expect(t.tap("y", 8002)).toBe(false);
    expect(t.tap("y", 8003)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("the confirmation counts the target's faction members it brings in", () => {
    const fra = (g: WwGame) => g.playerBySmallID(ID("FRA")) as never;
    expect(callIns(fakeGame(), fra(fakeGame()))).toBe(1);
    const g = fakeGame({ capitulated: ["GBR"] });
    expect(callIns(g, fra(g))).toBe(0);
  });

  it("the win names the faction, celebrates on its side and lists its MVPs", () => {
    const win = wwWinView(fakeGame({ me: "DEU", humans: { DEU: ["C1", "minsu"] } }), [
      "team",
      "axis",
      "C1",
    ]);
    expect(win.isWin).toBe(true);
    expect(win.title).toBe("[ww1936.win.your_faction] faction=[ww1936.faction.axis]");
    expect(win.mvp[0]).toBe("[ww1936.country.DEU] · minsu");
    const lost = wwWinView(fakeGame({ me: "GBR" }), ["team", "axis"]);
    expect(lost.isWin).toBe(false);
    expect(lost.title).toBe("[ww1936.win.faction_won] faction=[ww1936.faction.axis]");
  });

  it("<ww-top-band> renders the date and opens the drawer", async () => {
    const band = document.createElement("ww-top-band") as WwTopBand;
    const emitted: unknown[] = [];
    band.game = fakeGame({ me: "DEU" }) as never;
    band.eventBus = { emit: (e: unknown) => emitted.push(e) } as never;
    document.body.appendChild(band);
    band.tick();
    await band.updateComplete;
    expect(band.querySelector("[data-ww-date]")!.textContent).toContain("ww1936.date");
    band.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("[data-ww-wars]")!.click();
    expect(emitted[0]).toBeInstanceOf(ToggleWwDiplomacyEvent);
  });
});
```
