Expected: FAIL (모듈이 없다)

- [ ] **Step 2: 화면 모델**

`src/client/hud/ww1936/WorldWarModel.ts`:

```ts
import type { TileRef } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameMap";
import type { Winner } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import {
  COUNTRIES,
  CountryTag,
  FACTION_IDS,
  FactionId,
  isCountryTag,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { dateOfDay, dayIndex, dayOfDate } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import { HISTORY } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/History";
import {
  CampScore,
  campScores,
  CountryScoreInput,
  rankMembers,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Score";
import { translateText } from "../../Utils";
import type { GameView, PlayerView } from "../../view";

/** Height of the top band in px; the hover card moves down by it. */
export const WW_BAND_PX = 32;
/** A second tap within this many ms confirms an irreversible action (§14d). */
export const TWO_TAP_MS = 3000;
/** How many MVPs the win screen lists. */
export const WW_MVP_COUNT = 3;

export const FACTION_DOT_CLASS: Readonly<Record<FactionId, string>> = {
  axis: "bg-zinc-400",
  allies: "bg-sky-400",
  comintern: "bg-red-500",
};

/** What the World War 1936 screens read from the game (a fake in tests). */
export type WwGame = Pick<
  GameView,
  | "ticks"
  | "worldWarScenario"
  | "worldWarData"
  | "playerBySmallID"
  | "playerByClientID"
  | "myPlayer"
  | "ownerID"
  | "usernameOf"
  | "numLandTiles"
>;

export function countryName(tag: CountryTag): string {
  return translateText(`ww1936.country.${tag}`);
}

/** "Germany · minsu" for a human's country; the country alone for a nation. */
export function countryLabel(game: WwGame, p: PlayerView): string {
  const tag = p.countryTag();
  const name = tag === null ? p.displayName() : countryName(tag);
  const clientID = p.clientID();
  const user = clientID === null ? null : game.usernameOf(clientID);
  return user === null ? name : `${name} · ${user}`;
}

export function dateLabel(day: number): string {
  const d = dateOfDay(day);
  return translateText("ww1936.date", {
    year: d.year,
    // Two-digit keys: "1".."12" would be ordered as numbers, not as the
    // sorted strings tests/EnJsonSorted.test.ts expects.
    month: translateText(`ww1936.month.${d.month < 10 ? "0" : ""}${d.month}`),
    day: d.day,
  });
}

function prepEnd(game: WwGame): number {
  return game.worldWarData()?.prepEndTick ?? WW_PREP_TICKS;
}

export function today(game: WwGame): number {
  return dayIndex(game.ticks(), prepEnd(game));
}

/** Whole seconds of the preparation minute left; 0 once it is over. */
export function prepSecondsLeft(game: WwGame): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((prepEnd(game) - game.ticks()) / 10));
}

/** smallIDs at war with `smallID`, in the order the wars began. */
export function enemiesOf(game: WwGame, smallID: number): number[] {
  return (game.worldWarData()?.wars ?? []).flatMap((w) =>
    w.a === smallID ? [w.b] : w.b === smallID ? [w.a] : [],
  );
}

export function isCapitulated(game: WwGame, smallID: number): boolean {
  return (game.worldWarData()?.capitulated ?? []).some((c) => c.smallID === smallID);
}

function asPlayer(game: WwGame, smallID: number): PlayerView | null {
  const p = game.playerBySmallID(smallID);
  return p.isPlayer() ? (p as PlayerView) : null;
}

function factionOfPlayer(p: PlayerView | null): FactionId | null {
  const tag = p?.countryTag() ?? null;
  return tag === null ? null : COUNTRIES[tag].faction;
}

/** Same starting faction (decision 8: faction members are friends). */
export function sameFaction(a: PlayerView | null, b: PlayerView | null): boolean {
  const f = factionOfPlayer(a);
  return f !== null && a !== b && f === factionOfPlayer(b);
}

export interface BandModel {
  label: string;
  inPrep: boolean;
  myTag: CountryTag | null;
  myFaction: FactionId | null;
  wars: number;
}

export function bandModel(game: WwGame): BandModel {
  const left = prepSecondsLeft(game);
  const me = game.myPlayer();
  const myTag = me?.countryTag() ?? null;
  const pad = (n: number) => (n < 10 ? `0${n}` : `${n}`);
  return {
    label:
      left > 0
        ? translateText("ww1936.band.prep", {
            time: `${Math.floor(left / 60)}:${pad(left % 60)}`,
          })
        : dateLabel(today(game)),
    inPrep: left > 0,
    myTag,
    myFaction: myTag === null ? null : COUNTRIES[myTag].faction,
    wars: me === null ? 0 : enemiesOf(game, me.smallID()).length,
  };
}

/** Each country's numbers as the screens see them (engine: Victory.scoreInputs). */
export function viewScoreInputs(game: WwGame): CountryScoreInput[] {
  const s = game.worldWarScenario();
  if (s === null) return [];
  const vp = new Array<number>(s.countries.length + 1).fill(0);
  for (const r of s.landRegions) {
    const owner = game.ownerID(s.vpTile(r));
    if (owner > 0 && owner < vp.length) vp[owner] += r.vp;
  }
  return s.countries.map((tag, i) => {
    const id = i + 1;
    const p = asPlayer(game, id);
    return {
      smallID: id,
      faction: COUNTRIES[tag].faction,
      major: COUNTRIES[tag].major,
      alive: p !== null && p.isAlive() && !isCapitulated(game, id),
      vp: vp[id],
      tiles: p?.numTilesOwned() ?? 0,
    };
  });
}

export function viewCamps(game: WwGame): CampScore[] {
  const s = game.worldWarScenario();
  if (s === null) return [];
  return campScores(viewScoreInputs(game), s.totalVp(), game.numLandTiles());
}

export function campName(game: WwGame, camp: FactionId | number): string {
  if (typeof camp !== "number") return translateText(`ww1936.faction.${camp}`);
  const tag = game.worldWarScenario()?.tagOfSmallId(camp);
  return tag === undefined ? "" : countryName(tag);
}

export interface DiplomacyModel {
  factions: {
    id: FactionId;
    members: { tag: CountryTag; capitulated: boolean; alive: boolean }[];
  }[];
  myWars: CountryTag[];
  camps: { name: string; scoreBp: number; mine: boolean }[];
  /** The next date of the historical calendar (as written, not as rolled). */
  nextHistory: string | null;
}

export function diplomacyModel(game: WwGame): DiplomacyModel {
  const s = game.worldWarScenario();
  if (s === null) return { factions: [], myWars: [], camps: [], nextHistory: null };
  const me = game.myPlayer();
  const myID = me?.smallID() ?? 0;
  const myFaction = factionOfPlayer(me);
  const factions = FACTION_IDS.map((id) => ({
    id,
    members: s.countries
      .map((tag, i) => ({ tag, smallID: i + 1 }))
      .filter((m) => COUNTRIES[m.tag].faction === id)
      .map((m) => ({
        tag: m.tag,
        capitulated: isCapitulated(game, m.smallID),
        alive: asPlayer(game, m.smallID)?.isAlive() ?? false,
      })),
  })).filter((f) => f.members.length > 0);
  const day = today(game);
  const next = HISTORY.find(
    (e) =>
      dayOfDate(e.date) >= day &&
      s.smallIdOf(e.actor) !== 0 &&
      s.smallIdOf(e.target) !== 0,
  );
  return {
    factions,
    myWars: enemiesOf(game, myID).map((id) => s.tagOfSmallId(id)!),
    camps: viewCamps(game).map((c) => ({
      name: campName(game, c.camp),
      scoreBp: c.scoreBp,
      mine: c.camp === myFaction || c.camp === myID,
    })),
    nextHistory: next === undefined ? null : dateLabel(dayOfDate(next.date)),
  };
}

/**
 * Countries a declaration on `target` brings in besides the target (spec
 * §7.3): its living, uncapitulated faction members.
 */
export function callIns(game: WwGame, target: PlayerView): number {
  const s = game.worldWarScenario();
  const faction = factionOfPlayer(target);
  if (s === null || faction === null) return 0;
  let n = 0;
  s.countries.forEach((tag, i) => {
    const id = i + 1;
    if (id === target.smallID() || COUNTRIES[tag].faction !== faction) return;
    if (isCapitulated(game, id) || !(asPlayer(game, id)?.isAlive() ?? false)) return;
    n++;
  });
  return n;
}

export interface RegionInfo {
  name: string;
  vp: number;
  /** The 1936 owner when someone else holds the tile now. */
  originalOwner: CountryTag | null;
}

export function regionInfo(game: WwGame, tile: TileRef, korean: boolean): RegionInfo | null {
  const s = game.worldWarScenario();
  if (s === null) return null;
  const r = s.region(s.regionAt(tile));
  if (r === undefined) return null;
  return {
    name: korean ? r.nameKo : r.name,
    vp: r.vp,
    originalOwner: s.smallIdOf(r.owner) === game.ownerID(tile) ? null : r.owner,
  };
}

/** Region and sea-zone names come from scenario.json in two languages (spec §11). */
export function isKoreanUi(): boolean {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return false;
  const sel = document.querySelector("lang-selector") as { currentLang?: string } | null;
  return (sel?.currentLang ?? "en").startsWith("ko");
}

/** Plan decision 13: a "…Tag" param also comes as its translated country name. */
export function translateCountryParams(
  params: Record<string, string | number>,
): Record<string, string | number> {
  const out: Record<string, string | number> = { ...params };
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (!k.endsWith("Tag") || typeof v !== "string") continue;
    out[k.slice(0, -3)] = isCountryTag(v) ? countryName(v) : "";
  }
  return out;
}

/** Spec §8.2: an irreversible action needs a second tap within TWO_TAP_MS. */
export class TwoTap {
  private key: string | null = null;
  private at = 0;

  /** True when this tap confirms. */
  tap(key: string, nowMs: number): boolean {
    if (this.isArmed(key, nowMs)) {
      this.key = null;
      return true;
    }
    this.key = key;
    this.at = nowMs;
    return false;
  }

  isArmed(key: string, nowMs: number): boolean {
    return this.key === key && nowMs - this.at < TWO_TAP_MS;
  }
}

export interface WwWinView {
  title: string;
  isWin: boolean;
  /** The winning camp's best members, best first (spec §7.6). */
  mvp: string[];
}

export function wwWinView(game: WwGame, winner: NonNullable<Winner>): WwWinView {
  const s = game.worldWarScenario()!;
  const me = game.myPlayer();
  const inputs = viewScoreInputs(game);
  const total = s.totalVp();
  const land = game.numLandTiles();
  const labels = (members: number[]) =>
    rankMembers(inputs, members, total, land)
      .slice(0, WW_MVP_COUNT)
      .map((r) => asPlayer(game, r.smallID))
      .filter((p): p is PlayerView => p !== null)
      .map((p) => countryLabel(game, p));
  if (winner[0] === "team") {
    const faction = winner[1] as FactionId;
    const name = translateText(`ww1936.faction.${faction}`);
    const isWin = factionOfPlayer(me) === faction;
    const members = s.countries
      .map((tag, i) => (COUNTRIES[tag].faction === faction ? i + 1 : 0))
      .filter((id) => id !== 0);
    return {
      title: translateText(isWin ? "ww1936.win.your_faction" : "ww1936.win.faction_won", {
        faction: name,
      }),
      isWin,
      mvp: labels(members),
    };
  }
  // An unaligned country won on its own.
  const p =
    winner[0] === "player"
      ? game.playerByClientID(winner[1])
      : asPlayer(game, s.countries.findIndex((t) => COUNTRIES[t].name === winner[1]) + 1);
  const isWin = p !== null && p === me;
  return {
    title: isWin
      ? translateText("win_modal.you_won")
      : translateText("ww1936.win.country_won", {
          country: p === null ? winner[1] : countryLabel(game, p),
        }),
    isWin,
    mvp: [],
  };
}
```

```ts
import { COUNTRIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { assetUrl } from "@openfront/shared/AssetUrls";
import { EventBus, GameEvent } from "@openfront/shared/EventBus";
import { html, LitElement, nothing } from "lit";
import { customElement } from "lit/decorators.js";
import { Controller } from "../../Controller";
import { translateText } from "../../Utils";
import type { GameView } from "../../view";
import { bandModel, countryName, FACTION_DOT_CLASS } from "./WorldWarModel";

export class ToggleWwDiplomacyEvent implements GameEvent {}

/**
 * World War 1936 (spec §8.2): a slim band at the top — the date (or the
 * preparation countdown), my country and faction, and my wars, which open
 * the diplomacy drawer. WW_BAND_PX tall.
 */
@customElement("ww-top-band")
export class WwTopBand extends LitElement implements Controller {
  public game: GameView;
  public eventBus: EventBus;

  createRenderRoot() {
    return this;
  }

  getTickIntervalMs() {
    return 250;
  }

  tick() {
    if (this.game?.worldWarScenario()) this.requestUpdate();
  }

  render() {
    if (!this.game?.worldWarScenario()) return null;
    const m = bandModel(this.game);
    return html`
      <div
        data-ww-band
        class="fixed top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[1002] h-8 max-w-[calc(100vw-32px)] flex items-center gap-3 rounded-b-lg bg-gray-900/85 px-3 text-sm text-white shadow-md"
      >
        <span data-ww-date class="font-semibold tabular-nums ${m.inPrep ? "text-amber-300" : ""}">
          ${m.label}
        </span>
        ${m.myTag === null
          ? nothing
          : html`<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
              <img
                src=${assetUrl(`flags/${COUNTRIES[m.myTag].flag}.svg`)}
                alt=""
                class="h-3.5 w-5 rounded-sm object-cover"
              />
              <span class="truncate">${countryName(m.myTag)}</span>
              ${m.myFaction === null
                ? nothing
                : html`<span class="size-2 rounded-full ${FACTION_DOT_CLASS[m.myFaction]}"></span>`}
            </span>`}
        <button
          data-ww-wars
          class="rounded-md border border-white/15 bg-white/5 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs hover:bg-white/10"
          @click=${() => this.eventBus.emit(new ToggleWwDiplomacyEvent())}
        >
          ${translateText("ww1936.band.wars", { n: m.wars })}
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
  }
}
```

`src/client/hud/ww1936/WwDiplomacyPanel.ts`:

```ts
import { COUNTRIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { assetUrl } from "@openfront/shared/AssetUrls";
import { EventBus } from "@openfront/shared/EventBus";
import { html, LitElement, nothing } from "lit";
import { customElement, state } from "lit/decorators.js";
import { Controller } from "../../Controller";
import { translateText } from "../../Utils";
import type { GameView } from "../../view";
import { countryName, diplomacyModel, FACTION_DOT_CLASS, WW_BAND_PX } from "./WorldWarModel";
import { ToggleWwDiplomacyEvent } from "./WwTopBand";

/**
 * World War 1936 (spec §8.2): a right-hand drawer at most half the screen
 * wide — the factions and their members (capitulated ones struck through),
 * my wars, the camp scores if the game ended now, and the next date of the
 * historical calendar.
 */
@customElement("ww-diplomacy-panel")
export class WwDiplomacyPanel extends LitElement implements Controller {
  public game: GameView;
  public eventBus: EventBus;
  @state() private open = false;

  createRenderRoot() {
    return this;
  }

  init() {
    this.eventBus.on(ToggleWwDiplomacyEvent, () => {
      this.open = !this.open;
    });
  }

  getTickIntervalMs() {
    return 500;
  }

  tick() {
    if (this.open) this.requestUpdate();
  }

  render() {
    if (!this.open || !this.game?.worldWarScenario()) return null;
    const m = diplomacyModel(this.game);
    const flag = (tag: keyof typeof COUNTRIES) =>
      html`<img src=${assetUrl(`flags/${COUNTRIES[tag].flag}.svg`)} alt="" class="h-3 w-4 rounded-sm object-cover" />`;
    return html`
      <aside
        data-ww-diplomacy
        class="fixed right-0 bottom-4 z-[1003] w-[min(22rem,50vw)] overflow-y-auto rounded-l-xl bg-gray-900/92 p-3 text-sm text-white shadow-xl"
        style="top: ${WW_BAND_PX + 8}px"
      >
        <div class="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 class="m-0 text-sm font-bold">${translateText("ww1936.panel.title")}</h3>
          <button
            class="size-8 rounded-md hover:bg-white/10"
            aria-label=${translateText("common.close")}
            @click=${() => (this.open = false)}
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
        ${m.factions.map(
          (f) => html`<section class="mb-3">
            <div class="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/70">
              <span class="size-2 rounded-full ${FACTION_DOT_CLASS[f.id]}"></span>
              ${translateText(`ww1936.faction.${f.id}`)}
            </div>
            <ul class="m-0 flex list-none flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 p-0 text-xs">
              ${f.members.map(
                (x) => html`<li
                  class="flex items-center gap-1 ${x.capitulated || !x.alive
                    ? "text-white/40 line-through"
                    : ""}"
                >
                  ${flag(x.tag)} ${countryName(x.tag)}
                </li>`,
              )}
            </ul>
          </section>`,
        )}
        <section class="mb-3">
          <div class="mb-1 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/70">
            ${translateText("ww1936.panel.my_wars")}
          </div>
          ${m.myWars.length === 0
            ? html`<p class="m-0 text-xs text-white/60">${translateText("ww1936.panel.at_peace")}</p>`
            : html`<ul class="m-0 flex list-none flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 p-0 text-xs">
                ${m.myWars.map((t) => html`<li class="flex items-center gap-1">${flag(t)} ${countryName(t)}</li>`)}
              </ul>`}
        </section>
        <section class="mb-3">
          <div class="mb-1 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/70">
            ${translateText("ww1936.panel.scores")}
          </div>
          ${m.camps.slice(0, 6).map(
            (c) => html`<div class="mb-1">
              <div class="flex justify-between text-xs ${c.mine ? "font-bold text-amber-300" : ""}">
                <span>${c.name}</span><span class="tabular-nums">${(c.scoreBp / 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="h-1.5 rounded bg-white/10">
                <div class="h-1.5 rounded ${c.mine ? "bg-amber-400" : "bg-white/60"}" style="width: ${c.scoreBp / 100}%"></div>
              </div>
            </div>`,
          )}
        </section>
        ${m.nextHistory === null || this.game.config().historicalAi() === false
          ? nothing
          : html`<p class="m-0 text-xs text-white/70">
              ${translateText("ww1936.panel.next_history", { date: m.nextHistory })}
            </p>`}
      </aside>
    `;
  }
}
```

```ts
  /** World War 1936 (spec §8.2): the first tap arms, the second within 3 s declares. */
  private handleDeclareWarClick(e: Event, other: PlayerView) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (this.declareTap.tap(other.id(), Date.now())) {
      this.eventBus.emit(new SendDeclareWarIntentEvent(other));
      this.hide();
      return;
    }
    this.requestUpdate();
    setTimeout(() => this.requestUpdate(), TWO_TAP_MS + 50);
  }
```

```ts
              country: countryName(info.originalOwner),
```

### Task 10: 주 경계 선 `RegionBorderPass`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/client/render/gl/passes/RegionBorderPass.ts`, `src/client/render/gl/shaders/region-border/region-border.vert.glsl`, `src/client/render/gl/shaders/region-border/region-border.frag.glsl`
- Modify: `src/client/render/gl/Renderer.ts`, `src/client/render/gl/MapRenderer.ts`, `src/client/hud/GameRenderer.ts`(Task 9 다음, 같은 갈래)
- Test: `tests/client/render/gl/RegionBorderPass.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ScenarioIndex.regionIds`(Task 1), `SEA_ZONE_BASE`, GL 도구 `createProgram`, `createTexture2D`, `createMapQuad`, `shaderSrc`
- Produces: `regionEdgeMask(ids, w, h): Uint8Array`, `RegionBorderPass`, `Renderer.setRegions(ids)`, `MapRenderer.setRegions(ids)`

주 경계는 영토 채우기 위, 나라 국경(`borderStampPass`) 아래에 그린다(spec §8.2). 경계 표시는 시작 때 CPU에서 한 번 만든다(타일마다 오른쪽·아래 이웃과 번호가 다르면 표시: 땅–땅 = 1, 바다–바다 = 2, 해안은 그리지 않는다). 확대 1 미만에서는 안 보이고 3에서 다 보인다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트**

`tests/client/render/gl/RegionBorderPass.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import {
  RegionBorderPass,
  regionEdgeMask,
} from "../../../../src/client/render/gl/passes/RegionBorderPass";

function countingGL() {
  const counts = { draws: 0, uploads: 0 };
  const gl = new Proxy(
    {},
    {
      get: (_t, prop) => {
        if (typeof prop === "string" && /^[A-Z0-9_]+$/.test(prop)) return 0;
        if (prop === "drawArrays") return () => counts.draws++;
        if (prop === "texImage2D") return () => counts.uploads++;
        if (prop === "getProgramParameter") return () => true;
        if (prop === "getShaderParameter") return () => true;
        return () => ({});
      },
    },
  ) as WebGL2RenderingContext;
  return { gl, counts };
}

describe("RegionBorderPass (spec §8.2)", () => {
  test("marks land-land and sea-sea edges, never the coast", () => {
    // 4x3: two land regions over two sea zones.
    const ids = Uint16Array.from([1, 1, 2, 2, 1, 1, 2, 2, 40001, 40001, 40002, 40002]);
    expect([...regionEdgeMask(ids, 4, 3)]).toEqual([0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0]);
  });

  test("hidden when zoomed out, drawn when zoomed in", () => {
    const { gl, counts } = countingGL();
    const pass = new RegionBorderPass(gl, Uint16Array.from([1, 2, 1, 2]), 2, 2);
    expect(counts.uploads).toBe(1);
    pass.draw(new Float32Array(9), 0.5);
    expect(counts.draws).toBe(0);
    pass.draw(new Float32Array(9), 4);
    expect(counts.draws).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

Run: `npx vitest tests/client/render/gl/RegionBorderPass.test.ts --run`
Expected: FAIL (모듈이 없다)

- [ ] **Step 2: 패스와 셰이더**

`src/client/render/gl/shaders/region-border/region-border.vert.glsl`:

```glsl
#version 300 es
precision highp float;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 aPos;

uniform mat3 uCamera;

out vec2 vUV;

void main() {
  vec3 clip = uCamera * vec3(aPos, 1.0);
  gl_Position = vec4(clip.xy, 0.0, 1.0);
  vUV = aPos / vec2(float(MAP_W), float(MAP_H));
}
```

`src/client/render/gl/shaders/region-border/region-border.frag.glsl`:

```glsl
#version 300 es
precision highp float;
precision highp usampler2D;

// Per-tile edge mask (R8UI): 0 none, 1 land region edge, 2 sea zone edge.
uniform usampler2D uEdges;
// 0..1 with the zoom.
uniform float uAlpha;

in vec2 vUV;
out vec4 fragColor;

void main() {
  ivec2 tc = clamp(
    ivec2(int(vUV.x * float(MAP_W)), int(vUV.y * float(MAP_H))),
    ivec2(0),
    ivec2(MAP_W - 1, MAP_H - 1)
  );
  uint e = texelFetch(uEdges, tc, 0).r;
  if (e == 0u) discard;
  fragColor = e == 1u
    ? vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.35 * uAlpha)
    : vec4(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.12 * uAlpha);
}
```

`src/client/render/gl/passes/RegionBorderPass.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * RegionBorderPass — World War 1936 (spec §8.2): thin lines between regions
 * and between sea zones, over the territory fill and under the country
 * borders. Faint when zoomed out, full when zoomed in.
 */

import { SEA_ZONE_BASE } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import fragSrc from "../shaders/region-border/region-border.frag.glsl?raw";
import vertSrc from "../shaders/region-border/region-border.vert.glsl?raw";
import {
  createMapQuad,
  createProgram,
  createTexture2D,
  shaderSrc,
} from "../utils/GlUtils";

/** Lines start to show at this zoom (screen px per tile)… */
export const REGION_MIN_ZOOM = 1;
/** …and are full from this one. */
export const REGION_FULL_ZOOM = 3;

/**
 * 1 where a land tile's right or lower neighbour is another land region, 2
 * where a water tile's is another sea zone; 0 elsewhere (coasts included).
 */
export function regionEdgeMask(ids: Uint16Array, w: number, h: number): Uint8Array {
  const out = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      const t = y * w + x;
      const id = ids[t];
      if (id === 0) continue;
      const sea = id > SEA_ZONE_BASE;
      const right = x + 1 < w ? ids[t + 1] : id;
      const down = y + 1 < h ? ids[t + w] : id;
      if (
        (right !== id && right !== 0 && right > SEA_ZONE_BASE === sea) ||
        (down !== id && down !== 0 && down > SEA_ZONE_BASE === sea)
      ) {
        out[t] = sea ? 2 : 1;
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export class RegionBorderPass {
  private program: WebGLProgram;
  private edgeTex: WebGLTexture;
  private vao: WebGLVertexArrayObject;
  private uCamera: WebGLUniformLocation;
  private uEdges: WebGLUniformLocation;
  private uAlpha: WebGLUniformLocation;

  constructor(
    private gl: WebGL2RenderingContext,
    regionIds: Uint16Array,
    mapW: number,
    mapH: number,
  ) {
    this.program = createProgram(
      gl,
      shaderSrc(vertSrc, { MAP_W: mapW, MAP_H: mapH }),
      shaderSrc(fragSrc, { MAP_W: mapW, MAP_H: mapH }),
      "RegionBorderPass",
    );
    this.uCamera = gl.getUniformLocation(this.program, "uCamera")!;
    this.uEdges = gl.getUniformLocation(this.program, "uEdges")!;
    this.uAlpha = gl.getUniformLocation(this.program, "uAlpha")!;
    this.edgeTex = createTexture2D(gl, {
      width: mapW,
      height: mapH,
      internalFormat: gl.R8UI,
      format: gl.RED_INTEGER,
      type: gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,
      data: regionEdgeMask(regionIds, mapW, mapH),
      filter: gl.NEAREST,
    });
    this.vao = createMapQuad(gl, mapW, mapH);
  }

  draw(cam: Float32Array, zoom: number): void {
    const alpha = Math.min(
      1,
      Math.max(0, (zoom - REGION_MIN_ZOOM) / (REGION_FULL_ZOOM - REGION_MIN_ZOOM)),
    );
    if (alpha <= 0) return;
    const gl = this.gl;
    gl.useProgram(this.program);
    gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);
    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, this.edgeTex);
    gl.uniform1i(this.uEdges, 0);
    gl.uniformMatrix3fv(this.uCamera, false, cam);
    gl.uniform1f(this.uAlpha, alpha);
    gl.bindVertexArray(this.vao);
    gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 6);
    gl.bindVertexArray(null);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    const gl = this.gl;
    gl.deleteProgram(this.program);
    gl.deleteTexture(this.edgeTex);
    gl.deleteVertexArray(this.vao);
  }
}
```

(`createTexture2D`가 `data`를 `texImage2D`로 올리는지 확인한다. 테스트의 `uploads`가 그것을 센다. 올리지 않으면 `texSubImage2D`를 세도록 테스트를 맞춘다.)

`src/client/render/gl/Renderer.ts`:
1. import에 `import { RegionBorderPass } from "./passes/RegionBorderPass";`
2. 필드(`private mapLayerPasses` 근처)에 `/** World War 1936: region and sea-zone lines; null otherwise. */ private regionBorderPass: RegionBorderPass | null = null;`
3. `setMapLayers` 다음에:

```ts
  /** World War 1936 (spec §8.2): draw the lines between these region ids. */
  setRegions(regionIds: Uint16Array): void {
    this.regionBorderPass?.dispose();
    this.regionBorderPass = new RegionBorderPass(this.gl, regionIds, this.mapW, this.mapH);
  }
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `COUNTRY_TAGS`, `FACTION_IDS`, `COUNTRY_GROUPS`(Task 1)
- Produces: 아래 키 전부. Task 8·9·1의 화면이 쓴다.

지도자는 1936-01-01에 실제로 나라를 이끈 사람이다(spec §5.1, 주인 결정 7). 직함이나 평가는 붙이지 않는다(spec §11).

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트**

`tests/ww1936/WorldWarI18n.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  COUNTRY_GROUPS,
  COUNTRY_TAGS,
  FACTION_IDS,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const lang = (code: string) =>
  JSON.parse(
    fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, `../../resources/lang/${code}.json`), "utf8"),
  ) as Record<string, unknown>;

function get(obj: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
  return key.split(".").reduce<unknown>(
    (o, k) => (o !== null && typeof o === "object" ? (o as Record<string, unknown>)[k] : undefined),
    obj,
  );
}

/** {name}s in an ICU message, the plural arguments included. */
function params(msg: string): string[] {
  return [...new Set([...msg.matchAll(/\{(\w+)/g)].map((m) => m[1]))].sort();
}

const KEYS = [
  ...COUNTRY_TAGS.flatMap((t) => [`ww1936.country.${t}`, `ww1936.leader.${t}`]),
  ...FACTION_IDS.map((f) => `ww1936.faction.${f}`),
  ...COUNTRY_GROUPS.map((g) => `ww1936.group.${g}`),
  ...Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `ww1936.month.${String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`),
  "ww1936.date",
  "ww1936.band.prep",
  "ww1936.band.wars",
  "ww1936.panel.title",
  "ww1936.panel.my_wars",
  "ww1936.panel.at_peace",
  "ww1936.panel.scores",
  "ww1936.panel.next_history",
  "ww1936.declare.button",
  "ww1936.declare.confirm",
  "ww1936.region.vp",
  "ww1936.region.original_owner",
  "ww1936.win.your_faction",
  "ww1936.win.faction_won",
  "ww1936.win.country_won",
  "ww1936.win.mvp",
  "ww1936.lobby.pick_country",
  "ww1936.lobby.pick_hint",
  "ww1936.lobby.taken_by",
  "events_display.ww1936_prep_over",
  "events_display.ww1936_war_declared",
  "events_display.ww1936_war_joined",
  "events_display.ww1936_capitulated",
  "domestic.win_reason.ww_score",
  "domestic.win_reason.ww_last_camp",
  "game_settings.world_war_1936",
  "game_settings.historical_ai",
  "map.world1936",
];

describe("World War 1936 texts (CLAUDE.md: en + ko)", () => {
  const en = lang("en");
  const ko = lang("ko");

  test.each(KEYS)("%s is in English and Korean with the same parameters", (key) => {
    const e = get(en, key);
    const k = get(ko, key);
    expect(typeof e, `en ${key}`).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof k, `ko ${key}`).toBe("string");
    expect(params(k as string)).toEqual(params(e as string));
  });

  test("no other language file mentions the mode", () => {
    const dir = path.join(__dirname, "../../resources/lang");
    for (const f of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
      if (f === "en.json" || f === "ko.json" || !f.endsWith(".json")) continue;
      expect(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, f), "utf8"), f).not.toContain("ww1936");
    }
  });
});
```

(`map.world1936`은 Task 4의 생성기가 같은 값을 이미 넣었으면 그대로 둔다.)

합치고 정렬하는 한 줄(작업 파일 `/tmp/ww1936-en.json`에 위 내용을 저장한 뒤):

```bash
node -e '
const fs = require("fs");
const merge = (a, b) => { for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(b)) a[k] = v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v) ? merge(a[k] ?? {}, v) : v; return a; };
const sort = (o) => o && typeof o === "object" && !Array.isArray(o) ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(o).sort().map((k) => [k, sort(o[k])])) : o;
const [file, frag, sorted] = process.argv.slice(1);
const out = merge(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")), JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(frag, "utf8")));
fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(sorted === "sort" ? sort(out) : out, null, 2) + "\n");
' resources/lang/en.json /tmp/ww1936-en.json sort
```

(월 키는 두 자리 `"01"`…`"12"`다. 한 자리 숫자 키는 JavaScript가 숫자 순으로 읽어 `tests/EnJsonSorted.test.ts`의 문자열 순서와 어긋난다.)

### Task 12: 결정성·스냅샷·성능 (그룹 1을 모두 합친 뒤)

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/ww1936/WorldWarSnapshot.test.ts`, `tests/ww1936/World1936Load.test.ts`
- Modify: 없음(실패하면 원인 Task의 파일을 그 Task의 담당 범위 안에서 고친다)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 모든 앞 Task. `createGameRunnerFromSnapshot`, `snapshotGame`, `diffGraphs`, `diffSnapshots`(`tests/util/Snapshot.ts`), 테스트 유틸, 실제 지도(Task 4)
- Produces: 모드 켬 고정값(digest) 하나

- [ ] **Step 1: 스냅샷 오라클 테스트**

`tests/ww1936/WorldWarSnapshot.test.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { GameMapSize, GameMapType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import type { StampedIntent } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import { WW_PREP_TICKS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/WorldWarTypes";
import { createGameRunnerFromSnapshot, GameRunner } from "@openfront/engine/GameRunner";
import { snapshotGame } from "@openfront/engine/snapshot/GameSnapshot";
import { loadMapFiles } from "@openfront/shared/GameMapLoader";
import { TestDataMapLoader } from "../util/ScriptedGame";
import { diffGraphs, diffSnapshots } from "../util/Snapshot";
import {
  byTag,
  HUMAN,
  MINI,
  stamped,
  step,
  stepTo,
  wwGameStart,
  WwHuman,
  wwRunner,
} from "./util";

const HUMANS: WwHuman[] = [{ clientID: HUMAN, country: "DEU" }];
const hash = (r: GameRunner) => (r.game as unknown as { hash(): number }).hash();

async function restore(runner: GameRunner): Promise<GameRunner> {
  return createGameRunnerFromSnapshot(
    wwGameStart({}, HUMANS),
    runner.snapshot(),
    undefined,
    await loadMapFiles(new TestDataMapLoader(MINI), GameMapType.World1936, GameMapSize.Normal),
    () => {},
  );
}

/** Runs both with the same turns; they must stay identical tick by tick. */
function lockstep(
  a: GameRunner,
  b: GameRunner,
  ticks: number,
  intents: (tick: number) => StampedIntent[] = () => [],
): void {
  expect(diffGraphs(a.game, b.game)).toEqual([]);
  for (let i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
    const turn = intents(a.game.ticks());
    step(a, 1, turn);
    step(b, 1, turn);
    if (hash(a) !== hash(b)) throw new Error(`hash diverged ${i + 1} tick(s) after restore`);
    if ((i + 1) % 25 === 0 || i + 1 === ticks) {
      expect(diffSnapshots(snapshotGame(a.game), snapshotGame(b.game))).toEqual([]);
    }
  }
  expect(diffGraphs(a.game, b.game)).toEqual([]);
}

describe("World War 1936 snapshots (spec §7.8)", () => {
  // 1: the opening; 300: the preparation minute; 602: a declare_war
  // execution waiting for its first tick; 700: at war, AI attacking.
  test.each([1, 300, WW_PREP_TICKS + 2, 700])("restored at tick %i continues exactly", async (at) => {
    const a = await wwRunner({}, HUMANS);
    const pol = byTag(a.game, "POL").id();
    const intents = (t: number) =>
      t === WW_PREP_TICKS + 1 ? [stamped(HUMAN, { type: "declare_war", target: pol })] : [];
    while (a.game.ticks() < at) step(a, 1, intents(a.game.ticks()));
    const b = await restore(a);
    lockstep(a, b, 200, intents);
  }, 120_000);

  test("restoring mid-transfer continues exactly", async () => {
    const a = await wwRunner({}, HUMANS);
    stepTo(a, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
    const g = a.game;
    const ww = g.worldWar()!;
    const [deu, pol] = [byTag(g, "DEU"), byTag(g, "POL")];
    // A capitulation whose land is part-way over (on the real map a
    // transfer takes several ticks; the test map needs one, so the first
    // 1,500 tiles are moved by hand between ticks).
    ww.addCapitulation(pol.smallID(), deu.smallID(), g.ticks());
    ww.endWarsOf(pol.smallID());
    const tiles = Uint32Array.from(pol.tiles());
    ww.queueTransfer(pol.smallID(), deu.smallID(), tiles);
    const t = ww.transfers()[0];
    for (; t.next < 1500; t.next++) deu.conquer(tiles[t.next]);
    const b = await restore(a);
    expect(b.game.worldWar()!.transfers()[0].next).toBe(1500);
    lockstep(a, b, 50);
    expect(pol.isAlive()).toBe(false);
    expect(ww.transfers()).toEqual([]);
  }, 120_000);
});

describe("World War 1936 stays deterministic", () => {
  test("a test-map game is pinned", async () => {
    const runner = await wwRunner();
    const h = createHash("sha256");
    while (runner.game.ticks() < 3000) {
      step(runner);
      h.update(`${hash(runner)},`);
    }
    expect(h.digest("hex").slice(0, 16)).toBe("<첫 실행 값>");
  }, 120_000);
});
```

Run: `npx vitest tests/ww1936/WorldWarSnapshot.test.ts --run`
Expected: 고정값 테스트만 FAIL하고(받은 값이 메시지에 나온다), 나머지 PASS. 그 값을 두 번 더 실행해 같은지 확인한 다음 `"<첫 실행 값>"` 자리에 넣는다. 다른 테스트가 실패하면 그 Task의 스냅샷 코드(`WorldWar.snapshot/restoreState`, 실행 3개의 `snapshot/restoreSnapshot`)를 고친다. 이 고정값은 이 모드의 규칙이 바뀌면 의도적으로만 다시 고정한다(주석으로 남긴다).

- [ ] **Step 2: 실제 지도 시작 시간**

`tests/ww1936/World1936Load.test.ts`:

```ts
import { GameMapSize, GameMapType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import { loadTerrainMap } from "@openfront/engine-lib/game/TerrainMapLoader";
import { fillScenarioOwners } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";
import { loadMapFiles } from "@openfront/shared/GameMapLoader";
import path from "path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";
import { NodeGameMapLoader } from "../perf/fullgame/NodeGameMapLoader";

const maps = new NodeGameMapLoader(
  path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../resources/maps"),
);

describe("starting on the world1936 map (spec §5.3)", () => {
  test("decoding the regions and filling every owner is quick", async () => {
    const files = await loadMapFiles(maps, GameMapType.World1936, GameMapSize.Normal);
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const data = await loadTerrainMap(files);
    const t1 = performance.now();
    fillScenarioOwners(data.gameMap, data.scenario!);
    const t2 = performance.now();
    console.log(`world1936: terrain+regions ${(t1 - t0).toFixed(0)} ms, owners ${(t2 - t1).toFixed(0)} ms`);
    // Target on a desktop: 300 ms for the fill. The bound leaves room for CI.
    expect(t2 - t1).toBeLessThan(1500);
    let unowned = 0;
    data.gameMap.forEachTile((t) => {
      if (data.gameMap.isLand(t) && !data.gameMap.isImpassable(t) && data.gameMap.ownerID(t) === 0) unowned++;
    });
    expect(unowned).toBe(0);
  }, 60_000);
});
```

Run: `npx vitest tests/ww1936/World1936Load.test.ts --run`
Expected: PASS, 출력에 시간 두 개. 채우기가 300ms를 넘으면 결과 표에 적고 Task 13에서 실제 화면 시작 시간을 같이 본다.

- [ ] **Step 2: 별도 포트(9500)의 Vite 클라이언트로 실제 화면 확인(자동)**

실제 서버가 3000~3002와 9000을 쓰고 있다. 이 확인은 **그 포트를 열지도, 그 서버에 요청하지도 않는다.** 혼자 하기 게임은 브라우저 안에서 돌므로 Vite 클라이언트만 있으면 된다. `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-strategic-weapons.md` Task 14 Step 2와 같은 방법이다(경로와 포트만 다르다).

```bash
ss -ltn | grep -E ':(9500|9501) ' && echo "port busy: stop and ask" || echo free
mkdir -p /tmp/ww1936-check
cat > /tmp/ww1936-check/vite.ww.config.ts <<'EOF'
// The worktree's dev config on port 9500, with every route to ports
// 3000-3002 (the owner's running server) pointed at a closed port and the
// create-game middleware (which POSTs straight to 3001+) dropped.
import type { UserConfig } from "vite";
import base from "/home/eser/dev/OpenFrontIO-ww/vite.config.ts";

const DEAD = "localhost:39999";

export default (env: { mode: string; command: string }) => {
  const config = (base as (e: typeof env) => UserConfig)(env);
  config.plugins = (config.plugins ?? [])
    .flat()
    .filter(
      (p) =>
        !(p && typeof p === "object" && "name" in p) ||
        (p as { name: string }).name !== "random-worker-create-proxy",
    );
  config.server = {
    ...config.server,
    port: 9500,
    strictPort: true,
    open: false,
    hmr: { port: 9501 },
    proxy: {
      "/lobbies": { target: `ws://${DEAD}`, ws: true },
      "/w0": { target: `ws://${DEAD}`, ws: true },
      "/w1": { target: `ws://${DEAD}`, ws: true },
      "/api": { target: `http://${DEAD}` },
    },
  };
  return config;
};
EOF
(cd /home/eser/dev/OpenFrontIO-ww && SKIP_BROWSER_OPEN=true npx vite --config /tmp/ww1936-check/vite.ww.config.ts > /tmp/ww1936-check/vite.log 2>&1 &)
timeout 120 bash -c 'until curl -sf http://localhost:9500 >/dev/null; do sleep 1; done' && echo up
```

`run-openfront` 스킬의 Playwright 준비(`bash .claude/skills/run-openfront/setup.sh`, 이미 했으면 생략)를 마친 뒤 저장소 루트에 `ww1936-check.mjs`를 만든다. 앞부분(브라우저 실행, `requestAnimationFrame` 늦추기, WebGL 허용, 3000~3002·9000·`/api`·`/selfhost-api`·WebSocket 막기, 한국어 설정, "혼자 하기" 열기)은 strategic-weapons 계획의 `strategic-check.mjs`와 같다(`BASE = "http://localhost:9500"`, `OUT = "/tmp/ww1936-check/shots"`, `viewport = { width: 1180, height: 820 }`). 그다음:

```js
await page.evaluate(() => {
  const modal = document.querySelector("single-player-modal");
  modal.worldWar1936 = true;
  modal.country = "DEU";
});
await page.waitForTimeout(500);
const pickerShown = await page.evaluate(
  () => document.querySelectorAll("ww-country-picker button[data-tag]").length,
);
await page.screenshot({ path: `${OUT}/1-picker.png` });
const t0 = Date.now();
await page
  .locator('o-button[translationKey="game_settings.start"] button:visible')
  .first()
  .click();
await page.waitForSelector("[data-ww-band]", { timeout: 120_000 });
const startMs = Date.now() - t0;
await page.waitForTimeout(3000 + RAF);
const prep = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector("[data-ww-date]")?.textContent?.trim() ?? "");
await page.screenshot({ path: `${OUT}/2-prep.png` });
await page.waitForTimeout(65_000);
const date = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector("[data-ww-date]")?.textContent?.trim() ?? "");
await page.locator("[data-ww-wars]").click();
await page.waitForTimeout(1000 + RAF);
const drawer = await page.evaluate(() => {
  const d = document.querySelector("[data-ww-diplomacy]");
  if (!d) return null;
  const r = d.getBoundingClientRect();
  return { widthShare: r.width / window.innerWidth, text: d.textContent ?? "" };
});
await page.screenshot({ path: `${OUT}/3-drawer.png` });
const report = {
  pickerShown,
  startMs,
  prep,
  date,
  drawer: drawer && { widthShare: drawer.widthShare, hasFactions: /추축국/.test(drawer.text) && /연합국/.test(drawer.text) },
  horizontalScroll: await page.evaluate(
    () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth,
  ),
  errors,
};
console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
await browser.close();
const ok =
  pickerShown === 79 &&
  /^준비 0:\d\d$/.test(prep) &&
  /^1936년 /.test(date) &&
  report.drawer?.hasFactions === true &&
  report.drawer.widthShare <= 0.5 &&
  report.horizontalScroll === false &&
  errors.length === 0;
process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
```

Run: `node ww1936-check.mjs`
Expected: `pickerShown: 79`, `prep`이 "준비 0:5x", `date`가 "1936년 1월 …", 서랍에 추축국·연합국, 서랍 폭이 화면 절반 이하, 가로 스크롤 없음, `errors: []`, 종료 코드 0. `startMs`(시작 버튼 → 띠)를 결과에 적는다(목표: 데스크톱 10초 이하). 스크린샷 3장을 열어 확인한다: 나라 고르기에 메이저 7개 카드와 지역 4묶음, 지도 전체가 1936년 나라 색으로 칠해져 있다(빈 땅 없음), 독일 깃발이 흑백적 삼색기, 일본이 일장기, 줌을 당기면 주 경계 선이 보인다(2번 스크린샷 뒤 `page.mouse.wheel`로 확대해 한 장 더 찍어도 된다).

정리:

```bash
pkill -f "vite --config /tmp/ww1936-check/vite.ww.config.ts"
rm ww1936-check.mjs
ss -ltn | grep -E ':(9500|9501) ' || echo "9500 closed"
git status --short
```

Expected: `git status`가 깨끗하다. 이 호스트에서 Playwright가 동작하지 않으면 이 Step은 건너뛰고 그 사실을 기록한 뒤 Step 3을 주인에게 넘긴다.

- [ ] **Step 3: 주인이 실제 태블릿·여러 사람으로 확인할 목록**

1. 방 만들기에서 "1936 세계대전"을 켜면 지도·방식·팀·봇·단위 칸이 사라지고 나라 고르기가 나온다. 다른 사람이 고른 나라는 그 사람 이름과 함께 흐리다. 같은 나라를 두 사람이 누르면 먼저 누른 사람만 갖는다. 참가 화면 요약에 "1936 세계대전: 켜짐", "역사대로 진행 (AI): 켜짐".
2. 시작하면 스폰 단계 없이 바로 1936년 지도가 칠해지고, 위쪽 띠에 "준비 0:59"부터 줄어드는 시간이 보인다. 준비 중에는 아무도 공격하지 못하고, 선전포고 버튼도 없다. 1분 뒤 "준비가 끝났습니다" 알림과 함께 날짜가 1936년 1월 1일부터 흐른다(약 0.75초에 하루).
3. 이탈리아와 에티오피아는 처음부터 싸운다. 다른 나라는 선포 전에는 서로 공격하지 않는다(사람도 AI도).
4. 프랑스를 눌러 "선전포고"를 한 번 누르면 "확인: 선전포고 (진영 회원 1개국도 참전)"으로 바뀌고 3초 안에 다시 누르면 영국까지 전쟁에 들어간다. 3초가 지나면 원래 글자로 돌아간다.
5. 지도를 탭하면 정보 카드에 주 이름(한국어), 승점, 주인이 바뀐 땅이면 "1936년: (원래 나라)"가 보인다.
6. "역사대로 진행"을 켠 판에서 1936년 7월쯤 스페인 내전, 1939년 9월쯤 독일-폴란드 전쟁이 AI끼리 일어난다(며칠 늦거나 가끔 빠진다). 사람 나라는 달력이 움직이지 않는다.
7. 나라가 승점을 80% 잃으면(소련·중국은 90%) 항복 알림이 뜨고 남은 땅이 승자에게 몇 초 안에 넘어간다. 사람 나라가 항복하면 관전으로 바뀐다.
8. 45분이 되면 승리 창에 "(진영) 승리"와 사유 "전쟁 끝의 승점 판정 승리", 아래에 MVP가 보이고 내 진영이면 승리 연출이 나온다.
9. 순위표에 "독일 · (사용자 이름)"처럼 보이고 내 진영 나라가 강조된다. 지도 이름표는 영어 나라 이름이다.
10. 이 옵션을 끈 판(다른 지도, 다른 모드)은 지금과 똑같다.
